### [1] [Radio Monitoring of the Changing-look AGN Mrk 590 I: VLA Observations Reveal A Bow-shock Driven Radio Brightening Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26243)
*Gregory Walsh,Marianne Vestergaard,Daniel Lawther,Sandra Raimundo,Jun Yi Koay*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过VLA多频段观测（1-17 GHz，2015-2017年四个历元）对变脸活动星系核Mrk 590的射电辐射进行研究，发现其射电成分致密且无显著延展结构，射电频谱在振幅和形态上均呈可变性。贝叶斯推断建模表明，射电变亮的驱动力是射电喷流与星际介质相互作用产生的弓形激波，而非与2017年高能波段重燃相关的吸积事件，揭示了CLAGN种群行为的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mrk 590是一个在几十年间从Seyfert 1型缓慢消退至接近静止、随后于2017年重新点亮的变脸活动星系核（CLAGN）。虽然光学/紫外/X射线波段监测已揭示其吸积驱动的变异性特征，但射电源对导致其消退和重燃的变脸事件的响应此前未知，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用Karl G. Jansky甚大阵列（VLA）A构型在2015至2017年间四个历元对Mrk 590进行1-17 GHz多频段观测；对射电频谱的振幅和形态变异性进行识别，并实施贝叶斯推断建模程序以约束驱动射电可变性的物理机制。

Result: 射电源以微弱、致密成分为主导，无明显延展结构；射电频谱在此期间在振幅和形态上均表现出可变性。贝叶斯建模更倾向于支持由射电喷流与星际介质相互作用产生的弓形激波驱动射电变亮（在后期历元中体现），说明观测到的射电变异性与导致AGN高能波段重新变亮的吸积事件不相关。

Conclusion: 虽然在其他CLAGN中观察到了射电与吸积驱动辐射之间的相关变异性，但Mrk 590中缺乏这种相关性，展示了变脸AGN种群的多样性及其行为驱动机制的差异。

Abstract: Mrk 590 is a changing-look AGN (CLAGN) that slowly faded from a Seyfert Type 1 to near quiescence over a few decades before re-igniting in 2017. Monitoring in the optical/UV/X-ray wavebands has revealed accretion-driven variability signatures, but the response of the radio source to the changing-look events that drove its fading and re-ignition is unknown. We present multi-frequency (1-17~GHz), A-configuration, Karl G. Jansky Very Large Array (VLA) observations of Mrk 590 over four epochs from 2015 to 2017. The radio source is dominated by a faint, compact component, absent of any notable extended structures. We identify a variable radio spectrum over this period, both in amplitude and in shape, and implement a Bayesian inference modeling routine to constrain the mechanism driving the radio variability. Our routine favors radio variability powered by a bow shock produced through an interaction of a radio jet with the interstellar medium, which drives the radio brightening in later epochs. This demonstrates that the radio variability we observed is not correlated with the variable accretion event that led to the re-brightening of the AGN at higher energy wavebands. While correlated variability between the radio and accretion-driven emission has been observed in other CLAGN, the lack of correlated variability in Mrk 590 shows the diversity of changing-look AGN populations and the mechanisms driving their behavior.

</details>


### [2] [Using CFHT's SITELLE to Probe the Long-Sought Supernova Remnant Shell in the Crab Nebula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26280)
*Lucas V. da Conceição,Janette Suherli,Samar Safi-Harb,Carter Rhea,Ivo R. Seitenzahl,Ashley J. Ruiter*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用CFHT的成像傅里叶变换光谱仪SITELLE对蟹状星云进行深度、大视场积分场光谱观测，在2.4-10 pc的西侧天区搜索[Fe XIV]λ5303日冕线以寻找前向激波，未发现显著发射，得到平均面亮度上限≤3.79×10⁻¹⁷ erg cm⁻² s⁻¹ arcsec⁻²，这是对蟹状星云潜在超新星遗迹壳层日冕铁发射最深的大面积光学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蟹状星云被认为由超新星爆发产生，但长期以来其前向激波从未被直接探测到，而[Fe XIV]λ5303日冕线是激波加热气体的有效示踪物，因此作者希望通过深度搜寻该发射线来寻找这一长期未知的前向激波。

Method: 使用CFHT上的成像傅里叶变换光谱仪SITELLE，对两个11′×11′天区进行大视场积分场光谱观测，覆盖脉冲星以西约2.4-10 pc的投影半径范围（预期超新星壳层所在区域），经数据处理后在整个视场内搜索[Fe XIV]发射，并在三个代表性环带上计算平均面亮度上限。

Result: 在观测区域内未发现统计显著的[Fe XIV]发射，得到保守的平均面亮度上限为≤3.79×10⁻¹⁷ erg cm⁻² s⁻¹ arcsec⁻²，这是现存对蟹状星云壳层日冕铁发射最深的大面积光学约束。

Conclusion: 观测结果与以下情景一致：前向激波正在膨胀进入极低密度介质、激波气体较弱或欠电离、或壳层位于观测区域之外。该工作展示了宽视场光学积分场光谱在约束超新星遗迹中暗弱大尺度结构方面的能力。

Abstract: We present deep, wide-field integral field spectroscopy of the Crab nebula obtained with the imaging Fourier transform spectrometer SITELLE at the Canada--France--Hawaii Telescope (CFHT), to search for the long-sought forward shock. Our observations target the coronal line [Fe XIV]$λ$5303, a tracer of shock-heated gas, over two $11^{\prime} \times 11^{\prime}$ fields that probe projected radii of $\sim$2.4--10~pc west of the pulsar, encompassing the range in which a supernova shell is expected. After data processing and a search over the full field of view, we found no statistically significant [Fe XIV] emission in the surveyed regions. We derive a conservative average surface-brightness upper limit of $\lesssim 3.79 \times 10^{-17}~\mathrm{erg}~\mathrm{cm}^{-2}~\mathrm{s}^{-1}~\mathrm{arcsec}^{-2}$ over three representative annuli. This represents the deepest large-area optical constraint on coronal iron emission beyond the visible nebula from the Crab's putative supernova remnant (SNR) shell. Our results are consistent with scenarios in which the forward shock is expanding into a very low-density medium, the shocked gas is weak or underionized, or the shell lies outside the observed region. This work demonstrates the power of wide-field optical integral field spectroscopy for constraining faint, large-scale structures in SNRs.

</details>


### [3] [Synchrotron self-Compton Reverse Shock in Energy-Injection and Radiative Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26290)
*Fraija Nissim,Betancourt-Kamenetskaia Boris,Galván Antonio,Dainotti Maria Giovanna*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文推导了GRB反向激波区产生的同步自康普顿（SSC）闭合关系，涵盖厚壳与薄壳、均匀密度与恒星风流介质、有无能量注入等情形，并将预测的谱指数和时变指数与2FLGC目录中的PL和BPL拟合结果进行比较。结果显示PL光变拟合倾向于有能量注入的场景，BPL拟合倾向于无能量注入场景，且有能量注入时数据更支持均匀密度环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRB高能光变曲线和光谱呈现多样形态，需要通过同步辐射和SSC闭合关系来理解其物理起源，尤其是反向冲击在不同介质和动力学演化条件下的贡献尚未被系统研究。

Method: 推导反向冲击区域在厚壳/薄壳情形下、均匀密度/恒星风介质中、有无能量注入条件下的SSC闭合关系；假设电子谱指数为双幂律分布；将预测的谱指数与时变指数与2FLGC目录中的PL和BPL拟合结果进行比对分析。

Result: 反向冲击的SSC发射可以再现GRB余辉中常见的初始陡衰相和平台相；PL光变拟合倾向于支持有能量注入的场景，BPL拟合则倾向无能量注入场景；在有能量注入时，数据更支持均匀密度环境而非恒星风轮廓。

Conclusion: 反向冲击的SSC闭合关系能够解释GRB余辉早期多种光变形态，且通过拟合比较可以区分介质类型与能量注入的有无，为理解GRB余辉物理提供了新的诊断工具。

Abstract: Gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) are among the most energetic extragalactic transients, providing a unique laboratory to investigate the physical origin of the diverse morphologies observed in high-energy light curves and spectra. These morphologies, interpreted through synchrotron and synchrotron self-Compton (SSC) closure relations (CRs), may depend on the circumburst density profile, energy injection, and the dynamical evolution of the reverse shock in the adiabatic or radiative regime. We derive the SSC closure relations produced in the reverse-shock region for both thick- and thin-shell cases, considering constant-density and stratified circumburst media. The analysis assumes two power-law (PL) distributions for the electron spectral index and includes scenarios with and without energy injection from the progenitor. We show that, depending on the physical parameters, SSC emission from the reverse shock can reproduce the initial steep-decay and plateau phases commonly observed in GRB afterglows. We then compare the predicted spectral and temporal indices with those derived from PL and broken power-law (BPL) fits reported in the Second Fermi-LAT Gamma-ray Burst Catalog (2FLGC). Our results indicate that PL (BPL) light-curve fits preferentially favor scenarios with (without) energy injection. In scenarios with energy injection, the data preferentially support a constant-density environment over a stellar-wind profile.

</details>


### [4] [FRB 121102: No supernova-like ejecta or magnetar power, hinting at a binary WD merger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26567)
*Eli Waxman,Eran O. Ofek,Doron Kushnir*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用重复快速射电暴FRB 121102的色散测量及其伴随的持续射电源（PRS）观测，对其中心引擎提出严格约束：引擎功率需超过10^40 erg/s，支持中子星旋转供能而非磁能供能，或吸积供能；冷等离子体约束体的速度与动能不支持典型超新星抛射物，更符合白矮星双星并合的前身星系统模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定FRB 121102这类重复快速射电暴的中心引擎性质，区分旋转供能中子星、磁能供能中子星、吸积供能等不同模型，为理解重复FRB的物理起源提供关键约束。

Method: 结合时间依赖的色散量测量数据与早期对相关持续射电源（PRS）的射电观测，推导相对论性PRS等离子体的能量与年龄，进而约束底层引擎的功率下限；同时对比冷等离子体约束体的动力学特征与超新星抛射物的预期参数。

Result: PRS相对论等离子体能量>10^49.5 erg，年龄约60年，对应源功率>10^40 erg/s；若为中子星，则需旋转供能（~10 ms周期、~10^12.5 G磁场），吸积亦可；约束体的膨胀速度仅数百km/s，冷等离子体动能与典型超新星抛射物不符（除非高密度团块占比~10^-2.5），且抛射时间需早于PRS发射约10^3年。

Conclusion: 白矮星双星并合前身星模型可以同时满足所有约束：并合期间及之后低速抛射部分太阳质量物质，并合残骸经历约10^3年热演化后形成快速旋转中子星，从而解释FRB 121102及其PRS的全部观测特征。

Abstract: We use measurements of the time-dependent dispersion measure of the repeating FRB 121102, together with earlier radio observations of its associated persistent radio source (PRS), to derive stringent constraints on its underlying ``engine.'' The energy held by the relativistic PRS plasma is $>10^{49.5}$ erg, and its age is $\approx60$ yr, corresponding to an underlying source power exceeding $10^{40}$ erg s$^{-1}$. If the underlying source is a neutron star, this implies a rotational rather than a magnetic energy source, consistent with a $\sim10$ ms, $\sim10^{12.5}$ G neutron star. Alternatively, accretion may also be a viable energy source. The velocity and the kinetic energy of the cold plasma confining the relativistic PRS plasma are inconsistent with it being typical supernova ejecta (unless a significant fraction of the ejecta mass is carried by high-density clumps of $\approx10^{-2.5}$ fractional size)- its expansion speed is limited to a few hundred km/s, which also implies that it was ejected from the source more than $\approx 10^3$ yr preceding the onset of relativistic PRS plasma emission. These constraints may be satisfied by a white dwarf binary merger progenitor system, where a fraction of a solar mass was ejected at a slow speed during and after the merger, and a rapidly rotating neutron star was formed after $\sim10^3$ yr thermal evolution period of the merger remnant.

</details>


### [5] [Investigating the disappearance of a type-C QPO in black hole X-ray binary Swift J151857-572147 with AstroSat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26627)
*Biki Ram,Manoneeta Chakraborty,Mayukh Pahari*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文对黑洞低质量X射线双星Swift J151857.0-572147的AstroSat观测进行了时变与宽带光谱研究，发现源在S1和S2两个时间段内经历光谱态转变：S1阶段存在约8.02 Hz的C型准周期振荡（QPO）和低频凸起（LFB），S2阶段两者同时消失。能量依赖性分析表明RMS振幅和时滞随能量单调增加，指向低倾角源性质；QPO与LFB质心频率强正相关（Pearson系数0.98）。轨道分辨光谱分析显示冕的非热参数（光子指数、电子温度）在两段间显著偏离，而热参数无系统变化。两个特征的同时消失表明内吸积几何快速重组，强烈支持QPO起源于冕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞X射线双星中的准周期振荡（QPO）和低频噪声特征的起源与物理机制，特别是通过观测光谱态转变过程中这些特征的同步消失，来理解内吸积流和冕的几何重组过程。

Method: 利用AstroSat卫星对Swift J151857.0-572147进行时序和宽带光谱分析，将观测分为S1和S2两个时间段，进行能量依赖性RMS和时滞分析、幂密度谱（PDS）分析、硬度-强度图（HID）分析以及轨道分辨的宽带光谱拟合。

Result: S1阶段观测到约8.02 Hz的C型QPO和低频凸起（LFB），S2阶段两者同时消失；QPO的RMS振幅和时滞随能量单调增加（表明低倾角）；QPO与LFB频率强正相关（Pearson系数0.98）；HID揭示光谱态转变；冕的非热参数在S1到S2间显著变化而热参数无系统变化。

Conclusion: QPO和低频宽带噪声的同步消失表明内吸积几何发生了快速重组，结果强烈支持QPO起源于热内流（冕）而非吸积盘，两个特征的消失反映了康普顿化区域在光谱转变过程中的实质性转变。

Abstract: We present a timing and broadband spectral study of the AstroSat observation of the black hole low mass X-ray binary Swift J151857.0--572147, focusing on its evolution across two temporal segments, S1 and S2. During the first segment S1 lasting $\sim$ 50.6 ks, the source shows a clear type-C QPO at $\sim8.02$ Hz and a low-frequency bump (LFB) in the power density spectrum. By the later segment S2, the strength of the QPO feature decreases significantly and is no longer consistent with a coherent QPO, along with the simultaneous disappearance of the LFB. An energy-dependent analysis reveals that both the RMS amplitude and the time lag of the QPO in S1 increase monotonically with energy, suggesting a low inclination nature of the source. The QPO and LFB centroid frequencies show a strong positive correlation across orbital segments, with a Pearson coefficient of 0.98. Our variability analysis and hardness-intensity diagram reveal a spectral state transition of the source coincident with the turn-off of the QPO and the LFB, a conclusion supported by the evolution of broadband spectral parameters. Notably, the orbit-resolved spectral analysis reveals that the non-thermal parameters, photon index, and electron temperature of the corona exhibit significant deviation as the source traverses from S1 to S2, whereas the thermal parameters scatter without any systematic variation between the two segments. The simultaneous turn-off of the type-C QPO and the low-frequency broadband noise component, both originating from the hot inner flow, indicates a rapid reorganization of the inner accretion geometry. Our results strongly suggest a coronal origin for the QPO and demonstrate that the disappearance of both features reflects a substantial transformation of the Comptonizing region across the spectral transition.

</details>


### [6] [Spectro-Polarimetric Properties of CHIME FRB Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26654)
*Dengke Zhou,Yi Feng,Jiaying Xu,Chenyuan Xu,Jianhua Fang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用CHIME望远镜对28个重复FRB和70个非重复FRB进行系统光谱偏振分析，将重复源的σ_RM样本从14个扩展到36个（增加2.6倍）。重复源KDE峰值在1.3 rad m⁻²，约70%源σ_RM≥1 rad m⁻²，表明多数处于复杂磁离子环境中；而非重复源中没有源表现出显著去偏振（σ_RM≥5 rad m⁻²），约半数与恒定线偏振分数一致。作者指出CHIME有限频率覆盖可能影响结果，未来超宽带偏振观测将克服当前偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速射电暴（FRB）是毫秒级射电瞬变源，其偏振特性特别是低频去偏振参数σ_RM能揭示前身源周围的复杂磁离子介质，然而此前对重复FRB和非重复FRB的系统性σ_RM测量样本有限，缺乏大规模统计比较。

Method: 利用CHIME望远镜观测数据，对28个重复FRB测量σ_RM，对70个非重复FRB测试四种光谱偏振模型，并通过核密度估计（KDE）分析重复源σ_RM的分布特征，同时比较重复源与非重复源的去偏振行为差异。

Result: 重复FRB样本从14个扩展到36个（增加2.6倍），KDE峰值位于1.3 rad m⁻²，约70%源σ_RM≥1 rad m⁻²；非重复FRB中无源显示σ_RM≥5 rad m⁻²的显著去偏振，约半数与恒定线偏振分数一致。

Conclusion: 重复FRB多处于复杂磁离子环境，而非重复FRB普遍没有显著去偏振，暗示两类FRB的环境或起源可能不同；但CHIME有限频率覆盖可能引入观测偏差，未来超宽带偏振观测将提供更精确的σ_RM测量，深化对FRB环境的理解。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are enigmatic millisecond-duration radio transients whose polarization properties offer crucial insights into their origins and environments. In particular, low-frequency depolarization---quantified by the parameter \(σ_{\mathrm{RM}}\)---probes the complex magneto-ionic medium surrounding the progenitor, and has been observed across a population of repeating FRBs. We present a systematic spectro-polarimetric analysis of repeating and non-repeating FRBs using observations from the Canadian Hydrogen Intensity Mapping Experiment (CHIME). For 28 repeating FRBs, we measure \(σ_{\mathrm{RM}}\), expanding the known sample from 14 to 36 sources (an increase by a factor of 2.6). The kernel density estimate (KDE) of the repeating population peaks at \(1.3\ \mathrm{rad\,m^{-2}}\), with approximately 70\% of the sources showing \(σ_{\mathrm{RM}} \gtrsim 1\ \mathrm{rad\,m^{-2}}\), implying that most reside in complex magneto-ionic environments. For 70 non-repeating FRBs, we investigate four spectro-polarimetric models; no source exhibits significant depolarization with \(σ_{\mathrm{RM}} \gtrsim 5\ \mathrm{rad\,m^{-2}}\). Roughly half of the non-repeaters are consistent with a constant linear polarization fraction across frequency. We caution, however, that these results may be affected by the limited frequency coverage of CHIME. Future ultra-wideband polarimetry, spanning widely separated frequencies, will overcome current observational biases, enable precise \(σ_{\mathrm{RM}}\) measurements, and substantially deepen our understanding of FRB environments.

</details>


### [7] [Optical Discovery of New and Candidate Galactic Supernova Remnants Plus Optical Imaging of the Monogem Ring Supernova Remnant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26666)
*Robert A. Fesen,Marcel Drechsler,Bray Falls,Tim Schaeffer,Justin Anderson,Deepanshu Arora,Werner Becker,Carl Bjork,Steeve Body,Laurent Huet,Tarun Kottary,Mathew Ludgate,Nicolas Martino,Wolfgang Reich,Yann Sainty,Patrick Sparkman,Sean Walker*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过超过1500小时的Hα和[O III]窄带成像观测，利用业余级别设备发现了多个新的银河系超新星遗迹候选体，其中确认了5个（G27.8-17.1等）具有激波发射特征，并提出了2个新的候选体，同时揭示了Monogem Ring和G206.6+6.1等重要遗迹的光学结构。

Abstract: We investigate potential sources of ultrahigh-energy cosmic rays (UHECRs) and their acceleration mechanisms, focusing on astrophysical phenomena associated with massive stellar deaths and supermassive black holes. The composition of UHECRs from TDEs depends on the properties of the disrupted stars. AGN, particularly radio galaxies, remain promising sources, with acceleration occurring in their large-scale jets and lobes. Shear acceleration mechanisms have been proposed as a viable alternative for accelerating UHECRs, involving the re-acceleration of low-energy cosmic rays and being compatible with the observed spectrum and composition. Future multi-messenger observations, especially from upcoming observatories, are expected to provide critical data to refine our understanding of UHECR origins, test existing models, and explore new acceleration mechanisms.

The standard + narrow jet model fits the observed UHECR spectrum and composition, with the highest-energy flux dominated by the narrow-jet population confined to $z\le z_{\max,*}\simeq0.36$. For the narrow-jet GRB population, the fit implies a baryon loading factor $ξ_{\rm CR}^{\rm nj}\simeq10$. Abstract: We study Eratosthenes sieve as a discrete dynamic system. At each stage of the sieve there is a cycle of gaps ${\mathcal G}(p^\#)$ of length $φ(p^\#)$ and span $p^\#$. Abstract: We present a reduced-order modeling framework based on proper orthogonal decomposition for dynamic linear viscoelasticity governed by generalized Maxwell models. We further derive spatial error estimates and an error decomposition that separates the effects of spatial discretization, temporal discretization, and proper orthogonal decomposition truncation. The reduced formulation replaces the large full-order systems with low-dimensional problems, enabling efficient long-time simulations. Numerical experiments verify the predicted convergence behavior, assess different inner products for constructing the reduced basis, and demonstrate accurate recovery of transient oscillations and viscoelastic relaxation with substantial computational savings, even after accounting for snapshot generation and basis construction.

The same hybrid framework also plays a central role in the error analysis, where the weak formulation and quasi-uniform mesh structure of finite elements are combined with interpolation-based and nodewise finite difference method to control the strongly nonlinear damping term and to establish an optimal-order error estimate. Abstract: Sea ice dynamics plays a crucial role in the Earth's climate system, making it an important component of weather and climate prediction models. Its numerical simulation remains challenging, however, as it exhibits complex mechanical behaviors due to a nonlinear ice rheology and interactions with external physical forcings. In particular, sea ice presents linear kinematic features (LKFs), i.e., narrow bands of intense deformation associated with processes such as lead opening or pressure-ridge formation. Accurately representing these features is necessary as they affect thermodynamics and ocean-atmosphere exchange. Yet their number, localization, and structure are highly sensitive to spatial resolution and to the chosen discretization of the sea ice velocity. In this work, we investigate the spatial discretization of Hibler's viscous plastic sea-ice model using a fully discontinuous Galerkin (DG) representation of all variables, including the velocity field. The ability of DG elements to represent discontinuities while having high order local polynomial approximation makes them well suited for resolving the complex ice deformation features, and insuring robustness towards mesh-induced numerical artefacts. We assess the fully DG method using an established sea ice dynamics benchmark and compare the obtained sea ice deformation with state-of-the-art discretizations. Additionally, we study the theoretical convergence of the modified Elastic-Viscous-Plastic (mEVP) formulation as a pseudo-time iterative solver for the viscous-plastic (VP) momentum equations. We prove the convergence of the underlying continuous pseudo-time dynamical system towards the viscous plastic formal limit, thereby providing a theoretical foundation for the use of mEVP as an iterative solver for Hibler's VP model.

Abstract: This paper proposes a fast solver for time-harmonic elastic scattering by multiple particles embedded in layered media, with either Dirichlet or Neumann boundary conditions imposed on the particle surfaces. They are computationally challenging because of strong multiple scattering interactions among the particles and the layered interface. To reduce the cost for large particle systems, the resulting multiple scattering computation is further accelerated by the fast multipole method. The main formulation is developed in both two and three dimensions. Numerical experiments for rigid and traction-free particles validate the accuracy of the formulation, and demonstrate its flexibility in both direct scattering simulations and inverse scattering applications.

Abstract: Multiple scattering is a fundamental wave interaction phenomenon in acoustics and electromagnetics. The multipole expansion method (MEM) is the basis of many fast algorithms, such as the fast multipole method (FMM), for such problems. However, due to the infinitely many wave reflections involved, its convergence in three dimensions remains unexplored. In this paper, we prove spectral convergence of the MEM for time-harmonic acoustic scattering by finitely many well-separated spheres in three dimensions. Conclusion: 该工作为多类别交通流量建模提供了理论基础和数值方法，证明了模型具备良好的结构性质（双曲性和总线性退化性），并通过数值模拟验证了模型的有效性，为后续实际交通应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces a multi-class extension of a discrete-velocity kinetic traffic flow model based on a non-local Prigogine-Herman framework. We derive a hyperbolically scaled system of equations from a continuous kinetic formulation describing interactions between different vehicle classes through braking and relaxation terms. The model is then discretized with respect to the velocity variable for an arbitrary number of vehicle classes, and the structural properties of the resulting formulation are analyzed. In particular, we prove hyperbolicity and total linear degeneracy. Due to the non-conservative structure of the model, we employ a path-conservative finite volume scheme for the numerical approximation of the system. Finally, we derive the corresponding diffusively-corrected macroscopic multi-class model, investigate its stability and present numerical simulations on a single-lane road to illustrate the theoretical findings.

Abstract: In Abner J. Salgado and Ignacio Tomas. Diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta schemes: discrete energy-balance laws and compactness properties. J. Number. Math., 31(4):313-341, 2023, the notion of $U$-stability for Diagonally Implicit Runge-Kutta (DIRK) schemes was introduced. Here we establish the equivalence between $U$- and $B$- (algebraic) stability for two- and three-stage DIRK schemes, which then} provides suitable Bochner norm a priori estimates for $B$-stable methods. As applications, we first prove the convergence of $U$-stable DIRK schemes for linear coercive evolution problems under minimal regularity via energy estimates and compactness. Abstract: Operator learning in scientific machine learning is concerned with approximation of maps between infinite-dimensional function spaces; such maps frequently arise as the solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs). Neural operators have demonstrated broad empirical success at approximating such maps from data. However, most existing neural operator architectures enforce boundary conditions indirectly through training from data even though the boundary condition is often known exactly. Furthermore, existing modifications and approaches that do enforce boundary conditions explicitly suffer from impractical restrictions, including boundary smoothness, uniform grids, and separable, box-like domains. In this work, we propose an architecture which, independently of training, satisfies homogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions, whilst simultaneously retaining the expressivity of existing kernel-integral neural operator architectures. This is achieved by enforcing the property that the output of each layer is contained in the span of a subset of the homogeneous Dirichlet eigenfunctions of the Laplacian on the output domain. The method requires only that the output domain be bounded with Lipschitz boundary and places no restriction on the choice of discretization, making it applicable to arbitrary mesh data and general geometries. We prove universal approximation for the resulting architecture; furthermore the approach we adopt in the analysis proves universality for a broad class of kernel-integral neural operators thereby uniting existing theory for a variety of operator learning methods. We validate the proposed method on maps defined by the coefficient to solution map in 2D PDEs: Darcy flow on a square domain and the Helmholtz equation on a circular domain. Comparisons are made with alternative methods.

</details>

Conclusion: 所提出的多级交互粒子系统方法是一种高效且维度独立的技术，可计算极端事件概率，其收敛性能优于经典多级蒙特卡洛方法，并能有效聚焦于临界区域。

Abstract: We propose a novel method that combines a multilevel decomposition with the framework of Interacting Particle Systems to compute the probability that given quantities of interest take values above or below a certain pre-specified value. Through the application of a sequential sampling scheme, the algorithm is able to achieve convergence rates faster than those achieved by the classical Multilevel Monte Carlo algorithm, and competes with the current state of the art techniques, while also being dimension independent. Furthermore, the sample points generated by the algorithm form a sequence which concentrates near the critical value of the quantity of interest. Our results are rigorously established and later verified through numerical experiments.

</details>

<details>

Method: 设G是π-可解群，p为素数，σ=π∪{p}，则G存在Hall σ-子群H。作者通过对比Irr_{σ'}(G)与Irr_{σ'}(N_G(H))的基数来证明等式的成立。

Abstract: We prove a Hall $σ$-subgroup analogue of the McKay conjecture for $π$-separable groups proposed by G. Navarro. This result simultaneously generalizes the classical McKay conjecture and its $π$-separable version. More precisely, if $G$ is $π$-separable, $p$ is a prime and $σ=π\cup\{p\}$, then it is known that there exists $H$ a Hall $σ$-subgroup of $G$. In this case, we prove that $|{\rm Irr}_{σ'}(G)|=|{\rm Irr}_{σ'}(\mathbf{N}_{G}(H))|$.

</details>

Method: 作者通过建立一个充分条件，使得在$n$为偶数时，可以从"Auslander-Reiten$(n+2)$-角生成Grothendieck群关系统"这一前提推出范畴是局部有限的。此外，作者进一步构造并验证了满足该充分条件的一大类具体例子。

Conclusion: 本文对偶数$n$情形下的开放问题给出了部分肯定回答，通过在所建立的充分条件下推广了Zhou的定理，并展示了该条件的广泛适用性，为最终完全解决这一开放问题提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal C$ be an $(n+2)$-angulated category. Zhou proved that, when $n$ is odd, if the Auslander-Reiten $(n+2)$-angles generate the relations for the Grothendieck group of $\mathcal C$, then $\mathcal C$ is locally finite. Whether the corresponding statement remains valid for even $n$ is still open. In this paper, we give a partial affirmative answer to this problem by establishing a sufficient condition under which the same implication holds for even $n$. We further show that our sufficient condition is satisfied by a broad class of examples, thereby demonstrating that the result extends well beyond isolated cases.

Abstract: In this paper, we construct a lifting operator from the Grothendieck group of admissible Harish-Chandra modules of $G =\mathrm{SO}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$ (resp. $\mathrm{Sp}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$) to that of genuine representations of $\mathrm{Spin}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$ (resp. $\mathrm{Spin}_{2n+1}(\mathbb C)$). As a consequence, the lifting operator preserves unitarity on a large class of unitary representations.

Abstract: We investigate the form of the Saito metric on quotients of the reflection representation of an extended affine Weyl group, restricted to an arbitrary stratum of its discriminant. Method: 采用Tits-Kantor-Koecher（TKK）构造与分裂Lie代数构造的统一来构建Jordan分裂群；利用根系R的grading与标准抛物子代数𝔭_Θ的关系进行分类；通过定义A_{R,S}的Jordan代数点来参数化G-局部系统，并在圆盘情形下给出非交换簇结构。

Conclusion: 本文建立了簇簇、Jordan代数与代数群之间的深层联系，为Berenstein-Fomin-Zelevinsky、Guichard-Wienhard等经典理论的统一推广提供了新的框架，并为正性理论提供了代数层面的证明。

Abstract: In this article, we construct noncommutative cluster varieties, $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$, for each reduced root system $R$ and marked surface $S$ simultaneously generalizing the cluster varieties of Fock-Goncharov, Li, Goncharov-Shen, Berenstein-Retakh, Goncharov-Kontsevich, and our previously introduced polygonal cluster algebras. Additionally, we define a large class of algebraic groups, we call Jordan split groups. Given a reduced root system $R$ and a family of Jordan algebras, the Lie algebra for $G$ is constructed by unifying the Tits-Kantor-Koecher construction for a single Jordan algebra with the construction of a split Lie algebra.
  The notion of Jordan split groups is closely related to a grading of its Lie algebra by the root system $R$. We show that these gradings are usually induced by a choice of standard parabolic subalgebra $\mathfrak{p}_Θ$ and we classify $R$-graded pairs $(G,Θ)$ via a condition depending only on the subset $Θ\subset Δ$ of the set of simple roots. Next, we define Jordan algebra points of $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$ which parameterize $G$-local systems on $S$ with boundary decoration related to cosets $G/U_Θ$ when $G$ is Jordan split of type $R$. When $S$ is a disk, points of $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$ parameterize configurations of decorated flags. We use this to give noncommutative cluster structures on the double $R$-Bruhat cells of $G$, generalizing the cluster algebras of Berenstein-Fomin-Zelevinsky. When each Jordan algebra is formally real, we say that $G$ has a positive structure with respect to $Θ$. This defines a positive semigroup in $G$. For real algebraic groups, the pairs $(G,Θ)$ which have positive structures are exactly those which admit a positive structure as defined by Guichard-Wienhard and we give algebraic proofs of many of the properties of positive configurations of flags and of positive representations.

</details>

Method: 构建双目标两阶段随机优化模型，第一阶段决策设施永久加固，第二阶段决策情景依赖的疏散方案。开发精确Benders分解算法和可扩展的Lagrangian对偶方法（含定制原始恢复），并将两者嵌入自适应程序以生成信息丰富的Pareto前沿。

Result: 以德克萨斯州3,752家医院和养老院为案例：仅最小化经济成本会系统性地对位于社会脆弱区域的设施分配更少的保护；沿Pareto前沿适度移动可在有限额外成本下大幅降低中断影响。精确分解方法可求解所有测试实例（包括大规模压力测试，此时广泛公式受内存限制），Lagrangian方法则提供更快的近似高质量解。

Abstract: Flooding can damage healthcare facilities, interrupt local care capacity, and trigger costly patient evacuations. Addressing these risks requires long-term resilience investments under uncertainty. We develop a bi-objective two-stage stochastic optimization model that jointly determines permanent facility hardening and scenario-dependent evacuation decisions. The first objective minimizes expected evacuation, physical damage, and business-interruption costs. The second minimizes a service-disruption impact index that combines the duration and scale of service loss with a place-based social-vulnerability weight. We develop an exact Benders decomposition and a scalable Lagrangian-dual method with tailored primal recovery, embedding both within an adaptive procedure for constructing informative Pareto frontiers. A case study of 3,752 hospitals and nursing homes in Texas evaluates the framework under climate-informed tropical-cyclone flood scenarios. The results show that minimizing economic losses alone can systematically allocate less protection to facilities located in socially vulnerable areas, while moderate movement along the Pareto frontier can substantially reduce disruption impacts at limited additional expected cost. The exact decomposition solves all tested instances, including larger stress tests for which the extensive formulation becomes memory-limited, while the Lagrangian method provides substantially faster high-quality solutions.

</details>

Abstract: This paper develops directional necessary optimality conditions for constrained optimization problems in Asplund spaces. Under directional metric subregularity, we first derive a directional optimality condition in terms of limiting subdifferentials. We then introduce sufficient conditions for directional metric subregularity and establish their relationships with directional pseudo-normality and quasi-normality. For systems with joint constraints, we further propose a joint criterion for directional metric subregularity and use it to obtain necessary optimality conditions. The results not only extend several finite-dimensional directional constructions to an Asplund-space setting, but also yield conclusions that are new even in finite dimensions.

Abstract: We study optimization problems for separable functionals of the Euclidean or spherical lengths of dual edge pairs in finite extremal unit-diameter configurations in three dimensions. For a fixed diameter graph, these problems lead to nonconvex constrained optimization of the vertex coordinates. We first prove that a convergent sequence of extremal configurations retains an extremal geometric core after coincident points are merged and vertices incident to at most one diameter are removed. A spherical Crofton argument then gives a sharp lower bound for additive concave functionals, attained by the regular tetrahedron. We also analyze the effect of inserting or deleting dangling vertices. For the sum of products of spherical dual-edge lengths, we obtain an exact supremal reformulation of the three-dimensional Blaschke--Lebesgue area problem. The numerical study uses all 10,644 available extremal configurations with at most 16 vertices. We combine direct evaluation with gradient-based local optimization on each fixed graph and reconstruct the intrinsic diameter graph and its dual pairs after vertex collisions. For every supplied graph, the selected verified endpoint contains a regular tetrahedron. These computations do not certify the global maximum for any fixed graph, but they motivate structural conjectures connecting tetrahedral containment with the Blaschke--Lebesgue problem.

Abstract: The integration of fixed-route public transit and on-demand mobility services presents both a modeling challenge and a computational opportunity for large-scale network design. To achieve tractability at urban scale, we develop a novel column generation heuristic scheme with tailored pricing subproblems. Applied to networks and demand in Boston and Chicago, the framework yields operationally feasible designs that substantially increase demand served. Relative to transit-only and on-demand-only baselines, ridership increases by up to 20.99% and 93.58% in Boston, and by up to 10.63% and 149.84% in Chicago. Compared to a multi-modal benchmark, our approach improves ridership by 5.42% and 5.80% in Boston and Chicago, respectively. These results demonstrate that (i) joint co-design of transit routes, frequencies, and on-demand legs within a unified optimization framework yields substantially greater ridership than single-mode or decoupled approaches under equivalent budget constraints; and (ii) the proposed formulation and column generation pricing scheme admit tractable, operationally feasible, high-performing solutions relative to tested baselines.

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出循环Lyapunov函数（RLF）的概念，将Lyapunov直接方法中对子水平集前向不变的要求放宽为循环性（recurrence），并基于此开发了无需显式Lyapunov函数的GPU轨迹数据稳定性认证算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lyapunov直接方法是稳定性分析的核心工具，但寻找合适的Lyapunov函数通常需要巧思或大量计算。传统方法要求Lyapunov函数的每个子水平集都前向不变，这使其几何性质与系统轨迹紧密耦合，极大限制了方法的适用性和计算效率。

Method: 作者将不变性（invariance）放宽为循环性（recurrence），即集合内任意轨迹在有限时间内返回该集合，由此定义Recurrent Lyapunov Function（RLF）。在温和条件下证明RLF保证稳定性，并提出更强版本以实现渐近稳定和指数稳定。基于范数给出逆向定理，并开发了基于GPU的算法，仅从轨迹数据即可认证（实用）稳定性，无需寻找Lyapunov函数。

Result: 认证到ε邻域的稳定性仅需O(log(1/ε))次轨迹评估，常数随认证衰减率接近真实衰减率而增长，揭示了内在的性能-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 通过以循环性替换不变性，绕过了传统Lyapunov函数构造的瓶颈，为数据驱动的稳定性认证提供了一种高效且可扩展的框架，并揭示了精度与计算成本之间的本质权衡关系。

Abstract: Lyapunov's direct method is a cornerstone of stability and control, but it hinges on finding a Lyapunov function, a task demanding ingenuity or computation. A key difficulty is that every sub-level set of the function must be forward invariant, coupling its geometry to the system's trajectories. We relax this by replacing invariance with recurrence: a set is recurrent if every trajectory starting in it returns within a finite time. This yields the notion of a Recurrent Lyapunov Function (RLF), whose sub-level sets need only be recurrent. We show that, under mild conditions, RLFs guarantee stability, and we introduce stronger notions yielding asymptotic and exponential stability. We also give norm-based converse theorems: under the corresponding stability conditions, any norm is an RLF for their practical versions. We then develop GPU-based algorithms that certify (practical) stability from trajectory data alone, without a Lyapunov function. Certifying stability up to an $\varepsilon$-neighborhood needs only $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ trajectory evaluations, with constants growing as the certified decay rate nears the true one, exposing an intrinsic performance-cost trade-off.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce finite-dimensional approximations to the space of convex bodies based on polytopes in vertex representation. For a prescribed set of directions, the admissible point configurations form a polyhedral convex subcone of the Euclidean vector space, described by a finite system of linear inequalities. The interior of this cone contains only nondegenerate representations, in which all parameter points are distinct vertices of the represented polytope. We study the geometry of the parameter cone, redundancies in its defining inequalities, and natural projections of convex bodies onto the resulting polytope spaces. We derive quantitative approximation estimates in terms of how densely the prescribed directions cover the unit sphere and construct nested Galerkin sequences whose approximation error converges locally uniformly to zero. Finally, we use these approximation properties to establish the convergence of finite-dimensional approximations of constrained global optimization problems in the space of convex bodies.

Then we prove that the outer sequence generated by BPDHG approaches the optimal solution set of the LP problem we considered. Furthermore, we integrate the barrier technique into the {Primal Dual Linear Programming} (PDLP) framework to develop the corresponding Barrier PDLP (BPDLP) method. Numerical experiments show that the barrier modification can alleviate prolonged plateaus in the KKT residual on selected instances. We also investigate an empirical instance-dependent indicator for identifying LP problems on which BPDLP is more likely to outperform PDLP.

TL;DR: 本文研究ℓ_p几何中寻找小梯度查询点的一阶预言复杂度，重点考察在未知光滑度和距离尺度下需要多少信息才能自适应。在严格计数的局部值-梯度模型中，若无非退化局部尺度观测，则不存在仅依赖LR/ε的有限复杂度界。本文解决了Diakonikolas提出的通用ℓ_p参数无关扩展问题（对所有固定1<p<∞），建立了与已知参数情形匹配的维度无关确定性上界，并给出了参数无关的后初始化复杂度：1<p<2时为O_p(Ḡ^{1/2})，p=2时为O(Ḡ^{1/2})，p>2时为O_p(Ḡ^{p/(p+2)})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主要动机是解决无梯度/光滑度信息条件下的优化复杂度理论问题。具体而言，Diakonikolas提出了通用ℓ_p空间中的参数无关扩展问题，即在不知道光滑常数L、初始解距离R和最优值f*的情况下，如何高效找到梯度范数不超过ε的查询点。需要在严格的局部值-梯度预言模型中刻画所需的信息复杂度。

Method: 首先通过一维构造证明在严格计数模型中，若无非退化局部尺度观测，任何有限查询预算都无法仅依靠LR/ε界来保证成功。然后提出非退化割线初始化方法，该方法不需要知道L、R或f*。对于固定p>2，建立维度无关的确定性已知参数上界指数p/(p+2)，与已有下界匹配。有限局部例程适配相同的可观测尺度-半径流程，从而在未知L和R的情况下保持该指数。技术核心是使用割线初始化来获取局部尺度信息，结合可观测的尺度-半径估计实现参数无关。

Result: 对于所有固定1<p<∞，给出了参数无关的复杂度结果：后初始化配对预言复杂度为O_p(Ḡ^{1/2})（1<p<2）、O(Ḡ^{1/2})（p=2）、O_p(Ḡ^{p/(p+2)})（p>2），其中Ḡ=max{1,LR/ε}；各环节附加校准成本为O_p(log(e+L/M_0))。同时证明了在严格计数局部值-梯度模型中，没有非退化局部尺度观测就无法获得仅依赖LR/ε的有限复杂度界。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Diakonikolas的通用ℓ_p参数无关扩展问题（对所有固定的1<p<∞），表明在获得非退化割线初始化后，无需知道L、R或f*即可实现与已知参数情形同阶的复杂度。同时揭示了严格计数模型中局部尺度观测的不可回避性——这是参数无关自适应的必要前提。

Abstract: We study the first-order oracle complexity of finding a queried point with small gradient in $\ell_p$ geometry, with particular attention to the information needed to adapt the unknown smoothness and distance scales. In the strict counted local value--gradient model, no finite complexity bound can depend only on $LR/\eps$ without a nondegenerate local scale observation: a one-dimensional construction keeps $LR/\eps=4$ while defeating every prescribed finite query budget.
  We resolve Diakonikolas's general-$\ell_p$ parameter-free extension question for every fixed $1<p<\infty$. Under a nondegenerate secant initialization, the method knows neither the smoothness constant $L$, the initial solution distance $R$, nor $f^*$, and returns a queried point $\widehat x$ with $\|\nabla f(\widehat x)\|_q\le\eps$. For fixed finite $p>2$, we first establish the dimension-free deterministic known-parameter upper exponent $p/(p+2)$ in $K=LR/\eps$, matching the published lower polynomial exponent under its horizon and dimension qualifications. The finite local routine fits the same observable scale--radius procedure, so this exponent is preserved without knowing $L$ or $R$. Writing $\Kbar=\max\{1,LR/\eps\}$, the post-initialization pair-oracle complexity is $O_p(\Kbar^{1/2})$ for $1<p<2$, $O(\Kbar^{1/2})$ for $p=2$, and $O_p(\Kbar^{p/(p+2)})$ for $p>2$, together with the additive calibration cost $O_p(\log(e+L/M_0))$ in every regime.

</details>

*Yi Du*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文确定了Nesterov快速梯度法（FGM）在光滑凸函数上，任意视野N≥7时最小查询梯度范数的精确最坏情况值，即 L²R²/Σt_k²。通过构造一个FGM特定的球面多胞形K_N及投影包络函数，构造了与松弛PEP上界匹配的解析族，填补了此前仅数值验证的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kim和Fessler证明了松弛PEP上界，并在选定的视野上给出了精确插值PEP的紧致数值解，但缺少一个在整个视野范围N≥7上统一有效的解析匹配族。此前关于Nesterov方法最坏情形梯度范数的精确刻画存在空白。

Method: 针对每个N≥7，构造一个FGM特定的球面多胞形K_N，并使用标准的投影包络函数f_N(x)=max_{g∈K_N}{<x,g>-½‖g‖²}，其中∇f_N(x)=Proj_{K_N}(x)。K_N的顶点由三维种子通过一维球面锥提升生成，该提升保留所有投影不等式并在每个视野将对抗维度增加一。关键新要素包括：FGM特定的代数种子、证明其达到松弛界、以及将精确性传播到所有N≥7的共纬度提升。

Result: 对所有N≥7和所有d≥N-4，证明了sup_{f∈F_{0,L}(R^d)} min_{0≤k≤N}‖∇f(x_k)‖² = L²R²/Σ_{k=0}^N t_k²，其中每个查询梯度的范数均相同，且精确达到松弛PEP上界。

Conclusion: 本文给出了Nesterov快速梯度法在光滑凸函数上最坏情形梯度范数的精确解析刻画，通过构造投影包络函数族的统一方法，将精确性从数值验证提升为解析证明，适用于所有N≥7的视野。作者也指出并非每个rank-one松弛PEP都允许这样的种子构造。

Abstract: We determine the exact worst-case value, at every horizon $N\geq7$, of the smallest queried gradient norm generated by Nesterov's fast gradient method on smooth convex functions. Let $t_0=1$ and $t_{k+1}=(1+\sqrt{1+4t_k^2})/2$, and let $x_0,\ldots,x_N$ denote the points at which the method evaluates gradients. For every such $N$ and every dimension $d\geq N-4$, we prove \[
  \sup_{\substack{f\in\F_{0,L}(\R^d),\ x_\star\in\arg\min f
  \norm{x_0-x_\star}\leq R}}
  \min_{0\leq k\leq N}\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}^2
  =\frac{L^2R^2}{\sum_{k=0}^N t_k^2}. \] The relaxed-PEP upper bound is due to Kim and Fessler, who also reported tight numerical solutions of the exact-interpolation PEP at selected horizons. What remained missing was an analytic matching family valid uniformly over the horizon. For every $N\geq7$, we construct such a family using an FGM-specific spherical polytope $K_N$ and the standard projection-envelope function \[
  f_N(x)=\max_{g\in K_N}\left\{\ip{x}{g}-\frac12\norm{g}^2\right\},
  \qquad \nabla f_N(x)=\Proj_{K_N}(x). \] Every queried gradient has the same norm, and the vertices of $K_N$ are generated from a three-dimensional seed by a one-dimensional spherical cone lift. The lift preserves all projection inequalities and raises the adversary dimension by one at each horizon. The projection/Moreau-envelope template itself is classical; the new ingredients are the FGM-specific algebraic seed, the proof that it attains the relaxed bound, and the common-latitude lift that propagates this exactness to every $N\geq7$. We state precise hypotheses for that propagation and do not claim that every rank-one relaxed PEP admits such a seed.

</details>

Abstract: We study the deterministic first-order oracle complexity of finding stationary points of the value function in smooth nonconvex-Polyak-Łojasiewicz (NC-PŁ) minimax optimization. We assume that the objective is jointly $\ell$-smooth and satisfies the $μ$-PŁ condition in the dual variable, and that its value function $Φ(x):=\max_y f(x;y)$ satisfies $Φ(0)-\inf_xΦ(x)\leqΔ$. When $κ:=\ell/μ\gtrsim 1$ and $0<ε^2\lesssim\ellΔ$, we prove that every deterministic first-order method requires $Ω(\ellΔκ/ε^2)$ oracle queries in the worst case to find $x$ satisfying $\|\nablaΦ(x)\|\leqε$. This rate matches the known upper bound in its dependence on $(\ell,Δ,κ,ε)$ [Yang et al., 2022] and shows that the linear dependence on $κ$ is unavoidable for deterministic first-order methods.

</details>


### [82] [Mean-covariance turnpikes in Wasserstein distributionally robust linear-quadratic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26986)
*Yanzhi Wu,Zhengping Ji*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究离散时间随机线性系统在经验扰动数据下的长时域Wasserstein惩罚极小极大控制问题。由于对抗性扰动分布诱导时变的均值和协方差动力学，标准turnpike论证无法直接适用。作者刻画了（可能非中心数据的）一般非零均值参考，证明了状态均值、伴随、控制、最坏情况扰动均值及闭环协方差的时域一致双向指数turnpike估计，并构建了混合策略，证明其最坏情况代价差距随终端层长度指数衰减且对时域一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域鲁棒控制中，传统turnpike性质依赖于时不变的系统结构，但Wasserstein惩罚下的对抗扰动分布会引入时变的均值和协方差动力学，打破标准假设。需要新的理论框架来刻画此类问题的最优解结构，并降低长时域计算成本。

Method: (1) 通过约化的凸-凹Hamiltonian鞍点问题刻画非零均值参考；(2) 推导状态均值、伴随、控制、最坏情况扰动均值和闭环协方差的时域一致双向指数turnpike估计；(3) 构造混合策略：时不变仿射反馈 + 终端层的有限时域操纵，并分析其代价差距的指数衰减性质。

Result: 证明了在长时域下各关键量（均值状态、伴随、控制、最坏扰动均值、协方差）大部分时间停留在静态参考附近，且该性质对时域一致。混合策略的最坏情况代价差距随终端层长度指数衰减且对时域一致，有效降低长时域鲁棒控制的计算成本。

Conclusion: 该工作为Wasserstein惩罚的鲁棒最优控制建立了完整的turnpike理论，并提供了计算高效的混合策略设计方案，数值实验验证了估计结果及其对Wasserstein惩罚参数的依赖关系。

Abstract: We study long-horizon Wasserstein-penalized minimax control for discrete-time stochastic linear systems with empirical disturbance data, in which adversarial disturbance distributions induce time-varying mean and covariance dynamics, making standard turnpike arguments not directly applicable. For possibly uncentered data, we characterize the generally nonzero mean reference through a reduced convex-concave Hamiltonian saddle problem. We prove horizon-uniform, two-sided exponential turnpike estimates for the mean state, adjoint, control, worst-case disturbance mean, and closed-loop covariance, showing that they spend the majority of time near static references when the horizon is long. We further construct a hybrid policy combining time-independent affine feedback with finite-horizon steering over a terminal layer, proving that its worst-case cost gap decays exponentially with the terminal-layer length uniformly in the horizon, which helps reducing the computation cost for long-horizon robust controls. Numerical examples illustrate the estimates and their dependence on the Wasserstein penalty.

</details>

Result: 在E-最优实验设计和最大代数连通性问题上的实验表明，该方法在大规模实例和具有额外组合结构的问题上优于SCIP-SDP；而SCIP-SDP在中等规模、约束较简单的实例上表现更好。

Conclusion: 所提出的分支定界与熵平滑、Frank-Wolfe相结合的方法在求解大规模、结构复杂的组合特征值优化问题上具有显著优势，为这类问题提供了有效的替代方案。

Abstract: We study the maximization of the minimum eigenvalue under combinatorial and integrality constraints. We propose a new approach based on branch-and-bound combines entropic smoothing of the minimum eigenvalue function and Frank-Wolfe methods over concave relaxations of the constraints, thereby exploiting combinatorial structure through linear optimization oracles. We establish approximation and convergence guarantees, including for truncated gradients computed from partial eigendecompositions, and introduce rank- and eigenvalue-based pruning and duality-based variable fixing. We evaluate the method on E-optimal experimental design and maximum algebraic connectivity problems and compare it with SCIP-SDP. The results show that our approach is particularly effective for large-dimensional instances and problems with additional combinatorial structure, whereas SCIP-SDP performs better on moderately sized instances with simpler constraints.

</details>


### [84] [Exact low-dimensional reformulations for regularized spectral approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27052)

TL;DR: 本文研究了正则化谱逼近问题及其加权变体的精确低维重构。无权重情况下，保真项弱占优单调性可实现奇异值上的精确重构；有权重情况下，需在兼容性和同时对角化条件下建立该重构，并给出反例说明缺少同时对角化时重构会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化谱逼近问题在高维数据和机器学习中应用广泛，探索其低维精确重构条件有助于降低计算复杂度，并为加权情形提供理论保证。

Method: 利用弱占优（weak-majorization）单调性分析无权重情形下的奇异值精确重构；在加权情形下引入兼容性（compatibility）与同时对角化（simultaneous diagonalization）条件构建精确重构，并通过构造反例验证条件的必要性。

Result: 无权重情形下建立了基于奇异值的精确低维重构；加权情形下证明了在兼容性和同时对角化条件下精确重构成立，同时提供反例表明缺少同时对角化时重构可能失败。

Conclusion: 低维重构的有效性取决于问题的结构条件——无权重时由弱占优单调性保证，加权时则需要兼容性与同时对角化作为充分条件，且同时对角化是至关重要的前提。

Abstract: We study exact low-dimensional reformulations for a regularized spectral approximation problem and its weighted variant. In the unweighted setting, weak-majorization monotonicity of the fidelity term enables an exact reformulation over singular values. In the weighted setting, we establish such a reformulation under compatibility and simultaneous diagonalization conditions. We further show, via a counterexample, that this exact reformulation can fail in the absence of simultaneous diagonalization.

</details>


### [85] [Classification of facial exposedness of completely positive cones over symmetric cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27062)
*Mitsuhiro Nishijima*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于Euclidean Jordan代数的秩，对对称锥上的完全正锥的"面暴露性"进行了完整分类：秩≤2时面暴露，秩≥5时非面暴露，秩为3或4时给出了精确刻画。该分类结果与完全正锥的谱面性分类一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全正锥在优化与矩阵理论中具有重要地位，面暴露性是锥分析中一个核心几何性质，影响算法的收敛性与对偶理论。已有工作对完全正锥的谱面性（spectrahedrality）进行了分类，但面暴露性尚缺乏系统性刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者利用Euclidean Jordan代数的结构理论，通过将对称锥上的完全正锥的面结构与其关联Jordan代数的秩联系起来，针对秩为3和4的情形构造了具体的不变量或反例来刻画面暴露性，并在秩为1、2和≥5的情形下给出了统一的判定结果。

Result: 得到完全正锥面暴露性的完整分类：秩≤2时具有面暴露性；秩≥5时不具有面暴露性；秩为3和4时的面暴露性不单纯由秩决定，但给出了每种情况下的精确刻画。该分类与已有完全正锥谱面性的分类完全吻合。

Conclusion: 完全正锥的面暴露性与其Euclidean Jordan代数的秩之间存在清晰的对应关系，且该分类与谱面性分类一致，表明这两种几何性质在完全正锥上可能受相同的内在结构因素驱动。这一结果为锥最优化理论中面暴露性和谱表示之间的关系提供了新的理解。

Abstract: We classify the facial exposedness of completely positive cones over symmetric cones in terms of the rank of the associated Euclidean Jordan algebras. The completely positive cones are facially exposed when the rank is at most $2$, but are not facially exposed when the rank is at least $5$. Facial exposedness is not completely determined by the rank when the rank is $3$ or $4$, but we provide a characterization in each case. The resulting classification of facial exposedness in fact agrees with the corresponding classification of spectrahedrality for completely positive cones.

</details>


### [86] [Randomized Quasi-Gauss--Newton Methods for Solving General Nonlinear Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27084)
*Chengchang Liu,Luo Luo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一般非线性方程组的局部收敛性，提出了基于Gram矩阵近似的随机拟高斯-牛顿方法，分别解决了欠定和过定两种情形下的收敛问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拟牛顿方法大多只关注方形方程组，且要求初始Jacobian估计足够接近真实值，这限制了其在实际问题中的应用。本文旨在突破这些限制，为更一般的非线性方程组（欠定和过定）提供高效的求解方法。

Method: 提出了基于Gram矩阵近似的随机拟高斯-牛顿方法。对于欠定情形，方法收敛到最优解；对于过定情形，方法收敛到非线性最小二乘公式的驻点。方法通过随机化Gram矩阵近似来避免传统拟牛顿方法对初始Jacobian估计的严格要求。

Result: 欠定情形下，方法实现局部超线性收敛到最优解；过定情形下，方法实现局部无条件数依赖（condition-number-free）的收敛到非线性最小二乘公式的驻点。

Conclusion: 本文所提出的随机拟高斯-牛顿方法扩展了拟牛顿方法的应用范围，从方形系统推广到欠定和过定系统，且不需要初始Jacobian估计接近精确值，具有更广泛的实际适用性。

Abstract: This paper considers the local convergence for solving general nonlinear equations. We establish randomized quasi-Gauss--Newton methods based on the approximation of the Gram matrix, addressing both the underdetermined and the overdetermined settings. For the underdetermined case, we show that our methods achieve local superlinear convergence to the optimal solution. For the overdetermined case, we show that our methods achieve local condition-number-free convergence to the stationary point of the nonlinear least-square formulation of the problem. In contrast, existing quasi-Newton methods mostly focus on square systems and additionally require the initial Jacobian estimate to be sufficiently close to the exact one.

</details>


### [87] [Co-Optimized Generation, Transmission, and Storage Expansion: System Value and Optimal Duration of Pumped-Storage Hydropower](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27222)
*Rafael Benchimol Klausner,Rafael Kelman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个发电-输电-储能扩建规划框架，允许不同时长的储能候选方案在年化成本基础上竞争，使储能时长组合成为优化结果。应用于巴西互联系统2030-2050年情景，发现抽水蓄能（PSH）可用时模型选择建设31.2 GW/755 GWh的PSH而非电池储能，可降低年化系统成本6.8%。核心结论是：将储能时长限制为单一固定值会低估系统最优储能需求并高估热力容量的剩余需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩建规划模型在优化前就固定储能时长（如4小时），只决定建多少储能功率，而不决定放电时长。这忽略了储能时长本身是优化变量，可能严重低估或扭曲系统最优的储能配置方案。

Method: 构建一个滚动时域、两阶段随机线性规划的发电-输电-储能扩建框架。每个五年的阶段包含一整年小时分辨率数据，覆盖十个一致的水文、风能和太阳能场景。储能候选包括多种时长的方案（4-144小时PSH及4小时BESS），按年化成本竞争。

Result: 有PSH可用时：建设31.2 GW/755 GWh的PSH（无BESS），以12小时时长为主并有4.9 GW的72小时机组；无PSH时建设38 GW/152 GWh的BESS和额外7.8 GW燃气（主要是开式循环尖峰机组）。PSH使2050年化系统成本降低50亿美元/年（6.8%），运行成本降23.5%，热力发电减少34 TWh/年，长期边际成本降20%，2035年VRE弃电率从8.4%降至3.2%。

Conclusion: 将储能候选限制为单一固定时长会低估系统最优能量存储需求并高估热力备用容量需求。巴西的具体数值具有区域特异性，但机制是普适的。

Abstract: Expansion planning models usually fix storage duration before optimization, setting how much storage power to build but not for how long it can discharge. We present a generation-transmission-storage expansion framework in which candidates of many durations compete on annualized cost, making the duration mix an optimization outcome. It is a rolling-horizon, two-stage stochastic linear program, each five-year stage is a full year at hourly resolution under ten coherent inflow, wind, and solar scenarios. We apply it to the Brazilian Interconnected System over 2030-2050, where demand roughly doubles to 1,716 TWh/year and variable renewable energy (VRE) supplies most new capacity. Two cases are compared: 36 pumped-storage hydropower (PSH) candidates over four subsystems and nine durations (4-144 h) alongside 4-h battery energy storage systems (BESS), and BESS alone.
  With PSH available, the model builds 31.2 GW / 755 GWh of PSH and no BESS, dominated by 12-h capacity with 4.9 GW of 72-h units. Without PSH it builds 38 GW / 152 GWh of BESS and 7.8 GW more gas-fired capacity, mostly open-cycle peakers. PSH lowers 2050 annualized system cost by US$ 5.0 billion/year (6.8%), operating cost by 23.5%, thermal generation by 34 TWh/year, and long-run marginal cost by 20%; 2035 VRE curtailment falls from 8.4% to 3.2%. The magnitudes are specific to Brazil, but the underlying mechanism is general: limiting storage candidates to a single duration understates the system's optimal energy-storage requirement and overstates its residual need for thermal capacity.

</details>


### [88] [Asymptotic consensus and flocking under decaying persistent excitation on rooted digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27263)
*Chiara Cicolani,Elisa Continelli,Cristina Pignotti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有非普适交互、时滞和可能通信失败的一阶与二阶对齐模型，将已有结果扩展到仅需根图假设和更弱的持续激励条件，证明一阶Hegselmann-Krause模型在权重多项式衰减下仍能达成共识，二阶Cucker-Smale模型在额外假设下仍能实现群集行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐模型研究通常假设较强的连通性条件或较严格的持续激励条件，而实际多智能体系统中通信可能随时间逐渐减弱且交互图不满足强连通性。本文旨在放宽这些条件，探索在交互强度多项式衰减的情况下集体行为是否仍能涌现。

Method: 将一阶Hegselmann-Krause型模型和二阶Cucker-Smale模型置于仅假设根图（rooted digraph）和更弱持久激励条件（Persistence Excitation Condition）的框架下分析。允许固定长度时间区间内的交互量随时间呈多项式衰减，并在通信权重衰减指数与根节点最大距离之间建立合适的条件关系。

Result: 一阶Hegselmann-Krause型模型在通信权重衰减指数与根节点最大距离满足适当条件时证明渐近收敛到共识；二阶Cucker-Smale模型在对影响函数衰减施加额外假设后建立渐近群集（flocking）。结果表明即使通信逐渐减弱且不要求强连通交互图，集体行为仍可涌现。

Conclusion: 本文显著放宽了对齐模型的理论条件，证明在更弱的图假设和更宽松的激励条件下，共识与群集行为仍能实现，为理解现实世界中通信劣化环境下的多智能体协同提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate first- and second-order alignment models with non-universal interaction, time delays and possible communication failures, extending the results in [17] to interaction digraphs that are only assumed to be rooted and to a weaker Persistence Excitation Condition. In particular, we allow the amount of interaction over time intervals of fixed length to decay polynomially in time.
  For the first-order Hegselmann-Krause type model, we prove asymptotic convergence to consensus under a suitable condition relating the decay exponent of the communication weights to the maximal distance from the root. For the second-order Cucker-Smale model, we establish asymptotic flocking under an additional assumption on the decay of the influence function. These results show that collective behavior can still emerge under progressively weakening communication and without requiring strong connectivity of the interaction graph.

</details>


### [89] [Marine Autonomous Vehicle Fleet Scheduling to Maximise Scientific Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27271)
*Mehdi El Krari,Jonathan Smith,Maria Fox*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于混合整数线性规划（MILP）的框架，用于自动化并优化海洋自主航行器（MAVs）的部署调度，在满足电池容量和时间窗口等严格运营约束的前提下，最大化数据收集、最小化部署车辆数量和能耗。该框架的创新之处在于整合了常规船舶航线，支持任务中电池更换或加速航路点间转运。实验表明模型具有高度可扩展性，可在数秒内解决数十艘MAV的路径规划问题，数分钟内处理数百艘规模。此外，该框架还可作为仿真工具评估"假设"场景，并生成可视化以提升可解释性和辅助战略决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋科学界日益依赖MAV收集关键环境数据，但随着运营规模扩大，手动路由和规划大型自主舰队变得指数级复杂且耗时，亟需自动化优化工具来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出一个混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，纳入电池容量、数据收集时间窗口等严格运营约束，以最大化总数据收集量、最小化部署车辆数量和能耗为优化目标。关键创新是整合常规船舶航线，支持MAV进行任务中电池更换或加速转运。

Result: 计算实验表明模型具有高度可扩展性：可在数秒内解决数十艘MAV的路径规划问题，扩展到数百艘规模也仅需数分钟。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅用于运营调度，还可作为稳健的仿真工具评估"假设"场景和分析参数变化对部署策略的影响，并生成增强可解释性的可视化以支持利益相关者的战略决策。

Abstract: The marine science community increasingly relies on Marine Autonomous Vehicles (MAVs) to collect the critical environmental data required to understand global ocean systems. However, as these operations scale, manually routing and planning large autonomous fleets becomes exponentially complex and time-consuming. To address this, we propose a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model designed to automate and optimise MAV deployment schedules. The model accounts for strict operational constraints, including battery capacities and time windows for data collection, while aiming to maximise total data collection and minimise both the number of deployed vehicles and their energy consumption. A key novelty of this framework is integrating conventional ship itineraries, allowing MAVs to support vessels with mid-mission battery swapping or accelerated transit between waypoints. Computational experiments demonstrate that the model is highly scalable, solving routing problems for fleets of dozens of MAVs in seconds, and scaling to hundreds of vehicles in only a few minutes. Beyond operational scheduling, the framework serves as a robust simulation tool for evaluating 'what-if' scenarios and analysing the impact of varying parameters on deployment strategies. Finally, the solution generates a suite of visualisations designed to enhance explainability and support strategic decision-making for stakeholders.

</details>


### [90] [When MILP Beats QP: Piecewise-Linear Reformulations of Sequentially Coupled Bilinear Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27312)
*Quentin Ploussard,Maris Usis,Oluwabunmi Iwakin,Matija Pavičević*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对双线性项的高效混合整数线性规划（MILP）近似公式，利用单位双线性函数的对称性构造连续分段线性（CPWL）逼近，并严格推导了精确的最大/平均逼近误差。通过构造三种MILP公式（Triangle、Square、DC）并在一类新的优化模型（序列耦合双线性规划SCBP）上进行数值实验，证明了紧凑的Square公式在长序列问题上性能优越，使开源MILP求解器能有效超越QP求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双线性项导致的非凸二次规划（QP）在全局最优求解上计算难度极高；尽管CPWL逼近可将非线性转化为MILP，但域划分的几何构造极大地影响求解效率，因此需要设计更高效、充分利用问题结构的MILP公式。

Method: 1) 构建利用单位双线性函数内在对称性的高精度CPWL逼近；2) 解析推导CPWL逼近的精确最大误差与平均误差；3) 构造并对比三种MILP公式：Triangle、Square以及差凸（DC）公式；4) 形式化定义一类新的优化模型SCBP（序列耦合双线性规划）；5) 通过大规模数值实验比较MILP与QP求解器的性能。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的紧凑型Square MILP公式在长序列SCBP实例上具有卓越的计算性能，成熟的开源MILP求解器在超QP求解器方面表现出优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于对称性的Square MILP公式为求解含双线性项的非凸优化问题提供了一种高效、可扩展的替代方案，尤其在长序列耦合场景中优于直接QP求解，具有重要的理论价值与工程实践意义。

Abstract: The presence of bilinear terms in mathematical modeling generally yields nonconvex quadratic programs (QPs) that remain computationally challenging to solve to global optimality. While continuous piecewise linear (CPWL) approximations can reformulate these nonlinearities into mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs), the geometric construction of the domain partition heavily dictates the resulting solver efficiency. In this paper, we present efficient MILP formulations for approximating bilinear terms and rigorously evaluate their computational merits against direct QP solvers. First, we introduce CPWL approximations with arbitrary high degrees of accuracy that explicitly account for and exploit the inherent symmetries of the unit bilinear function. Second, we analytically establish the exact maximum and average approximation errors of the CPWL approximations. Third, we construct and compare three distinct MILP formulations (a ``Triangle'', a ``Square'', and a difference-of-convex ``DC'' formulation) of the CPWL functions. Fourth, we formalize a highly relevant class of optimization models, Sequentially Coupled Bilinear Programs (SCBP), where variables represent system states and state changes. Finally, through extensive numerical experiments, we demonstrate that our compact ``Square'' MILP formulation achieves superior computational performance on SCBP instances with long sequence horizons, allowing mature open-source MILP solvers to effectively outperform QP solvers.

</details>


### [91] [UGM: A Unified Framework and New Perspectives for Accelerated Gradient Methods in Smooth and Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27368)
*Danqing Zhou,Shiqian Ma,Junfeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出统一加速梯度方法框架UGM，涵盖多种加速与传统梯度方法，将其迭代更新解释为重球法与原版梯度下降的混合，并通过Lyapunov函数建立统一收敛分析，据此开发增强型加速梯度算法，实验验证其在二次优化和逻辑回归中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加速梯度方法种类繁多，缺乏统一的框架来理解它们之间的内在联系与权衡机制，难以从理论上系统性地指导新算法的设计与改进。

Method: 提出了统一框架UGM，该框架包含广泛范围的加速与传统梯度类方法；将框架的迭代更新内在解释为重球法与普通梯度下降的混合组合；利用Lyapunov函数建立统一的收敛性分析框架；基于该分析，利用梯度信息与迭代变量之间的内积关系优化迭代更新，开发出一族增强型加速梯度算法。

Result: 在无约束二次优化和逻辑回归任务上的大量数值实验表明，所提出的算法在典型结构条件下相比现有基线方法取得了更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: 经典加速梯度方法本质上是在快速但不稳定的重球动态中整合一个保守的梯度下降步骤，从而实现加速与稳定性之间的有利权衡；基于统一分析框架可系统性地设计更优的增强型加速梯度算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a unified framework for accelerated gradient methods, dubbed UGM, which subsumes a wide range of accelerated and conventional gradient-type methods designed for minimizing $L$-smooth and $μ$-strongly convex functions. We demonstrate that the iteration update of the proposed framework can be intrinsically interpreted as a hybrid combination of the heavy-ball method and vanilla gradient descent. This interpretation reveals that classical accelerated gradient methods essentially integrate a conservative gradient descent step into the fast yet unstable heavy-ball dynamics, which achieves a favorable trade-off between acceleration and stability. We further establish a unified convergence analysis using Lyapunov functions. Guided by our analysis, we develop a family of enhanced accelerated gradient algorithms that leverage the inner product relationship between gradient information and iterative variables to optimize iterative updates. Extensive numerical experiments on unconstrained quadratic optimization and logistic regression validate that the proposed algorithms achieve superior performance compared with existing baseline methods under typical structural conditions.

</details>

### [92] [Coordinate-Deletion Bundles from Composition Algebras: Hopf Defects, $KO$-Classes, and Real Projective Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26300)
*Marina Palaisti*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文通过删除RP^d上每个标准仿射坐标图的某个齐次坐标，将剩余坐标解释为实合成代数（复数、四元数、八元数）的元素，并利用左乘矩阵作为转移数据，显式构造了RP^d上的秩d实向量丛E_D。该丛在每个删除点处具有S^{d-1}→SO(d)的局部clutching映射（即Hopf clutching映射），其截面恰好以坐标点为非退化零点，从而推出w(E_D)=1+x^d，并利用实K理论和Euler类相消得到E_D≅γ^{⊕d。这给出了这些常见向量丛的显式framed-defect实现，特别是RP^8上带九个指定局部Hopf缺陷的半代数八元数实现，并描述了任意域上的Pfister二次丛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望为复、四元、八元Hopf clutching映射对应的向量丛提供显式构造和几何实现，并建立其与标准典型线丛γ的直和之间的同构关系，以及研究其over arbitrary fields的代数/二次型推广。

Method: 利用实合成除代数D（维度d=2,4,8）的左乘矩阵构造显式转移函数；在每个仿射坐标图中删除一个齐次坐标，用左乘矩阵作为gluing data构造秩d向量丛E_D。通过矩阵确定的平凡化分析各删除点的局部clutching映射（恰为Hopf clutching map u↦L_u），并构造有指定非退化零点的截面来研究其拓扑不变量。

Result: 构造的向量丛满足w(E_D)=1+x^d（全部Stiefel-Whitney类），且通过实K理论与Euler类相消证明E_D≅γ^{⊕d}。特别地，得到RP^8上带九个局部Hopf缺陷的γ^{⊕8}的半代数八元数实现，并给出了任意域上关联的Pfister二次丛的描述。

Conclusion: 该构造统一地以复/四元/八元Hopf clutching映射实现了典型向量丛γ^{⊕d}，提供了明确的framed-defect/semialgebraic实现，并建立了与Pfister二次型的联系，为理解这些经典向量丛的代数结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Let \(D\in\{\C,\Hh,\Oct\}\) be a real composition division algebra of dimension \(d\in\{2,4,8\}\). We construct an explicit rank-\(d\) real vector bundle $ E_D\longrightarrow\RP^d$ by deleting one homogeneous coordinate on each standard affine chart, interpreting the remaining coordinates as an element of \(D\), and using the corresponding left-multiplication matrices as transition data. The bundle admits a matrix-determined trivialization away from the \(d+1\) coordinate points. Relative to this trivialization, each deleted point has local clutching map $S^{d-1}\longrightarrow\SO(d),\; u\longmapsto L_u$, which is respectively the complex, quaternionic, or octonionic Hopf clutching map.
  A section arising from the same matrices has exactly the coordinate points as nondegenerate zeros. Consequently, $w(E_D)=1+x^d$, and real \(K\)-theory together with Euler-class cancellation gives $E_D\congγ^{\oplus d}$. Thus the construction supplies explicit framed-defect realizations of these familiar bundles. In particular, it gives a semialgebraic octonionic realization of \(γ^{\oplus8}\) on \(\RP^8\) with nine specified local Hopf defects. We also describe the associated Pfister quadratic bundle over arbitrary fields.

</details>

Abstract: Given an object $A$ in a big tensor-triangulated category, we study the homological and cohomological Bousfield classes of the associated localization: the tensor-triangulated category of $A$-local objects. We show that the homological support classifies the homological Bousfield classes of the $A$-local category precisely when an $A$-relative form of the homological detection property holds. Moreover, we prove that this holds if and only if $A$ is Bousfield equivalent to a coproduct of homological residue fields. The analogous classification of cohomological Bousfield classes by homological cosupport is strictly stronger: it is equivalent to an $A$-relative form of homological stratification. This equivalence between stratification and the classification of cohomological Bousfield classes is new even in the absolute case. A further surprise is that stratification is also equivalent to the classification of homological Bousfield classes together with the statement that every cohomological Bousfield class is homological. Applied to chromatic homotopy theory, these results classify the homological Bousfield classes of any localization of spectra with respect to a coproduct of Morava $K$-theories. This covers many localizations of interest. We also completely characterize when such chromatic localizations are relatively homologically stratified. This yields new examples of cohomological Bousfield classes that are not homological. In particular, it answers a question of Wolcott concerning the category of harmonic spectra. Our examples are produced by exhibiting local spectra with empty homological cosupport.

Result: 论文证明了如下主要结果：（1）抽象GKM图的可定向性与其有理（非等变）图同调代数的Poincaré对偶性等价；（2）作为应用，可定向GKM图在删除任意单个顶点后仍保持连通性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了GKM图可定向性与图同调Poincaré对偶之间的深刻联系，为抽象GKM图的研究提供了一种结合组合与代数的新视角，并展示了如何利用代数性质推导出图的组合结构性质。

Abstract: We investigate a combinatorial notion of orientability for abstract GKM graphs and its connections to graph cohomology in the sense of Guillemin--Zara. In particular, we prove that orientability of the GKM graph is equivalent to Poincaré duality of the rational (non-equivariant) graph cohomology algebra. As an application, we prove that orientable GKM graphs remain connected after removing any single vertex.

</details>


### [95] [Persistence Meets Resistance: Doubling Down on Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27269)
*Benedikt Kolbe,Tim Mayr*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限度量空间过滤的持久同调中稳定不变量的近似计算问题。在n点度量空间（加倍维数增长为o(log n)且直径有界）中，提出了新的近似算法：1参数情形下首次实现了任意稳定条形码加法ε-近似的线性时间算法；多参数情形下（归一化测度双过滤，如multicover和subdivision-Rips双过滤），任意稳定不变量可在与n无关的常数时间内概率近似。此外，通过细粒度复杂性视角，证明了矩阵秩计算可归约到Vietoris–Rips或Čech过滤条形码的近似问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久同调是计算拓扑中的核心工具，但精确计算稳定不变量（如条形码）往往计算代价高昂。对于大规模度量空间数据，需要高效的近似算法。现有方法在速率和精度上存在局限，特别是在多参数过滤情形下缺乏有效的近似策略。本文旨在突破这些限制，通过新颖的算法设计（1参数）和数据驱动的概率方法（多参数），以及通过细粒度复杂性分析揭示问题的计算本质。

Method: 本文采用多种技术手段：（1）1参数情形下，重新审视贪婪排列（greedy permutations）技术，以新方式推导出任意稳定条形码的加法ε-近似的首个线性时间算法；（2）多参数情形下，推导均匀样本的收敛速率和近似质量界，将方法推广到选定的多参数过滤（归一化测度双过滤，包括multicover和subdivision-Rips双过滤），实现常数时间（不依赖于n）的概率近似；（3）通过细粒度复杂性归约，展示矩阵秩计算可归约到Vietoris–Rips或Čech过滤条形码的近似，给出两种归约变体（一种针对足够好的加法近似，另一种针对任意常数因子乘法近似）。算法的运行时间常数依赖于加倍维数、直径和成功概率。

Result: （1）在1参数情形下，首次实现了任意稳定条形码的加法ε-近似的线性时间算法；（2）在多参数情形下，对归一化测度双过滤（multicover和subdivision-Rips），实现了运行时间与n无关（常数时间）的概率近似算法；（3）建立了两类结果，证明计算矩阵秩可以归约到Vietoris–Rips/Čech过滤条形码的近似计算，揭示了该近似问题的细粒度复杂性下界。

Conclusion: 本文在稳定不变量近似计算方面取得了重要进展：1参数情形下实现了线性时间的近似算法，多参数情形下实现了常数时间的概率近似，并通过细粒度复杂性归约揭示了该问题与矩阵秩计算的深层联系。这些结果显著推进了持久同调在高效数据处理中的适用性。

Abstract: We present results on the approximate computation of stable invariants for filtrations of finite metric spaces in the context of persistent homology. We establish novel approximation algorithms in the setting of $n$-point metric spaces where the growth of the doubling dimension is in $o(\log n)$ and the diameter is bounded.
  In the $1$-parameter case, by revisiting known techniques (greedy permutations) in a new way, we derive the first linear-time algorithms for the problem of computing additive $\varepsilon$-approximations of any stable barcode.
  By deriving bounds on the convergence rate and the approximation quality of uniform samples, we extend the approach to selected multiparameter filtrations. We show that for normalized measure bifiltrations, including the multicover and subdivision-Rips bifiltration, any stable invariant can be probabilistically approximated in time constant in $n$. The constants in the running times of our algorithms depend on the doubling dimension, the diameter and the success probability.
  We further study the problem through the lens of fine-grained complexity and show that computing the rank of a matrix reduces to that of approximating the barcode of the Vietoris--Rips or Čech filtration. We present two variants of the reduction, one for sufficiently good additive approximations and the other for any constant factor multiplicative approximations.

</details>

*Max Blans*

Main category: math.AT

Result: 成功证明了一个$(\infty,2)$-范畴版本的operad与cooperad间的Koszul对偶等价，该结果推广并统一了文献中已有的各种operadic Koszul对偶形式。

Conclusion: 该工作为operadic Koszul对偶提供了一个统一的高阶范畴论框架，表明不同形式的operadic Koszul对偶都可以视为这一$(\infty,2)$-范畴等价的特殊情形。

Abstract: We prove an $(\infty,2)$-categorical version of Koszul duality for operads and cooperads in spectra by showing that there is an equivalence between a Morita category of operads and bimodules and a dual Morita category of cooperads and bicomodules. This result subsumes various forms of operadic Koszul duality present in the literature.

</details>

### [97] [Statistics as a local phase: crystalline order and quench dynamics of emergent dimers in Ising gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26264)
*Umberto Borla,Riccardo Cioli,Jad C. Halimeh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了2+1维Z₂晶格规范理论中，微观粒子的玻色/费米统计在强耦合受限形成复合玻色子（二聚体）后如何存留。研究发现底层物质统计性完全编码于单一局域跳跃相位φ（玻色子为0，费米子为π），并通过张量网络方法绘制了以φ为连续插值参数的基态相图，识别出二聚体超流态、电荷密度波态以及一种新颖的二聚体对结晶成有序共振平板的带隙相；最后提出一种淬火协议作为探测二聚体内部统计结构的动力学手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当微观粒子被禁闭束缚形成涌现的玻色子复合体时，其原始的玻色或费米统计信息是否以及如何存留下来，这是一个关于统计性质在复合体形成过程中如何传递的基本物理问题。

Method: 在2+1D Z₂晶格规范理论的强耦合极限下，将受限电荷对描述为有效的二聚体模型；将统计信息编码为单参数φ（0到π插值玻色与费米）；采用张量网络方法精确映射基态相图；并设计淬火协议以动力学方式区分二聚体的内部统计结构。

Result: 统计信息完全由局域跳跃相位φ编码，相互作用与统计无关；φ驱动二聚体超流态与电荷密度波态之间的相变，磁耦合使相邻二聚体结合为共振对，与二聚体间排斥竞争；发现了一种新的带隙相，其中二聚体对结晶为有序共振平板图案；淬火协议下相同二聚体构型因组成粒子统计性不同而表现出显著不同的动力学演化。

Conclusion: 微观粒子的统计性质可以通过复合玻色子中的局域相位有效存留，并可通过动力学淬火协议进行探测，这为实验模拟器（原生实现玻色自由度的系统）提供了一种检测有序相和内部结构的可行方案。

Abstract: How does the Bose or Fermi statistics of microscopic particles survive when confinement binds them into emergent bosonic composites? We address this question in the strong-coupling limit of a $2+1$D $\mathbb{Z}_2$ lattice gauge theory, where charges are confined into tightly bound pairs that can be described by an effective dimer model. We find that the statistics of the underlying matter is encoded entirely in a single local hopping phase $\varphi$ -$0$ for bosons, $π$ for fermions- while interactions remain statistics-independent. Treating $\varphi$ as a continuous parameter that interpolates between the two, we map the ground-state phase diagram with the help of tensor-network methods. The angle $\varphi$ itself drives a transition between a dimer-superfluid and dimer charge density wave state, while the magnetic coupling binds neighboring dimers into resonating pairs, in competition with the inter-dimer repulsion. We identify a novel gapped phase in which dimer pairs crystallize into an ordered pattern of resonating plaquettes. Finally, we propose a quench protocol under which identical dimer configurations evolve in markedly different ways depending on the statistics of their constituents. This provides a dynamical probe of the internal structure of dimers, and detects ordered phases through real time signatures, within reach of simulators that natively realize bosonic degrees of freedom.

</details>

Result: 该方法成功超越了高频极限，能够同时捕捉涌现效应与非绝热现象；验证了在无相互作用和强关联系统（借助tDMRG）中的适用性；给出了共振驱动下带隙内态形成的微观机制，并初步探索了有限温度对应的情形。

Abstract: Periodically driven quantum systems provide a powerful route to engineer novel states of matter by controlling their effective Hamiltonians through external fields. However, most treatments are usually simplified by considering the high-frequency limit, and do not account for the processes taking place in the transient regime during the onset of the drive. In this work, we introduce a time and momentum resolved tunneling spectroscopy protocol to probe the instantaneous energy spectrum of Floquet-driven systems beyond the high-frequency regime, capturing both emergent effects and non-adiabatic phenomena without requiring explicit reconstruction of the full time-dependent Green's function. We benchmark the method on driven non-interacting fermionic models, and then generalize the approach to strongly correlated systems with the aid of time-dependent density matrix renormalization group techniques. We also provide a microscopic description to the emergence of in-gap states under resonant driving, and briefly explore its finite temperature analog.

</details>


### [99] [Dynamical signatures of deconfined spinons in dimerized sawtooth chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26347)
*Nishan Ranabhat,Brandon B. Le,Seung-Hun Lee,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用U(1)对称的DMRG和TDVP方法，研究了锯齿链海森堡模型在二聚化相中的基态性质和动力学响应，确定了其激发谱为去限制的双自旋子连续谱（由kink和antikink畴壁组成），并给出了自旋子色散的闭式表达式来固定连续谱边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解锯齿链海森堡模型在二聚化相中超越精确可解VBS点之外的基态性质和分数化激发谱，为实验上Ti3+ kagome氟化物中由强各向异性交换相互作用产生的锯齿链构建块提供微观理论图景。

Result: 在二聚化相的所有区域中，DSF均为宽带连续谱，由下边缘的亮带主导，且单triplon能量ω≈J_AB处的强度被抑制。谱被识别为两个简并单重态覆盖之间kink和antikink畴壁组成的去限制双自旋子连续谱，闭式自旋子色散可追踪三个区域的亮带，有限分离双kink计算能复现实测强度分布。
