Abstract: We study the ground-state properties and dynamical response of the Heisenberg model on the sawtooth chain in and away from the exactly solvable valence-bond-solid (VBS) point. We employ U(1)-symmetric density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and time-dependent variational principle (TDVP) methods to compute equilibrium diagnostics and the zero-temperature dynamical structure factor (DSF) $S^{zz}(q,ω)$ across the dimerized phase, a valence-bond-ordered state in which the two symmetry-equivalent apex--base bonds of each triangle develop unequal spin correlations, probing the approach to the continuous lower phase boundary, the exact VBS point, and the approach to the first-order upper boundary. In every regime, the DSF is a broad continuum dominated by a bright band at its lower edge, with the intensity at the one-triplon energy $ω\simeq J_{AB}$ suppressed. We identify the spectrum as a deconfined two-spinon continuum of kink and antikink domain walls between the two degenerate singlet coverings. Closed-form spinon dispersions fix the continuum edges and track the dominant band across the zone in all three regimes, while an explicit finite-separation two-kink calculation in a constrained Hilbert space reproduces the measured intensity distribution. Our results provide a microscopic picture of fractionalized excitations in the dimerized sawtooth chain and are relevant to the recently discovered Ti$^{3+}$ kagome fluorides, where strongly anisotropic exchange interactions can generate sawtooth-chain building blocks.

</details>


### [100] [Competition between Geometrical Frustration and the Kondo Effect in CePdAl Revealed by High-Resolution Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26534)
*Yusei Shimizu,Shota Nakamura,Yoichi Ikeda,Yohei Kono,Shunichiro Kittaka,Toshiro Sakakibara,Yosikazu Isikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过高灵敏度磁强计在80 mK下观测到CePdAl中无磁化平台的清晰一级变磁相变，表明Kondo效应被抑制且有序磁矩发生自旋翻转从而解除几何阻挫；4.3 T处的非线性磁化率异常暗示极化顺磁态中存在非偶极关联；高场区域未发现非费米液体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CePdAl是一种具有准kagome结构的重费米子化合物，其几何阻挫与Kondo效应相互竞争。理解这一竞争关系以及磁场诱导的自旋液体态的本质，是该领域的关键科学问题。

Method: 使用高灵敏度磁强计在极低温（80 mK）下对CePdAl进行高精度磁化率测量，观测变磁相变、非线性磁化率异常以及高场区域的输运/磁学行为。

Result: 在80 mK下观察到无磁化平台的一级变磁相变；在4.3 T处发现非线性磁化率异常；高场区域未观察到非费米液体行为的证据。

Conclusion: 这些发现为理解CePdAl中磁场诱导的自旋液体态提供了关键的热力学约束条件，即阻挫的解除通过抑制Kondo效应和有序磁矩自旋翻转实现，且极化顺磁态中存在非偶极关联。

Abstract: CePdAl is a heavy-fermion compound with a quasi-kagome structure, where geometrical frustration competes with the Kondo effect. Using a high-sensitivity magnetometer, we observe clear first-order metamagnetic transitions without magnetization plateaus in CePdAl at 80 mK, indicating a lifting of frustration through the suppression of the Kondo effect and spin flips of ordered moments under fields. An anomaly in the nonlinear magnetic susceptibility at 4.3 T suggests nondipolar correlations in the polarized paramagnetic state. Furthermore, we found no evidence of non-Fermi-liquid behavior in the high-field region, where the antiferromagnetic order is completely suppressed. These findings establish the essential thermodynamic constraints for understanding the field-induced spin-liquid state in CePdAl.

</details>


### [101] [Field-amplified readouts of weak altermagnetic exchange in MnF$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26540)
*Guowen Jiang,Feilong Wang,Yunhua Wang,Fawei Zheng,Bin Xi,Hong-Gang Luo,Jize Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过过定的第一性原理总能映射方法，解决了MnF₂作为原型交错磁体中子格奇交换（altermagnetic exchange）的争议，将其解析为第七近邻不平衡δJ₇≈+8 μeV。在显式包含偶极-偶极相互作用后，该哈密顿量可复现低能隙和有限动量分裂。作者提出利用纵向磁场B∥c作为隐藏尺度的线性放大器，提供两种带符号、场线性可观测读数——补偿场B*(Q)和固定场下伙伴动量的分裂对比——将μeV量级的交错磁交换纳入现有仪器可探测范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MnF₂作为教科书式双子格反铁磁体，最近被重新定位为原型交错磁体，但其定义性的子格奇交换仍存在争议：该交换仅以正交（quadrature）方式进入磁振子分裂，与偶极-偶极相互作用耦合，导致其幅度被抑制、符号被抹除，难以直接观测。

Method: 采用过定的第一性原理总能映射（overdetermined first-principles total-energy mapping）解析出该尺度的来源为第七近邻交换不平衡δJ₇≈+8 μeV。构建含显式偶极-偶极相互作用的哈密顿量，并提出两种利用纵向磁场B∥c作为线性放大器的可观测读数：一是补偿场B*(Q)——最小分裂位置，在旋转相关的伙伴动量处大小相等、符号相反；二是固定场下伙伴动量的分裂对比。

Result: δJ₇≈+8 μeV的第七近邻不平衡可同时复现低能隙和可见的有限动量分裂；补偿场|B*|≈0.34 T，给出符号和尺度；在1 T固定场下伙伴分裂对比约为0.14 meV，是零场超额值的六倍。两种读数在0.12 meV能量分辨率下仍然可靠。

Conclusion: 该构造方法可推广至任意易轴共线交错磁体，使μeV量级的交错磁交换能够被现有实验仪器直接探测，解决了交错磁交换因正交耦合效应导致的尺度抑制与符号模糊问题。

Abstract: MnF$_2$, the textbook two-sublattice antiferromagnet, has reemerged as a prototypical altermagnet, yet the sublattice-odd exchange that defines this identity remains under active debate: it enters the magnon splitting only in quadrature with the dipole--dipole interaction, its magnitude suppressed and its sign erased. An overdetermined first-principles total-energy mapping resolves this scale as a seventh-neighbor imbalance $δ{J_7}\simeq+8~μ$eV. The resulting Hamiltonian, with the dipole--dipole interaction included explicitly, reproduces the low-energy gap and the visible finite-momentum splitting. A longitudinal field $B\parallel c$ then acts as a linear amplifier of the hidden scale, opening two signed, field-linear readouts. The first is the compensation field $B^\ast(\mathbf Q)$, the position of minimum splitting, which is equal and opposite at the rotation-related partner momenta: a shift from zero field is itself evidence of a finite imbalance, its side gives the sign, and its magnitude, $|B^\ast|\simeq0.34$~T here, gives the scale. The second is the fixed-field contrast of the partner splittings, $\simeq0.14$~meV at $1$~T, six times the zero-field excess: a sign check from just two spectra. Both readouts survive a $0.12$~meV energy resolution, and the construction carries over to any easy-axis collinear altermagnet, bringing $μ$eV altermagnetic exchange within present instrumental reach.

</details>


### [102] [Thermally quenched metastability in metal-insulator transitions via elemental substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26609)
*Hiroshi Oike,Yasunori Takahashi,Keisuke Shibuya,Masaki Nakano,Tatsuki Hanada,Motoaki Hirayama,Hiroko Tokoro,Fumitaka Kagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次实验证明了金属-绝缘体相变中可通过快速冷却（>10^9 K/s）实现热淬火亚稳态，在钨掺杂VO2中稳定了长寿命的亚稳态金属相，揭示了成核主导的弛豫动力学和钨取代引入的热激活势垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热淬火能够抑制相变过程中的热力学平衡驰豫，从而揭示亚稳态相（如结构玻璃和淬火合金）。但金属-绝缘体相变由集体电子重组而非原子扩散主导，热淬火亚稳态能否在其实现仍是开放问题。

Method: 对钨取代的VO2进行超过10^9 K/s的极快冷却实验，动力学避免金属-绝缘体相变，并通过温度依赖的弛豫测量分析其动力学特征。

Result: 证明了快速冷却可动力学避免金属-绝缘体相变，稳定长寿命亚稳态金属相；温度依赖弛豫显示出成核主导的动力学，激活势垒源于钨取代。

Conclusion: 确立了元素取代作为实现金属-绝缘体相变热淬火亚稳态的途径，将亚稳态相控制扩展至电子相领域。

Abstract: Thermal quenching inhibits equilibration toward the thermodynamic ground state during phase transitions, revealing metastable phases such as structural glasses and quenched alloys. Whether such thermally quenched metastability can be realized in metal-insulator transitions has remained an open question because these transformations are governed by collective electronic reorganization rather than atomic diffusion. We demonstrate that rapid cooling exceeding 10$^9$ K s$^{-1}$ kinetically avoids the metal-insulator transition, stabilizing a long-lived metastable metallic phase in tungsten-substituted VO$_2$. Temperature-dependent relaxation reveals nucleation-dominated kinetics with a thermal activation barrier introduced by tungsten substitution. Our results establish elemental substitution as a route to thermally quenched metastability in metal-insulator transitions, expanding metastable phase control to electronic phases.

</details>


### [103] [Interlayer-engineering of Charge Order Wave Vector in Kagome Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26751)
*Muntafa M. Mahi,Quazi D. M. Khosru,M. Zahid Hasan,Mahbub Alam,Md Shafayat Hossain*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，研究了kagome金属CsV$_3$Sb$_5$中层间耦合对电荷序波矢选择的影响。在单层极限下，体系主导晶格不稳定性位于q=(1/4,0,0)，对应4×1调制；随着层间耦合增强，M点声子逐渐软化并在c≈12.24 Å时变得不稳定，演化为体相中强2×2不稳定性。层间耦合被识别为连接竞争性4×1与2×2电荷序倾向的控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome金属AV$_3$Sb$_5$中电荷序波矢的本质归属问题尚未解决：哪些是内禀的，哪些由维度和晶格耦合决定。特别是体相中稳健的2×2电荷序与主要由表面探针观测的4×1调制的微观起源及其关系仍不清楚。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，通过调节CsV$_3$Sb$_5$的层间间距（从单层极限到体相极限），系统地研究了层间耦合强度对声子谱和晶格不稳定性的影响，追踪M点与q=(1/4,0,0)处声子模式随层间耦合的变化。

Result: 在单层kagome金属中，M点无声子不稳定性，主导不稳定性位于q=(1/4,0,0)，对应4×1调制；随着层间耦合增加，M点声子逐步软化并在c=12.24 Å附近失稳，最终演化为体相中强2×2不稳定性。这表明4×1是低维内禀倾向，2×2在层间耦合增强后被选中。

Conclusion: 层间耦合是固定化学计量比下控制电荷序波矢选择的关键参数，它统一解释了kagome金属中多种电荷序波矢共存与竞争的机制，为理解4×1与2×2电荷序的关联提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Charge orders in the kagome metals AV$_3$Sb$_5$ sit at the center of a rich phase diagram that also includes superconductivity, nematicity, and signatures of time-reversal-symmetry breaking. Yet even the basic question of which charge ordering wave vectors are intrinsic, and which are selected by dimensionality and lattice coupling, remains unsettled. Importantly, the microscopic origin of different charge orders and, in particular, the relationship between the robust bulk $2 \times 2$ charge order and the controversial $4 \times 1$ modulation, which is primarily resolved by surface probes, remains unresolved. Here, we use first-principles calculations to study the role of interlayer coupling in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ by tuning the interlayer separation from the monolayer limit to the bulk limit. In the monolayer AV$_3$Sb$_5$ (A = Rb, Cs), the phonon spectrum exhibits no instability at the M point; instead, the dominant lattice instability occurs at q = (1/4, 0, 0), consistent with a $4 \times 1$ modulation. As interlayer coupling increases in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$, an M-point phonon progressively softens and becomes unstable already near c = 12.24 Å, evolving into the strong $2 \times 2$ instability characteristic of the bulk. These results identify interlayer coupling as a control parameter at a fixed stoichiometry that links competing $4 \times 1$ and $2 \times 2$ tendencies, providing a unified framework for understanding why multiple charge-order wave vectors coexist and compete in kagome metals.

</details>


### [104] [3D- (H-theta-phi) magnetic phase diagram of antiferromagnetic metal GdB6 with electron and lattice instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27134)
*A. N. Azarevich,A. V. Bogach,T. F. Garipova,V. V. Voronov,M. Rajnak,S. Gabani,K. Flachbart,N. B. Bolotina,O. N. Khrykina,V. M. Gridchina,A. Yu. Tsvetkov,S. Yu. Gavrilkin,N. E. Sluchanko*

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有立方晶格和Gd S型磁性离子的反铁磁金属GdB6中电荷输运和磁化各向异性的起源。通过低温X射线衍射发现静态Jahn-Teller畸变和动态电荷条纹等纳米级电子不稳定性，构建了详细的H-T相图（包含AF(I)和AF(II)两个主相），并在氦温度下于(110)和(111)面发现叶轮型H-φ磁相图。研究提出自旋密度波5d分量在AF(II)磁结构中起重要作用，动态电荷条纹抑制沿<100>和<110>方向的RKKY间接交换，产生各向异性的电荷散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解GdB6中电荷输运和磁化各向异性的起源，特别是厘清具有立方对称性和S型磁性离子体系中反常各向异性的微观机制，以及动态电荷条纹与磁性交换之间的关联。

Method: 采用精密低温X射线衍射测量检测晶格畸变和电子不稳定性；通过磁电阻和磁化的角度φ依赖测量，在(110)和(111)晶面构建H-φ磁相图；结合H-T相图分析，评估自旋密度波5d分量的贡献。

Result: 发现小的静态Jahn-Teller畸变和动态电荷条纹共存；构建了含AF(I)和AF(II)两主相的H-T相图；在氦温度下观察到叶轮型H-φ磁相图，由径向和圆形边界分隔两个AF相；结果支持AF(II)态中自旋密度波5d分量的重要作用。

Conclusion: 动态电荷条纹通过抑制沿<100>和<110>方向第一、第二近邻Gd3+离子间的RKKY交换，连同振动耦合的Gd-Gd对，共同导致了GdB6中异常的各向异性电荷散射和叶轮型相图，揭示了S型磁性离子体系中自旋-晶格-电荷耦合的复杂机制。

Abstract: The origin of charge transport and magnetization anisotropy was studied in GdB6, an antiferromagnetic (AF) metal (Néel temperature TN ~ 15.5 K) with cubic lattice and Gd S-type magnetic ions. Both small static Jahn-Teller distortions and nanoscale electronic instabilities (dynamic charge stripes) were found in precise low temperature X-ray diffraction measurements. The detailed magnetic field (H) vs temperature (T) phase diagrams were constructed with two main magnetic phases AF(I) and AF(II). Using the angular phi-dependences of magnetoresistance and magnetization, impeller-type patterns of the H-phi magnetic phase diagrams in the (110) and (111) planes were found at helium temperatures, which included the AF phases I and II separated from each other by radial and circular boundaries. The results argue in favor of the important role of the spin density wave 5d- component in the magnetic structure of AF(II) state. Charge fluctuations in stripes are proposed to be responsible for the suppression of the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yoshida (RKKY) indirect exchange between the first and second neighboring Gd3+ ions located along the <100> and <110> directions. These dynamic charge stripes and vibrationally coupled Gd-Gd pairs produce unusual anisotropy of charge scattering and the impeller-type diagrams in GdB6 with S-type magnetic ion.

</details>


### [105] [Multi-orbital physics in inverse Lieb lattice altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27200)
*Mercè Roig,Jannik Gondolf,Andreas Kreisel,Brian M. Andersen,Daniel F. Agterberg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于对称性的微观哈密顿模型，超越了仅含亚晶格自由度的最小模型，将亚晶格与轨道自由度均纳入考量，用于研究逆Lieb晶格d波交替磁体（特别是钒氧硫族化物家族）中的多轨道电子关联物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆Lieb晶格作为交替磁性的有前景平台，已有多种该结构的材料被提出为d波交替磁候选。然而，先前广泛使用的亚晶格-only最小模型不足以完整描述此类交替磁体的多轨道特性，需要发展包含轨道自由度的微观模型来更真实地刻画这些材料。

Method: 论文从对称性出发构建微观哈密顿量，同时纳入亚晶格和轨道自由度，超越仅含亚晶格的最小模型。将该模型应用于钒氧硫族化物家族交替磁体，该材料在交替磁状态下费米面以xy和xz/yz轨道特征为主导。通过该多轨道模型研究电子关联物理，包括Hund耦合对轨道间磁序的诱导作用以及拓扑边缘态的轨道选择性。

Result: 研究表明xy轨道对稳定单个V₂O层内观察到的交替磁态至关重要；xz/yz轨道中的交替磁序通过Hund耦合被诱导产生。此外，多轨道模型揭示了拓扑区域，其中拓扑边缘态自然呈现轨道选择性。

Conclusion: 多轨道微观模型比亚晶格-only最小模型更能完整描述逆Lieb晶格交替磁体的物理，xy轨道在稳定磁序中起关键作用，Hund耦合在多轨道间传递磁有序，且系统展现出轨道选择的拓扑边缘态，为理解此类交替磁体的电子结构提供了更全面的框架。

Abstract: The inverse Lieb lattice has recently emerged as a promising platform for altermagnetism, with several materials with this structure proposed as $d$-wave altermagnetic candidates. Here, we develop a symmetry-based microscopic Hamiltonian for these materials that includes both sublattice and orbital degrees of freedom, going beyond the sublattice-only minimal models that have been extensively used to study such altermagnets. We apply these models to examine multi-orbital electron correlation physics in the vanadium oxychalcogenide family altermagnets, which contain dominant $xy$ and $xz/yz$ orbitals character at the Fermi level in the altermagnetic state. We demonstrate that $xy$ orbitals are crucial to stabilize the altermagnetic state observed within a single V$_2$O layer, and altermagnetic order in the $xz/yz$ orbitals is induced through Hund's coupling. Additionally, we show that these multi-orbital models reveal topological regimes in which topological edge states are naturally orbital selective.

</details>


### [106] [A continuous confinement-deconfinement transition in a triangular quantum magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27411)
*Suguru Hosoi,Sejun Park,Michihiro Hirata,Minseong Lee,Adam P Dioguardi,Joe D Thompson,Filip Ronning,Allen O Scheie,Kumpei Imamura,Kenichiro Hashimoto,Takasada Shibauchi,Bishnu P Belbase,Arjun Unnikrishnan,Johannes Knolle,Arnab Banerjee,Yuji Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在三角晶格反铁磁体TlYbS₂中观察到了从玻色型磁振子（磁性有序相）到费米型自旋子（无能隙量子自旋液体）的连续禁闭-退禁闭转变，表现为热导率残余线性项、泡利型磁化率和温度无关的NMR Knight位移等证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种在不同激发类型（玻色型磁振子 vs 费米型自旋子）之间发生连续转变的量子磁性体系，该转变在概念上类似于量子色动力学中的禁闭-退禁闭转变，是长期未被发现的物理现象。

Method: 对三角晶格反铁磁体TlYbS₂施加c轴方向磁场，结合热导率、磁化率（Pauli-like susceptibility）和核磁共振（NMR）Knight位移等多种测量手段，系统表征从磁有序态到量子自旋液体态的转变行为，并分析临界磁场附近的散射率和态密度行为。

Result: 发现TlYbS₂在TN≈0.53 K下进入反铁磁有序态；c轴磁场在μ₀Hc≈3 T处抑制该有序，驱动体系进入具有自旋子费米面的无能隙量子自旋液体。接近Hc时，巡游激发的散射率显著增强但态密度无临界增强，这与传统磁量子临界行为不同。上述结果支持存在连续禁闭-退禁闭转变。

Conclusion: TlYbS₂中的观测证据表明存在一种由分数化激发调控的连续禁闭-退禁闭转变，超越Ginzburg-Landau范式，为理解量子磁性中的分数化准粒子转变提供了新的实验平台。

Abstract: A continuous transition between phases hosting distinct excitations---bosonic magnons versus fermionic spinons---is a long-sought phenomenon in quantum magnetism, analogous to the confinement--deconfinement transition in quantum chromodynamics. We report evidence for such a transition in the triangular-lattice antiferromagnet TlYbS$_2$. Antiferromagnetic order develops below $T_\mathrm{N} \approx 0.53\,\mathrm{K}$. A $c$-axis field suppresses this order, driving the system into a gapless quantum spin liquid with a spinon Fermi surface above $μ_0 H_\mathrm{c} \approx 3\,\mathrm{T}$, evidenced by a finite residual linear term in thermal conductivity, a Pauli-like susceptibility, and a temperature-independent NMR Knight shift. Approaching $H_\mathrm{c}$ from above, the scattering rate of itinerant excitations is strongly enhanced while their density of states shows no critical enhancement, atypical of conventional magnetic quantum criticality. These results point to a continuous confinement--deconfinement transition governed by fractionalized excitations beyond the Ginzburg--Landau paradigm.

</details>


### [107] [Exact Stiffness and Dynamical Responses from Fock-Space Fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27446)
*Jonah Herzog-Arbeitman,Eslam Khalaf,Zhaoyu Han*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明量子几何嵌套（QGN）模型具有一种基于Fock空间碎片化的强可解性：基态上的激发被解耦为具有固定粒子-空穴算子数量的Krylov子空间，从而保持动力学不变性。利用这一结构，作者证明了QGN模型中自发破缺连续对称性的刚性精确等于其在Gauss流形上的变分值，证实了量子多体bootstrap中的猜想，并推广到更多可精确计算的物理响应函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确可解的量子多体模型极为罕见，即使谱具有代数结构，动力学响应仍然难以获得，因为它涉及大量激发态。作者旨在寻找一类具有强可解性的模型，并建立精确的动力学响应计算方法。

Method: 利用QGN模型中Fock空间碎片化结构：精确的无挫折（frustration-free）基态上的激发被解耦为具有固定粒子-空穴算子数量的Krylov子空间，保持动力学不变性。将该结构与无穷小相位扭转耦合基态的行为相结合，证明基态仅与单粒子-单空穴碎片耦合，该碎片恰好对应于变分流形内基态的切空间，从而使变分曲率精确成立。

Result: 证明了QGN模型中自发破缺连续对称性的刚性精确等于Gauss流形上的变分值，验证了量子多体bootstrap猜想。此外，作用在固定Fock碎片内的微扰，其响应函数可精确由对应的少体扇区决定，从而实现对静态磁化率、光学电导率、动力学结构因子和单粒子Green函数等物理量的精确计算。

Conclusion: QGN模型展现出一种异常强的可解性形式，源于Fock空间碎片化。这使得在一般多体系统中难以处理的动力学响应在此类模型中变得精确可计算，为精确多体物理提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Exactly solvable quantum many-body models are rare, and even when their spectra are algebraically organized, dynamical responses generally remain difficult to obtain because they probe an extensive number of excited states. Here we show that quantum geometric nesting (QGN) models admit an unusually strong form of solvability rooted in \emph{Fock-space fragmentation}: excitations on top of the exact frustration-free ground states decouple into Krylov subspaces with a fixed number of particle and hole operators, and hence remain dynamically invariant. Exploiting this structure, we prove that the stiffness of the spontaneously broken continuous symmetry in QGN models is exactly equal to its variational value in the Gaussian manifold, confirming a conjecture from quantum many-body bootstrap~\cite{GaoHanKhalaf2026}. The proof shows that an infinitesimal phase twist couples the ground state only to the one-particle, one-hole fragment, which coincides with the tangent space of the ground state within the variational manifold, thereby making the variational curvature exact. More generally, perturbations whose action remains within a fixed Fock-space fragment have response functions determined exactly by the corresponding few-body sector, enabling exact access to quantities including static susceptibility, optical conductivity, dynamical structure factors, and single-particle Green's functions.

</details>


### [108] [Metallogenic quantum criticality: Fermi surface nucleation at transitions between gapped phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27453)
*Zhengyan Darius Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发现了一类全新的量子相变——"成金属量子临界点"(MGQCP)，其中两个全gap绝缘体之间的量子临界点竟然涌现出整个无gap的费米面，彻底颠覆了传统观念中量子临界点只有孤立动量点处有gapless激发的认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统组织原则认为，gapped相之间的量子相变由尺度不变的量子场论描述，gapless自由度仅存在于动量空间中的孤立点。作者试图检验这一原则是否可以被根本性地推翻——即量子临界点能否涌现出整个扩展的gapless费米面。

Method: 作者构建了一族受控的有效场论来描述不同分数量子霍尔绝缘体之间的MGQCP，并提出了周期性调制的量子霍尔双层层结构作为最小微观实现。该临界理论具有独特的混合物理性质：输运和压缩率遵循与共形场论一致的标度律，但电子谱函数在费米动量处呈现幂律非解析性，类似于非费米液体金属。

Result: 成功构造了MGQCP的有效场论框架，证明了一个完整的gapless费米面可以在分离两个全gap绝缘体的量子临界点上涌现。该临界理论同时表现出共形场论式的标度行为和金属式的费米面特征，并讨论了在具有可调Chern带的moire材料中的应用前景。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一类更广泛的量子临界现象：尽管相邻两相中都不存在gapless激发，gapless激发的扩展流形仍可在动量空间中涌现。这为量子相变的研究开辟了新的方向，并为moire材料中的临界现象提供了新视角。

Abstract: Continuous quantum phase transitions in interacting many-body systems exhibit universal phenomena that are largely independent of microscopic details. An organizing principle distilled from canonical examples such as the magnetic transition in Ising models is that the low-energy physics of transitions between gapped phases is governed by scale-invariant quantum field theories with gapless degrees of freedom at isolated points in momentum space. In this paper, we show that this principle can fail in a radical way: an entire gapless Fermi surface can emerge at a quantum critical point separating two fully gapped insulators. These exotic phase transitions are dubbed \textit{metallogenic quantum critical points} (MGQCP). We construct a controlled family of effective field theories for MGQCPs between distinct fractional Chern insulators and propose a periodically modulated quantum Hall bilayer as a minimal microscopic setting. The critical theory exhibits an unusual mixture of physical signatures: while transport and compressibility follow scaling laws compatible with a conformal field theory, the electron spectral function develops a power-law non-analyticity at the Fermi momentum, characteristic of non-Fermi-liquid metals. We discuss how the concept of MGQCP may shed new light on critical phenomena in moire materials with tunable Chern bands. More generally, our results point to a broader class of quantum critical phenomena in which extended manifolds of gapless excitations emerge in momentum space despite being absent in both neighboring phases.

</details>

### [109] [Quadratic Complexity of Voronoi Diagrams in $\mathbb{R}^3$ for Lines in a Single Ruling of a Regulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27114)
*Eunku Park*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维欧氏空间中位于光滑双直纹实二次曲面同一族直纹上的直线集合的最近和最远Voronoi图。证明了该特殊类下两个图的组合复杂度均为O(n²)，并通过构造性例子证明最近图的复杂度达到Θ(n²)的下界。利用Plücker嵌入将问题归结为二次曲线的相关代数几何问题，并给出了精确的O(n²)时间算法来枚举所有有限顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意直线站点下最近Voronoi图的组合复杂度仅知介于Ω(n²)和O(n^{3+ε})之间，存在巨大间隙。为了提高对直线Voronoi图复杂度的理解，研究者聚焦于一类特殊的直线配置——同一双直纹实二次曲面的同一族直纹中的直线，希望在此自然几何约束下获得精确的复杂度界限和有效算法。

Method: 通过Plücker嵌入将直线族表示为二次曲线（圆锥），将直线与欧氏球面相切的条件限制为二元四次方程。证明在一般位置条件下，规则顶点（四条支撑直线、三个界定二等分面横截相交）对应四个支撑参数恰好是该四次方程的根；符号交替性质迫使参数圆的两段弧不含站点，从而将可能的循环支撑类型限制为n(n-3)/2种，每种类型用Bézout定理将圆心数上界限定为8。该约化同时给出按参数循环排序后以常数度实单变量表示枚举所有顶点的精确O(n²)算法。

Result: 证明了直纹类中最近和最远Voronoi图至多有4n(n-3)个顶点，总组合复杂度为O(n²)。对每个n≥4，在固定的非旋转单叶双曲面的一个直纹上存在一般位置的n条直线，其最近Voronoi图至少有(n-2)(n-3)/2个不同的规则最近顶点，从而证明该类的最近图最坏情况复杂度为Θ(n²)；最远图对每个一般位置输入都有恰好n(n-1)个三维胞腔，复杂度恒为Θ(n²)。

Conclusion: 本研究在直线Voronoi图复杂度研究上取得了精确结果：在同一二次曲面直纹限制下，最近和最远Voronoi图的最坏情况复杂度均为Θ(n²)，填补了此前Ω(n²)到O(n^{3+ε})之间的间隙。算法上，常数度实单变量表示保证了顶点的精确枚举，实现了O(n²)时间的实用算法。

Abstract: We study nearest and farthest Voronoi diagrams of lines in $\mathbb{R}^3$ under the Euclidean metric when all $n$ lines belong to one ruling of a smooth doubly ruled real quadric. For arbitrary line sites, the combinatorial complexity of the nearest Voronoi diagram is known only to lie between $Ω(n^2)$ and $O(n^{3+\varepsilon})$. Under general-position assumptions, we prove that both diagrams in the ruling class have at most $4n(n-3)$ vertices and $O(n^2)$ total combinatorial complexity. Conversely, for every $n \ge 4$, one ruling of a fixed non-rotational one-sheeted hyperboloid contains a general-position set of $n$ lines with at least $(n-2)(n-3)/2$ distinct regular nearest vertices, where regular means that exactly four lines support the vertex and their three defining bisectors meet transversely. Thus the worst-case complexity of the nearest Voronoi diagram in this class is $Θ(n^2)$, while the farthest diagram has $Θ(n^2)$ complexity for every general-position input, since it has exactly $n(n-1)$ three-dimensional cells. Under the Plücker embedding, the ruling is a conic, and the condition for a line to be tangent to a Euclidean sphere restricts to a binary quartic. At a regular vertex, the four supporting parameters exhaust its roots, and sign alternation forces two arcs of the parameter circle to be site-free. This leaves only $n(n-3)/2$ possible cyclic support types, while Bézout's theorem bounds the number of centers for each type by eight. The same reduction yields an exact $O(n^2)$-time algorithm that, after cyclically sorting the site parameters, enumerates all finite nearest and farthest vertices as constant-degree real univariate representations.

</details>


### [110] [Lunar Generalizations of the Euclidean Minimum Spanning Tree in the Plane and their Expected Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27118)
*Ondřej Draganov,Herbert Edelsbrunner,Sophie Rosenmeier,Morteza Saghafian*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文受彩色持续同调的启发，将经典的欧几里得最小生成树（EMST）推广到月球最小生成树（lunar EMST），用于处理带有s+1种颜色的n个二维平面点。当点在[0,1]^2上均匀随机分布且颜色随机分配时，期望代价收敛到某个依赖于s的常数乘以√n。本文的主要贡献是证明了该常数的存在性，但和经典EMST类似，其精确值仍然难以确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期提出的彩色持续同调（chromatic persistent homology）理论需要一种新的几何结构来刻画不同颜色点之间的空间关系。经典EMST仅处理单一颜色的点集，无法捕捉多颜色情形下的拓扑与几何信息，因此需要将其推广到多颜色场景。

Method: 将s+1种颜色的点推广到lunar EMST结构：以不同颜色的点为中心、半径为r的s+1个圆盘的交集称为lune；lunar EMST反映了r从0到∞过程中lune并集的历史演化，其代价定义为树中弧和节点形成时半径差的二倍。通过概率分析论证了随机均匀分布和随机着色下期望代价的渐近性质。

Result: 证明了当n趋于无穷大时，lunar EMST的期望代价收敛到某个依赖于颜色数s的常数乘以√n，但该常数的精确值无法显式计算，与经典EMST的情况相似。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入lunar EMST这一新结构架起了彩色持续同调与经典几何图论之间的桥梁，证明了其期望代价的渐近常数存在，然而常数的精确值仍然是一个开放的难题。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent introduction of chromatic persistent homology, we generalize the Euclidean minimum spanning tree (EMST) for $n$ points in $\mathbb{R}^2$ to the lunar EMST for the case in which the points come in $s+1$ colors. Calling the intersection of $s+1$ disks of radius $r$ centered at points with pairwise different colors a \emph{lune}, the generalized EMST reflects the history of the union of lunes as $r$ goes from $0$ to $\infty$, and its \emph{cost} is twice the difference between the radii when the arcs and nodes of the tree are formed. If the points are chosen uniformly at random in $[0,1]^2$ and colored randomly, the expected cost converges to some constant (that depends on $s$) times $\sqrt{n}$, as $n$ goes to infinity. The main contribution of this paper is a proof that this constant exists, however similar to the case of the classic EMST, its precise value remains elusive.

</details>

### [111] [Efficient binned profile likelihood minimization for precision measurements with RABBIT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26376)
*David Walter,Josh Bendavid,Kenneth Long*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍Rabbit (Rapid Automatic Bin-Based Inference Tool)，一个基于TensorFlow 2的Python框架，利用可微分编程和自动微分技术，通过Krylov子空间中的trust-region最小化器高效处理LHC实验中海量bins和nuisance parameters的binned profile最大似然拟合，支持CPU和GPU加速，并通过JIT编译达到接近C++的执行速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC上的精确测量越来越依赖于包含数千个bins和nuisance parameters的分箱profile最大似然拟合，HL-LHC将进一步推高这些数值。快速且稳健地最小化此类似然函数对于及时的分析开发和准确的推断至关重要，而现有工具在挑战性场景下可能无法在合理时间内收敛。

Method: Rabbit框架利用TensorFlow 2中的可微分编程来实现：(1) 自动微分提供精确的梯度和Hessian-vector乘积，供Krylov子空间中的trust-region最小化器使用；(2) 即时编译(JIT)实现接近C++的执行速度；(3) 灵活统计模型支持解析处理；(4) 对称化选项建立高斯近似；(5) 线性化似然公式提供确定性解；(6) 通过模型的可微变换测量物理观测量，包括unfolded微分截面。

Result: 在合成模型上的基准测试表明，Rabbit在bins和参数数量方面的扩展性优异，在挑战性场景下优于既有工具，因为后者在合理时间内无法收敛。

Conclusion: Rabbit为LHC高精度测量提供了一种快速、稳健且可扩展的最大似然拟合工具，通过可微分编程技术有效应对HL-LHC时代大数据量拟合的挑战。

Abstract: Precision measurements at the LHC increasingly rely on binned profile maximum likelihood fits with thousands of bins and nuisance parameters, and the High-Luminosity LHC will push these numbers further. Fast and robust minimization of such likelihoods is crucial for timely analysis development and accurate inference. We present Rabbit (Rapid Automatic Bin-Based Inference Tool), a Python framework that exploits differentiable programming in TensorFlow 2 to perform this task on CPUs and GPUs. Automatic differentiation provides exact gradients and Hessian-vector products for a trust-region minimizer operating in Krylov subspaces, and just-intime compilation yields near-C++ execution speed. Rabbit implements flexible statistical models with analytic treatments where possible, supports symmetrization options that establish Gaussian approximations and a linearized likelihood formulation with deterministic solutions, and focuses on measuring physical observables through differentiable transformations of the model, including unfolded differential cross sections. Benchmarks on synthetic models demonstrate excellent scaling with the number of bins and parameters, outperforming established tools in challenging regimes where these fail to converge within reasonable time.

</details>


### [112] [Search for high-mass resonances in photon-jet final states using 140 fb$^{-1}$ of $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26935)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用ATLAS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞下采集的140 fb⁻¹数据，搜索光子+喷流（γ-jet）末态中的高质量新物理现象。未发现超出标准模型预期的显著偏差，因而对激发夸克、量子黑洞和高斯型共振产生设定了严格限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找超出标准模型的新物理现象，特别是通过高横向动量光子与喷流的γ-jet不变质量谱来探测高能标下的新共振态，包括夸克复合性（激发夸克）、量子黑洞以及其他可能的超出标准模型的新物理信号。

Method: 利用ATLAS探测器在LHC上以质心能量13 TeV收集的质子-质子对撞数据（总积分亮度140 fb⁻¹），测量γ-jet不变质量谱，并将其与标准模型背景预期进行比较，搜索局部超出（localised excess）。针对不同耦合强度f和不同味道（轻夸克u*/d*及重夸克b*/c*）的激发夸克模型，量子黑洞（Randall-Sundrum和Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali模型），以及通用高斯型共振产生进行了系统搜索。

Result: 未观察到超出标准模型预测的显著偏差。在f=1条件下，排除了激发夸克质量上限达6.0 TeV（轻夸克qL*）、3.5 TeV（c*）和2.6 TeV（b*）。量子黑洞质量阈值在Randall-Sundrum和Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali模型中分别被排除至5.3 TeV和7.5 TeV（95%置信水平）。这是LHC上首次对γ-jet末态中c*产生提供直接约束。此外还利用高斯型共振获得了可见截面的约束。

Conclusion: γ-jet不变质量谱对多种新物理模型提供了强有力的排除界限。激发夸克模型中c*首次得到直接约束，量子黑洞和通用高斯共振模型也获得了显著的限制，进一步压缩了这些理论框架的参数空间。

Abstract: A search for new high-mass phenomena in events with a high-transverse-momentum photon and a jet is performed using data collected by the ATLAS experiment from proton-proton collisions at the LHC, with a centre-of-mass energy of 13 TeV. The analysis is based on a total integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. The $γ$$-$jet invariant mass spectrum measured in data is examined for localised excesses above the Standard Model background. No significant deviation from the Standard Model prediction is observed, allowing stringent limits to be set on the production cross sections of specific new physics scenarios. To investigate quark compositeness, excited quark models are studied for different couplings ($f$) and flavours: light $q_{\mathrm{L}}^{*}$ $(u^{*}/d^{*})$ and heavy ($b^{*}$, $c^{*}$). For $f=1$, masses up to 6.0 TeV ($q_{\mathrm{L}}^{*}$), 3.5 TeV ($c^{*}$), and 2.6 TeV ($b^{*}$) are excluded. These results provide the first direct constraints on $c^{*}$ production in the $γ$$-$ jet final state at the LHC. Additionally, the analysis excludes quantum black hole mass thresholds up to 5.3 (7.5) TeV in the Randall-Sundrum (Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali) models at a 95% confidence level, further constraining these theoretical frameworks. Constraints on the visible cross-section are also obtained using generic Gaussian-shaped resonance production.

</details>


### [113] [Accelerating Optical Photon Simulation in DUNE with Opticks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27306)
*Ilker Parmaksiz,Aaron Higuera,Laura Paulucci,Viktor Pec,Estanislao Forino*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次在10千吨尺度上实现了基于GPU的光学光子模拟，利用Opticks加速DUNE远探测器水平漂移（FD-HD）几何中的光子传输，相比单线程和多线程GEANT4分别获得313倍和83倍的加速，同时保持完全相同的物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DUNE等大型探测器几何中，光学光子模拟是计算量最大的任务之一。在10千吨尺度的远探测器模块中，基于GEANT4的逐光子传输在CPU上计算成本过高，严重制约了高统计量光学研究和机器学习标注数据集的生成。

Method: 基于Opticks框架，将DUNE FD-HD几何中的光学光子传输实现为GPU加速版本。在完整FD-HD几何上运行，并与参考GEANT4模拟进行多维度验证；同时将Opticks集成到基于LArSoft的DUNE软件栈中。

Result: 在完整FD-HD几何上，Opticks以313±3倍于单线程GEANT4、83±1倍于四线程GEANT4的速度传播相同的光子，且在所有考虑的指标上均通过了相对于GEANT4参考模拟的验证。GPU模拟保持了完整的蒙特卡洛保真度，使千吨尺度的高统计量光学研究和机器学习标注数据集生成成为可能。

Conclusion: GPU加速的光学光子模拟能够在保持与GEANT4等效物理精度的同时，将计算成本降低到足以支撑千吨级探测器高统计量光学研究和机器学习数据集生成的水平，为DUNE等大型探测器实验提供了可行的计算解决方案。

Abstract: Optical photon simulation is among the most computationally demanding tasks in complex and large detector geometries. In the Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE), the scale of the far-detector modules makes photon-by-photon transport with \texttt{GEANT4} prohibitively expensive on CPUs. We present the first implementation and performance evaluation of GPU-accelerated optical photon simulation at the 10~kt scale, based on \texttt{Opticks} and applied to the DUNE far-detector horizontal-drift (FD-HD) geometry. On the full FD-HD geometry, \texttt{Opticks} propagates the same photons as \texttt{GEANT4} with a speedup of $313 \pm 3$ over single-threaded and $83 \pm 1$ over four-thread \texttt{GEANT4}, and is validated against the reference \texttt{GEANT4} simulation across all metrics considered. We further integrate \texttt{Opticks} into the \texttt{LArSoft}-based DUNE software stack. Photons simulated on the GPU with \texttt{GEANT4}-equivalent physics retain full Monte Carlo fidelity at a computational cost that makes high-statistics optical studies and the generation of labeled datasets for machine learning feasible at the kiloton scale.

</details>

### [114] [WALL-SS: Scaling Long-horizon World Models via Next-Scale Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26239)
*Maeve Zhang,Rain Sun,Xiang Wang,Cyril Zhang,Shalfun Li,Meng Cao,Howard Lu,Ethan Chen,Harry Jhou,KZ Zheng,Lights Shi,Regis Cheng,Lorenzin,Robert Wang,Victor Yao,Gody Li,Elise Mon,Yohann Tang,Ryan Yu,PS Zhang,Vincent Chen,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WALL-SS，一种基于尺度级自回归缩放的生成式世界模型，通过将具身轨迹建模为观测与动作交织的因果序列，实现动作可控、长时程的机器人视觉仿真，支持变长生成、流式扩展和在线策略对齐优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型为机器人提供预测性环境模型，但现有方法多局限于片段级未来预测，缺乏统一的生成式框架来关联动作与后果、支持灵活时域和持续交互，并难以实现奖励驱动的优化。

Method: WALL-SS将具身轨迹表示为观测与动作交替的因果序列，采用从粗到细的尺度级自回归方式生成每一帧未来观测，并包含三个互补组件：(1) 动作条件化的下一尺度预测，注入尺度对齐的动作表示以增强动作-未来耦合；(2) 尺度压缩的长时程记忆，细粒度保留近期交互并压缩远期观测与动作，配合尺度级梦推理增强自生成上下文的鲁棒性；(3) 在线策略对齐，通过动作跟随和长时一致性的奖励优化自回归视觉动力学，同时保持预训练视觉分布。

Result: 实验表明WALL-SS在动作跟随和轨迹精度上优于基线，支持在有界内存下进行连贯的分钟级流式生成长度，且在线策略对齐能持续减少动作漂移和长时程不一致性。

Conclusion: WALL-SS通过统一的因果序列生成框架和尺度级自回归设计，在长时程机器人仿真中实现了更好的动作可控性、流式扩展能力和策略优化支持，为生成式世界模型在机器人学习和规划中的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Generative world models provide robots with predictive models of how the world evolves under interaction, with growing potential for simulation, planning, policy evaluation, and robot learning. Beyond clip-level future prediction, a unified generative formulation should relate actions to consequences, support flexible horizons and continuous interaction, and enable reward-driven optimization. We introduce WALL-SS, a world model that generates visual futures through Scale-wise autoregressive Scaling, enabling action-controllable and long-horizon robotic simulation. WALL-SS represents embodied trajectories as causal sequences of temporally interleaved observations and actions, making action-dependent state transitions explicit while naturally supporting variable-length generation, streaming extension through reusable causal states, and direct optimization through sequence probabilities. To make this formulation effective over long horizons, we generate each future observation in a coarse-to-fine manner and develop three complementary components within the same hierarchy. Action-conditioned next-scale prediction injects scale-aligned action representations to improve action-future coupling and model both successful and failed behaviors. Scale-compressed long-horizon memory retains recent interactions at fine resolution while compressing distant observations and actions, with scale-wise dream forcing enhancing robustness to self-generated context. Finally, on-policy alignment optimizes autoregressive visual dynamics with action-following and long-term consistency rewards while preserving the pretrained visual distribution. Experiments show that WALL-SS improves action following and trajectory accuracy, supports coherent minute-long streaming rollout under bounded memory, and consistently benefits from on-policy alignment in reducing action drift and long-horizon inconsistency.

</details>


### [115] [Constraint-Aware Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Static Shape Estimation of Co-Manipulative Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26273)
*Rana Danesh,Pari Qarehdaghi,Farrokh Janabi-Sharifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于约束感知物理信息神经网络（PINN）的方法，用于肌腱驱动连续体机器人（CCR）在协同操作柔性物体时的静态形状估计。该方法将投影静态平衡残差和结构级几何残差融入网络训练，在有限且含噪的训练数据下显著优于纯数据驱动的人工神经网络（ANN），并实现了比迭代非线性求解器快约10万倍的推理速度，同时通过实验微调进一步提升了形状估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同操作连续体机器人（CCR）的静态形状估计具有挑战性，因为连续体臂与被操作的柔性物体形成闭环系统，必须同时满足静态平衡和几何闭环约束。传统方法（如迭代非线性求解器）计算代价高昂，而纯数据驱动的神经网络在有限且含噪的训练数据下难以保证物理一致性和闭环约束的满足。

Method: 提出一种约束感知的物理信息神经网络（PINN），基于几何变量应变公式对肌腱驱动CCR进行建模。网络训练中引入两个关键残差项：(1) 投影静态平衡残差，用于强制执行力学平衡条件；(2) 配置级几何残差，用于保证闭环链的几何一致性。该方法结合物理约束与数据驱动学习，在仿真中与纯数据驱动ANN进行对比，并通过实验数据微调进一步提升性能。

Result: 在140个样本且含50%标签噪声的条件下，PINN相比ANN分别将相对配置误差、平衡残差和闭环残差降低了67.88%、67.35%和88.06%。使用完整数据集时，PINN达到0.1597%的相对配置误差，推理时间仅0.1773 ms，而迭代非线性求解器需要17.97秒。实验微调使标记物RMSE从2.657 mm降至0.497 mm，R²从-0.788提升至0.937。

Conclusion: 所提出的约束感知PINN能够实现准确、物理一致且计算高效的闭环连续体机器人静态形状估计，在数据稀缺和含噪条件下仍保持鲁棒性，并具有比传统数值求解器快数个数量级的推理速度，适用于实时应用场景。

Abstract: Static shape estimation of co-manipulative continuum robots (CCRs) is challenging because the continuum arms and manipulated flexible object form a closed chain that must satisfy both static equilibrium and geometric loop-closure constraints. This paper presents a constraint-aware physics-informed neural network (PINN) for static shape estimation of a tendon-driven CCR modeled using the geometric variable strain formulation. The proposed method incorporates a projected static equilibrium residual and a configuration-level geometric residual to enforce the governing mechanics and closed-chain geometry. In simulation, the PINN is compared with a purely data-driven artificial neural network (ANN) under limited and noisy training data. With 140 samples and 50% label noise, the PINN reduces the relative configuration error, equilibrium residual, and closed-chain residual by 67.88%, 67.35%, and 88.06%, respectively. Using the full dataset, the PINN achieves 0.1597% relative configuration error with an inference time of 0.1773 ms, compared with 17.97 s for an iterative nonlinear solver. Experimental fine-tuning reduces the marker RMSE from 2.657 mm to 0.497 mm and increases R2 from -0.788 to 0.937. These results demonstrate accurate, physically consistent, and computationally efficient static shape estimation of closed-chain CCRs.

</details>


### [116] [CLIPPER: Replayable Shortlisted Optimization for Repeated Spatial Coverage Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26819)
*Julian Teusch,Jörg Philipp Müller,Monika Sester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了CLIPPER，一种用于城市尺度微型交通规划的低延迟规划框架。它通过构建有界候选池、精确计算当前增益并检查所有活动约束，在保持与全量贪心算法几乎相同覆盖率的前提下，将规划时间降低13.6~28.9倍（CLIPPER-F）或9~15%（CLIPPER-A），并在德国三个城市（Braunschweig、Munich、Berlin）的实验中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市微型交通规划需要频繁更新可行方案（如地理围栏排除、强制保留点、间距规则、区域上限等约束），每次策略修改都需要新的可行规划。全量贪心算法在城市尺度下每次替代方案需要数十秒，难以支持快速迭代和方案对比。

Method: 提出CLIPPER框架，包含两种变体：(1) 形成有界候选池，但在选择前重新计算精确当前增益并逐一检查所有活动约束；初始排序基于每个候选单独产生的覆盖率；离线全量扫描衡量池内遗漏的增益，在线时使用保守下界触发池扩展或审计。(2) CLIPPER-F为每个提议组分配相同数量的候选槽位；CLIPPER-A则在各组间共享一个候选预算。

Result: 在Braunschweig、Munich和Berlin三个城市的实验中：CLIPPER-F的完整链条平均覆盖率与全量贪心算法差距不超过0.245个百分点，平均规划时间降低13.6~28.9倍；CLIPPER-A在覆盖率优先策略下仅使用全量贪心算法9~15%的规划时间，平均覆盖率差距在Braunschweig为1.82个百分点、Munich为0.12、Berlin为0.27。

Conclusion: CLIPPER使得在城市尺度规划状态下进行快速、可重放的比较成为可能，同时强制执行所有编码的模型约束，为城市交通规划人员提供了高效迭代和方案评估的工具。

Abstract: Operational requirements developed with the City of Braunschweig frame municipal micromobility planning under geofenced exclusions, mandatory retained sites, spacing rules, and area-level caps. Each policy edit requires a new feasible plan; full-set greedy takes tens of seconds per alternative at city scale. We present CLIPPER (Constraint-exact Low-latency Iterative Planning with Pooled Evaluation and Replay). It forms bounded candidate pools but recomputes exact current gains and checks every active constraint before selection. Coverage from each candidate alone sets the initial order. Offline full-set scans measure gains omitted by the pool; online, a conservative bound triggers expansion or audit. CLIPPER-F gives each proposal group the same number of candidate slots. Across Braunschweig, Munich, and Berlin, its mean coverage over complete chains stays within 0.245 percentage points of full-set greedy under the same policy, with 13.6--28.9 times lower mean rollout time. CLIPPER-A instead distributes one shared candidate budget across the groups. Under its coverage-prioritized policy, it uses 9--15% of full-set greedy's rollout time under the same policy, with mean gaps of 1.82 percentage points in Braunschweig, 0.12 in Munich, and 0.27 in Berlin. Together, CLIPPER enables rapid, replayable comparison of recorded city-scale planning states while enforcing every encoded model constraint.

</details>


### [117] [Dispersive Forward Tree Search for Optimal Control: Coverage, Complexity, and Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26314)
*Shashank A. Deshpande,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前向传播的动力学运动规划器DFT*，针对微分平坦非线性系统实现确定性有限样本近最优性保证。通过构造局部散度控制命令集，覆盖已认证的轨迹类，结合代价条件占优剪枝以多项式规模的树实现近最优规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于转向（steering）的运动规划器需要求解状态到状态的边界值问题，这对非线性平台往往不可行。前向传播方法虽然避免了转向需求，但其有限样本行为缺乏理论刻画且实际性能不佳。因此需要一个既有确定性性能保证又能在实践中高效实现的前向传播动力学规划器。

Method: 在微分平坦非线性系统类别中，证明前向树中局部散度（dispersive）控制命令在认证的树规模下包含近最优轨迹。提供控制仿射系统的散度命令集通用构造机制，并证明覆盖认证轨迹类需要指数规模的树，进而提出代价条件占优剪枝过程，使树规模在规划时域上保持多项式量级。实现为广度优先扩展的散度前向树搜索（DFT*），可自然映射到并行硬件，并为单轮车、拖车和四旋翼设计了高效的散度采样器。

Result: DFT*在嵌入式处理器上与最先进的动力学规划器相比，在可比的求解时间内持续提供具有竞争性且通常更优的解决方案质量，并行计算的扩展可进一步加速。在滚动时域循环中实现DFT*，在嵌入式计算预算下展示了动态环境的实时规划能力。

Conclusion: 本文首次为前向传播型动力学规划器提供了确定性有限样本近最优性保证，弥合了理论保证与实用性能之间的差距。DFT*证明了在嵌入式平台上实现高效、可并行、具有理论保证的实时动力学规划是可行的。

Abstract: Steering-based planners require solutions to state-to-state boundary value problems, which can be inaccessible for nonlinear platforms. Forward propagation evades the steering requirement, but the finite-sample behavior of the associated planners remains uncharacterized and their implementations underperform in practice. This paper develops a propagation-based kinodynamic planner with deterministic finite-sample near-optimality guarantees. We work within the large class of differentially flat nonlinear systems and show that a forward tree of locally dispersive control commands contains a near-optimal trajectory at a certified tree size. We provide a general mechanism to construct dispersive command sets for control-affine systems, which are necessary to implement the search algorithm prescribed by the theory. We show that covering the certified trajectory class irrespective of cost provably demands a tree exponentially sized in the problem horizon, and present a cost-conditioned dominance pruning procedure that retains near-optimality at a tree size polynomial in the horizon. We implement the resulting search algorithm, Dispersive Forward Tree search (DFT*), as breadth-first expansion of the forward tree, which maps naturally onto parallel hardware. We design efficient dispersive samplers for the unicycle, the trailer car, and the quadrotor and evaluate challenging planning tasks for these platforms. DFT* delivers consistently competitive and often substantially better solution quality than state-of-the-art kinodynamic planners at comparable solution times on embedded-tier processors, accelerating further as parallel compute is scaled. We also implement DFT* in a receding-horizon loop to demonstrate real-time planning in dynamic environments at embedded-tier compute budgets.

</details>


### [118] [Cross-Platform Benchmark of Neural 3D Reconstruction for Autonomous Laboratory Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26383)
*Yongho Kim,Mengjiao Han,Victor Mateevitsi,Silvio Rizzi,Michael E. Papka,Nicola Ferrier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对实验室机器人场景下的神经三维重建方法进行了系统性的计算平台基准测试，比较了NeRF和3D高斯泼溅在不同GPU设备（从单板计算机到服务器级节点）上的训练与渲染性能，并评估了SAM3D单目重建方法的延迟与保真度差距。结果表明高斯泼溅在更高GPU开销下优于NeRF渲染质量，板载算力不足以支撑交互速率的完整逐场景优化，而SAM3D可在数秒内提供合理几何但细节存在偏差。作者据此提出分层重建管线：轻量级前馈重建维持实时感知-跟踪回路，重型神经重建在合适计算资源上有选择性地调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室自动化机器人依赖三维重建管线将原始相机流转化为可操作的物体表示，但必须在物理控制回路的延迟预算内完成。神经三维重建方法虽然展现了高质量的视图合成能力，但其在实验室机器人实际运行的计算平台上的实时可行性缺乏系统性表征，这正是本文要填补的空白。

Method: 作者构建了一个跨计算平台的系统基准框架，在从单板计算机到服务器级节点的GPU设备上，对NeRF和3D高斯泼溅（3D Gaussian Splatting）的训练与渲染进行统一评估，同时将Meta的SAM3D单目单图重建置于相同的评估维度上，以量化其在延迟和保真度方面相对逐场景优化的差距。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1) 高斯泼溅在渲染质量上优于NeRF，但消耗更多GPU资源；(2) 板载计算能力不足以在交互速率下完成完整逐场景优化；(3) SAM3D能在数秒内生成合理的物体几何，但其细节不匹配可能危及下游操控任务的可靠性。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持分层（tiered）重建管线的设计理念：以轻量级前馈重建作为实时感知与跟踪回路的基础，同时将计算密集的神经重建方法选择性地调度到合适的计算资源上执行，从而实现实时性与重建质量的平衡。

Abstract: Autonomous robots performing laboratory tasks depend on 3D reconstruction pipelines that can turn raw camera streams into actionable object representations within the latency budget of a physical control loop. Neural 3D reconstruction methods have demonstrated high-quality view synthesis, but their real-time viability across the compute platforms on which laboratory robots actually run remains poorly characterized. In this work, we present a systematic compute-platform benchmark of neural 3D reconstruction methods, evaluating NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting training and rendering on GPU-enabled computing devices ranging from single-board computers to server-class nodes, and place Meta's SAM3D single-image reconstruction on the same axes to quantify its latency and fidelity gap relative to per-scene optimization. Our results show that Gaussian Splatting yields higher rendering quality than NeRF at greater GPU cost, and that onboard compute is insufficient for full per-scene optimization at interactive rates. Our preliminary assessment on SAM3D indicates that it delivers plausible object geometry within seconds, but with detail mismatches that can compromise downstream manipulation. Together, these findings motivate tiered pipelines in which lightweight feed-forward reconstruction sustains the real-time perception-and-tracking loop for laboratory robots, while heavier neural reconstruction is scheduled selectively on suitable compute.

</details>


### [119] [RTNav: Towards Real-Time Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26496)
*Easop Lee,Lingyu Zhang,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RTNav是一种面向实时环境下的零样本目标导航架构，显式将推理延迟、异步环境步进和有界计算作为设计考量，在实时变体的HM3D基准上将成功率提升最多11%，SPL提升最多5.1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航方法大多在同步模拟器中开发，环境等待智能体行动，推理时间被忽略。然而在真实世界中，视觉-语言基础模型的推理延迟不容忽视，墙钟时间计入任务预算时，现有顺序式感知-推理-行动架构的性能会显著下降。作者通过实验证明了这一性能退化现象，并以此作为提出新方法的动机。

Method: RTNav将推理延迟、异步环境步进和有界计算作为显式设计考量的一种简单而有效的架构。它改变了传统顺序执行的模式，通过异步化的方式让环境推进与推理过程并行，从而在有限计算预算下最大化智能体在时间限制内的行动效率。

Result: 在HM3D-v1、HM3D-v2和HM3D-OVON的实时变体上，RTNav相较先前工作在成功率上提升最多11%，在按完成时间加权的成功率（SPL）上提升最多5.1个点。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在真实世界的连续运行场景中，推理延迟和异步执行是目标导航系统设计中不可忽视的关键因素。RTNav证明了将实时性约束直接纳入架构设计可以显著提升实际部署性能，为未来导航智能体从同步模拟走向真实部署提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Navigation in unknown environments to find unforeseen objects has become increasingly feasible with capable vision and language foundation models. However, these models also introduce non-negligible inference latency, which becomes an important concern when agents must operate continuously in the real world. Most state-of-the-art methods are still developed in synchronous simulators, where the environment waits for the agent to act and inference time is effectively free. As a result, agents are often designed around the sequential execution of perception, reasoning, and action, with little regard for time constraints. Under real-time execution, where wall-clock time counts towards the task budget, the inefficiencies of these architectures become clear. We show that recent zero-shot object navigation methods suffer consistent performance degradation under such realistic timing conditions. Motivated by this observation, we propose RTNav, a simple but effective architecture that treats inference latency, asynchronous environment stepping, and bounded compute as explicit design considerations. Evaluated on real-time variants of HM3D-v1, HM3D-v2, and HM3D-OVON, RTNav improves the success rate by up to 11% and the Success weighted by Completion Time by up to 5.1 points over prior work.

</details>


### [120] [Closing the Loop on the Poppy Humanoid: Bipedal Locomotion with Linear-Quadratic Control and Learned Cost Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26505)
*Xulin Chen,Borui He,Ruipeng Liu,Naveed Tahir,Zhenyu Gan,Garrett E. Katz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对开源低成本机器人Poppy的可靠自主双足行走问题，提出了一种基于线性二次调节器（LQR）框架的闭环行走控制器，从开环轨迹播放数据中学习二次代价函数，显著提升行走可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Poppy人形机器人是适合AI研究和教育的开源低成本平台，但目前在标准Poppy硬件上尚无已发表的可靠、自主双足运动方法，存在方法论空白。

Method: 基于LQR框架构建闭环行走控制器用于轨迹跟踪。先从名义行走轨迹的开环播放中收集数据，再通过数据学习LQR控制器的二次代价函数，从而改进运动可靠性。

Result: 通过实证验证闭环控制器，与开环轨迹播放相比，行走性能在统计学上取得显著改善。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种在标准Poppy硬件上实现可靠双足行走的功能性闭环控制方案，填补了该平台自主运动方法论空白。

Abstract: The Poppy Humanoid is an open-source, low-cost robot suitable for research and education in artificial intelligence. However, we are unaware of any published methodology that achieves reliable, unassisted bipedal locomotion on the standard Poppy hardware. This paper contributes a functional closed-loop walking controller for Poppy, based on the linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) framework for trajectory tracking. Starting with data collected from open-loop playback of a nominal walking trajectory, our proposed method learns a quadratic cost function for an LQR controller that substantially improves the reliability of the motion. The closed-loop controller is validated empirically, demonstrating statistically significant improvements in walking performance compared to open-loop trajectory playback.

</details>


### [121] [Memory Anchors for Continual Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26545)
*Maximilian Du,Zhanyi Sun,Chen Xu,Paarth Shah,Masha Itkina,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"Memory Anchors"（记忆锚点）概念，指出在持续学习机器人策略时，回放缓冲区中仅少数经验对防止灾难性遗忘起关键作用。这些经验位于新旧任务表征发生坍缩但动作冲突的区域。排除了10%的记忆锚点会导致LIBERO基准上遗忘率增加4.5倍，而富集记忆锚点可将高冲突任务遗忘降低63%，并在真实机器人上实现成功持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署在真实世界中的机器人策略需要持续学习新任务而不遗忘已有行为。现有的回放缓冲区方法通常对所有过往经验进行随机采样，忽视了不同经验在防止灾难性遗忘中可能存在的不均衡贡献。

Method: 首先识别"Memory Anchors"：即新旧任务观察表征发生坍缩（collapse）但需要冲突动作的区域。在这些区域重放旧数据能有效防止旧任务知识的破坏性覆盖。随后在回放缓冲区中针对性地富集这些记忆锚点，而非均匀随机采样。

Result: 在LIBERO基准套件上，排除仅10%的记忆锚点再采样缓冲区，灾难性遗忘增加4.5倍以上；反之，在回放缓冲区中富集记忆锚点可使高冲突任务的遗忘率降低63%，并能在真实机器人上成功完成两个任务序列的持续学习。

Conclusion: 回放缓冲区中的经验对防止灾难性遗忘的贡献高度不均，识别并富集"Memory Anchors"（新旧任务表征冲突区域的旧经验）可以显著提升机器人策略的持续学习性能，为回放缓冲区采样策略提供了更有针对性的新方向。

Abstract: Robot policies deployed in the wild should have the capability to continually learn new tasks without forgetting existing behaviors. A common approach to combat such catastrophic forgetting is to train on new task data with a replay buffer of previously learned task data. Although this buffer is commonly sampled randomly from all prior experiences, we show that a small set of these experiences contributes greatly in anchoring past performance. We call these experiences Memory Anchors. We identify Memory Anchors in regions where representations of new-task observations collapse onto those of old-task observations even though the tasks require conflicting actions, like when a familiar object must be manipulated in a new way. Rehearsing old data in this region plays a key role in preventing destructive overwriting of past task knowledge, serving as this critical Memory Anchor role. Excluding only 10% Memory Anchors before sampling the buffer leads to more than a 4.5x increase in catastrophic forgetting on the LIBERO benchmark suites. Conversely, enriching the replay buffer with Memory Anchors can decrease high-conflict task forgetting by 63% and enables successful continual learning of two task sequences on a real robot. Videos and additional visualizations can be found at https://robot-adaptation.github.io/MemoryAnchors

</details>


### [122] [TrapVLA: Trapping Vision-Language-Action Models in Configured Failure Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26578)
*Jun-Hui Liu,Kun-Yu Lin,Yi-Lin Wei,Xu-Han Chen,Yinghao Li,Zhuohao Li,Yuan-Ming Li,Qing Zhang,Xiaoyi Fan,Dongmei Jiang,Yan Li,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的新型后门攻击任务"Configured Failure Trapping"（配置化失败陷阱），通过隐秘文本触发器激活攻击并诱导机器人按指定方式失败（如指定位置偏移的抓取），而非以往攻击中任意失败即可。作者构建了数据引擎、自动化评估套件及两个新基准（Trap-LIBERO和Trap-RoboTwin），并提出TrapVLA方法通过显式学习触发器诱导的动作残差来引导策略产生配置化失败行为。实验表明该方法在仿真和真实机器人环境中均能有效注入配置化失败模式，同时保持干净数据性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对VLA模型的后门攻击仅将"任意任务失败"视为攻击成功，缺乏对失败方式的精确控制，攻击隐蔽性和危害度不足。作者希望通过可控的失败模式（如指定位置偏移），使攻击更难被检测且更具现实威胁，从而推动该领域更严谨的攻防研究。

Method: 1) 提出Configured Failure Trapping攻击任务，要求攻击者精确控制机器人失败方式；2) 设计数据引擎合成高质量目标轨迹（含触发器诱导的配置化失败示例）；3) 构建自动化评估套件衡量配置化失败保真度；4) 建立Trap-LIBERO和Trap-RoboTwin两个基准，涵盖四种代表性失败模式；5) 提出TrapVLA方法，显式学习触发词诱导的动作残差，将策略策略导向配置化失败行为；6) 在仿真和真实机器人场景进行验证。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实机器人环境中，TrapVLA能够有效将配置化失败模式注入VLA模型，同时在大幅保留干净数据上的任务性能。实验验证了该方法在多种失败模式下的有效性以及攻击的隐蔽性。

Conclusion: Configured Failure Trapping是比传统后门攻击更具挑战性和隐蔽性的新型攻击任务，TrapVLA证明了该攻击的可行性与威胁性，为VLA模型的安全防御研究提供了新基准和评估工具。

Abstract: This work introduces Configured Failure Trapping, a novel backdoor attack task against Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, which aims to activate attacks through stealthy textual triggers and induce configured failure modes. Unlike prior backdoor attacks that treat any task failure as a successful attack, Configured Failure Trapping requires the attacker to control how the robot fails (e.g., causing the robot to grasp with a specified positional offset), making it substantially more challenging and hard to detect. To support the new task, we propose an effective data engine for synthesizing high-quality target trajectories and an automated suite for measuring configured-failure fidelity. Then, based on this foundation, we construct two new benchmarks, namely Trap-LIBERO and Trap-RoboTwin, that instantiate Configured Failure Trapping across four representative failure modes. To address this task, we identify sparse action deviation as a critical challenge and accordingly propose a novel method named TrapVLA, which explicitly learns trigger-induced action residuals to steer the policy toward the configured failure behavior. Extensive experiments across simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic settings show that TrapVLA effectively injects configured failure modes into VLA models while largely preserving performance on clean data. Project page: https://john-liua.github.io/TrapVLA/

</details>


### [123] [SOLO: Stable Omni-terrain Long-Horizon Perceptive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26583)
*Pihai Sun,Gang Han,Jingkai Sun,Jiahao Ma,Zeran Su,Zelin Tao,Peiran Liu,Shuai Shi,Wei Cui,Zifan Wang,Jialin Yu,Wen Zhao,Kangning Yin,Jiaxu Wang,Jiahang Cao,Lingfeng Zhang,Hao Cheng,Jian Tang,Yijie Guo,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SOLO是一个统一的框架，通过两个关键设计解决具身人形机器人在长时程复杂地形遍历中的脆弱性问题：Query Reconstructor（QR）用傅里叶编码的细胞查询从深度-本体感受令牌中检索空间特异的证据，保持锐利地形边界；Trajectory-Aware MSE（TA-MSE）蒸馏将下一状态师生分歧加入PPO奖励，通过GAE将未来分歧惩罚传播到前置动作。仿真中高度图L1误差降低3.3-4.0倍，压力测试地形上平均穿越成功率97.5%、踩踏石成功率96%，远超密集重构变体的75-75.6%和0-3%。零样本部署于仅配备胸前深度相机的机器人上，完成了连续1.5公里室外路线和室内混合地形课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够在复杂地形上长时间行走而不失去平衡，但基于感知的人形机器人策略会因感知误差和控制误差的累积而变得脆弱。论文旨在解决这两个共同导致长时程脆弱性的根源：密集地形重建会平滑掉对动作至关重要的细节，以及逐点模仿缺乏时间维度的信用分配。

Method: SOLO由两个核心组件构成：(1) Query Reconstructor（QR），使用傅里叶编码的细胞查询从深度-本体感受令牌中检索空间特异的证据，从而保留锐利的地形边界；(2) Trajectory-Aware MSE（TA-MSE）蒸馏，在PPO奖励中加入下一状态教师-学生分歧项，使Generalized Advantage Estimation（GAE）能够将未来的分歧惩罚传播到前置动作上，实现时间维度的信用分配。

Result: 在仿真中，QR将高度图L1误差降低3.3-4.0倍；TA-MSE在课程推进上超过PPO和MSE+PPO基线。在压力测试地形上，SOLO达到97.5%的平均穿越成功率和96%的踩踏石成功率，而密集重构变体分别仅为75.0-75.6%和0-3%。零样本部署中，仅用胸前深度相机和本体感受，SOLO完成了1.5公里户外连续路线和室内混合地形课程。

Conclusion: SOLO通过同时改善地形重建的空间精度和训练的时序信用分配两个维度，显著提升了人形机器人在长时程复杂地形遍历中的稳健性，并在真实世界零样本部署中验证了其有效性，展现了从仿真到现实的泛化能力。

Abstract: Humans traverse complex terrain over long distances without losing balance, whereas perceptive humanoid policies become fragile as perception and control errors accumulate. We present SOLO, a unified framework addressing two compounding causes of this long-horizon fragility: dense terrain reconstruction smooths action-critical details, and pointwise imitation lacks temporal credit assignment. Its Query Reconstructor (QR) uses Fourier-encoded cell queries to retrieve spatially specific evidence from depth-proprioception tokens, preserving sharp terrain boundaries. Trajectory-Aware MSE (TA-MSE) Distillation adds next-state teacher-student disagreement to the PPO reward, enabling Generalized Advantage Estimation to propagate future disagreement penalties to preceding actions. In simulation, QR reduces height-map L1 error by factors of 3.3-4.0, while TA-MSE surpasses PPO and MSE+PPO in curriculum progression. On stress-test terrains, SOLO achieves 97.5% mean traversal success and 96% stepping-stone success, versus 75.0-75.6% and 0-3% for dense-reconstructor variants. Deployed zero-shot with only a chest-mounted depth camera and proprioception, SOLO completes a continuous 1.5-km outdoor route and an indoor mixed-terrain course. Project page: https://sunpihai-up.github.io/solo/

</details>


### [124] [Relaxation-Aware Multimodal Sensing of Soft Gripper Driven by Structure-Perception-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26622)
*Yanzhe Wang,Hao Wu,Ziyi Zheng,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集结构-感知-学习于一体的变刚度软体抓手框架，通过视觉与红外热成像实时追踪形变与温度场，结合温度耦合的粘弹性力表示和物理信息学习模型，在280秒抓取保持任务中实现平均绝对误差0.066N的力控制，比固定孔径和仅瞬时反馈的基线提升80%和95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人手在长期抓取保持中因软聚合物的粘弹性特性导致应力松弛，抓取力持续衰减，稳定持续的抓取仍是根本性挑战。人类抓取通过相位相关的刚度调节与连续感知反馈来应对这一问题，但现有软体抓手缺乏类似的能力整合。

Method: 提出集成结构-感知-学习三要素的框架：(1) 设计变刚度软体抓手；(2) 利用机载视觉和红外热成像实时跟踪形变与温度场，连续感知交互状态；(3) 构建温度耦合的粘弹性力表示结合物理信息学习模型，重建力趋势并在保持阶段提供显式补偿。

Result: 在280秒力控抓取保持任务中，方法将平均绝对误差维持在0.066N，相比于固定孔径基线和仅瞬时反馈基线分别提升80%和95%，有效缓解了应力松弛导致的力衰减。

Conclusion: 结果支持机制-人工智能协同设计的观点：机制决定了可行的交互范围，而学习补偿了粘弹性动力学的残余不确定性，两者结合使稳定持续的软体抓取成为可能。

Abstract: Achieving stable, sustained grasping with soft robotic hands remains a fundamental challenge. Compliance enables safe and adaptive contact, yet the intrinsic viscoelasticity of soft polymers leads to stress relaxation and a continuous decay of grasping force during holding. Inspired by human grasping, which combines phase-dependent stiffness regulation with continuous sensing and feedback, this paper presents an integrated structure--perception--learning framework. We develop a variable-stiffness soft gripper that uses onboard vision and infrared thermography to track deformation and the temperature field in real time, preserving continuous tracking of the interaction state. To mitigate relaxation-induced force decay, we propose a temperature-coupled viscoelastic force representation, together with a physics-informed learning model, to reconstruct the force trend and provide explicit compensation during holding. Experiments show that, in a 280s force-controlled grasp-and-hold task, the proposed method maintains the desired force with a mean absolute error of 0.066N, outperforming fixed-aperture and instantaneous-only baselines by 80% and 95%, respectively. Overall, the results support a mechanism--AI co-design view: mechanisms shape feasible interactions, while learning compensates remaining uncertainty in viscoelastic dynamics, together enabling stable, sustained grasping.

</details>


### [125] [FLARE: A Failure-Aware Framework for Autonomous Correction and Recovery in Visual-Language Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26645)
*Ganlong Zhao,Zijia Tang,Xingping Chen,Zhanghui Kuang,Ye Tian,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FLARE框架，通过"Retry"（重试）和"Reset"（重置）两种机制赋予视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）错误恢复能力，使其在复杂长时间机器人操作任务中面对执行误差（如抓取失败、物体掉落、意外碰撞）时能够自主恢复并提高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常在轨迹单调、无失败演示的数据上训练，导致其性能脆弱，无法从常见的执行错误（如抓取未成功、物体掉落、意外碰撞）中恢复。这种对"完美"数据的依赖严重限制了VLA在真实世界操作中的鲁棒性。

Method: FLARE框架包含两部分：(1) "Retry"机制：在演示中注入扰动和桥接片段，将机器人位姿与环境状态解耦，使策略能够自主处理执行偏差；(2) "Reset"流程：利用MLLM对执行视频进行离线失败分析，自动识别分布外（OOD）状态，据此高效、定向地收集少量以物体为中心的"Reset"技能库并进行训练，以将环境恢复到任务有效状态。推理时，在线MLLM监控器在任务执行与"Reset"技能之间进行仲裁调度。

Result: 在具有挑战性的、接触密集的操作任务实验中，FLARE方法显著提升了任务成功率与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过"Retry"与"Reset"双机制结合、离线自动失败分析与在线监控仲裁，FLARE有效弥补了VLA在错误恢复能力上的不足，为VLA在实际操作中的鲁棒部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action Models~(VLAs) have demonstrated significant promise in generalizing to complex, long-horizon robotic manipulation tasks. However, their performance remains brittle, as they are typically trained on trajectory-monotonic, failure-free demonstrations. This reliance on ``perfect" data leaves them unable to recover from common execution errors, such as a missed grasp, a dropped object, or an unexpected collision. In this paper, we propose FLARE, a novel framework that endows VLAs with robust error recovery capabilities through a ``Retry" and ``Reset" paradigm. First, we introduce a ``Retry" mechanism by injecting perturbation and bridging segments that decouple robot pose from environment state into demonstrations, enabling the policy to autonomously handle execution deviations. Second, to address critical, state-breaking (OOD) failures, we introduce a ``Reset" pipeline. We leverage an MLLM for offline failure analysis to automatically identify OOD states from execution videos. This analysis enables the efficient, targeted collection of a small library of object-centric ``Reset" skills, which are trained to restore the environment to a task-valid state. Our full framework integrates these learned policies. At inference, an online MLLM monitor arbitrates between task execution and ``Reset" skills. Experiments on challenging, contact-rich manipulation tasks show our approach significantly improves task success and robustness.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond the Proving Ground: Independent Public-Road Testing of Assisted Lane Change Systems using LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26669)
*Marcello Cellina,Akos Kriston,Antonio Migneco,Davide Maggi,Stefano Favelli,Fabrizio Re,Fabrizio Minarini,Andrea Nuovo,Riccardo Dona,Biagio Ciuffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在公共道路上独立测试辅助车道变更（Assisted Lane Change）系统的方法论，并在法国A31高速公路上进行了测试活动。测试使用基于LiDAR的车辆检测与跟踪系统的测试车辆，结合RTK-GNSS接收器评估性能，并与UNECE第79号法规的最小距离要求进行对比。在27个预定义变道操作中，18个完成、9个被抑制，其中6例系统允许了不符合法规最小距离要求的操作，3例在考虑测量不确定度后偏差仍具有统计显著性。这是首次旨在评估辅助车道变更系统符合第79号法规安全距离要求的公共道路测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用高级驾驶辅助系统（ADAS）的测试对安全性和合规性至关重要，但现有测试场地场景无法反映真实驾驶复杂性，而地理围栏测试需要制造商协作且限制评估独立性。因此需要一种独立且能在真实道路条件下评估辅助车道变更系统合规性的方法。

Method: 在法国A31高速公路上进行测试活动，使用配备LiDAR车辆检测与跟踪系统的测试车辆，测试覆盖了测试车辆与接管车辆之间不同车距和车速的组合。利用RTK-GNSS接收器评估检测和跟踪性能，将记录的变道轨迹与UNECE第79号法规的变道抑制要求进行比较。

Result: 在27个预定义变道操作中，18个完成、9个被抑制。6例中系统允许了不符合法规最小距离要求的操作，其中3例在考虑测量不确定度后偏差仍具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 这是首次面向辅助车道变更系统符合第79号法规安全距离要求的公共道路测试活动，结果证明了基于LiDAR的感知测试方法适用性，该方法可支持市场监督和未来法规修订，揭示审批程序中未覆盖的真实驾驶行为。

Abstract: Testing of commercial Advanced Driver Assistance Systems is essential to ensure safety and compliance during type approval and in service operation. However, proving ground scenarios may not reflect real world driving complexity, while geo fencing can require manufacturer collaboration and limit assessment independence. This work presents a methodology for independently testing Assisted Lane Change systems on public roads. A campaign on the A31 French motorway used a test vehicle equipped with a LiDAR based vehicle detection and tracking system. Tests covered combinations of inter vehicle distance and speed between the test vehicle and the take over vehicle. Real time kinematic global navigation satellite system receivers assessed detection and tracking performance. Recorded lane change trajectories were compared with the lane change suppression requirements of UNECE Regulation Number 79. Of 27 predefined lane change manoeuvres, 18 were completed and 9 suppressed. In 6 cases, the system allowed manoeuvres that did not meet regulatory minimum distance requirements. In 3 cases, the deviation remained statistically significant after accounting for measurement uncertainty. To the authors knowledge, this is the first public road campaign designed to assess Assisted Lane Change compliance with Regulation Number 79 safety distance requirements. The results demonstrate the suitability of LiDAR based sensing for this purpose. The methodology can support market surveillance and future regulatory revisions by revealing real world behaviours not covered by approval procedures.

</details>


### [127] [PredVLA: A Sub-Million-Parameter Predictive-Coding Policy for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26673)
*Hiroki Sawada,Shunichi Kasahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PredVLA是一个仅含68万可训练参数、无需机器人数据预训练的语言条件预测编码策略，在LIBERO基准上达到86.9%（短程）和75.4%（含长程）的平均成功率，显著超越同等参数规模的Transformer和LSTM策略（分别为其3.7倍和7.4倍），并通过预测误差驱动的潜变量推理实现可量化的在线状态修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练视觉-语言-动作模型主导了现代机器人操作基准，但模型规模对语言条件控制是否必要尚不明确。作者旨在探究在极小参数量预算下，根本不同的控制架构是否仍能保持竞争力。

Method: PredVLA采用分层生成式循环动力学架构，预测视觉特征与本体感觉，观测仅通过在线推理由感官预测误差影响潜状态，从而形成预测编码范式。模型仅0.68M可训练参数，无机器人数据预训练。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，PredVLA在三个短程任务套件上达到86.9%的平均成功率，包含长程套件时为75.4%。在受控对比（相同冻结前端、演示、动作解码器和评估协议）下，其平均成功率为同等参数规模Transformer和LSTM策略的3.7倍和7.4倍。禁用预测误差推理可得到精确的开环控制条件，使观测驱动修正的贡献可量化。

Conclusion: 亚百万参数的循环生成策略即可在现代语言条件操作基准上取得强性能，同时提供显式的预测误差驱动在线状态修正机制，表明模型规模并非语言条件控制表现的必要决定因素。

Abstract: Large pretrained vision-language-action models dominate modern robot-manipulation benchmarks, but it remains unclear how much model scale is necessary for strong language-conditioned control, or whether fundamentally different control architectures can remain competitive at much smaller parameter budgets. We present PredVLA, a language-conditioned predictive-coding policy with only 0.68 million trainable network parameters and no robot-data pretraining, whose hierarchical generative recurrent dynamics predict visual features and proprioception while observations influence latent state only through online inference from the resulting sensory prediction errors. On LIBERO, PredVLA achieves an 86.9% mean success rate across the three short-horizon suites and 75.4% when the long-horizon suite is included. Under a controlled comparison using the same frozen front end, demonstrations, action decoder, and evaluation protocol, PredVLA achieves 3.7x and 7.4x mean success rates of parameter-matched Transformer and LSTM policies, respectively. The predictive-coding formulation also makes the contribution of observation-driven correction directly measurable: because observations influence the recurrent state only through prediction-error-based latent inference, disabling this inference yields an exact open-loop control condition. Together, these results show that a sub-million-parameter recurrent generative policy can achieve strong performance on modern language-conditioned manipulation benchmarks while providing an explicit mechanism for prediction-error-driven online state correction.

</details>


### [128] [Residual Deep Reinforcement Learning-Based Computed Torque Control for a Cable-Driven Lower-Limb Rehabilitation Robot under Disturbances and Parametric Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26739)
*Mohammad-Hossein Fakouri,Ali Keymasi-Khalaji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种残差深度强化学习增强的计算力矩控制框架用于绳索驱动下肢康复机器人的精确轨迹跟踪。计算力矩控制生成标称命令，有界的DDPG策略仅提供额外的补偿力矩，在仿真中验证了该框架在不确性、扰动及联合约束条件下较传统方法的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绳索驱动下肢康复机器人面临模型不确定性、外部扰动、关节约束及绳索单向牵引等挑战，传统基于模型的控制器对模型失配敏感，而纯学习型控制缺乏透明性且难以处理约束。需要一种既能保持模型可解释性又能增强鲁棒性的混合控制方案。

Method: 提出残差深度强化学习增强的计算力矩控制框架：计算力矩控制生成标称指令，有界深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）策略仅提供额外补偿力矩。在标称、不确定、扰动、组合及泛化条件下进行仿真评估，并执行轨迹跟踪、关节极限、绳索需求、工作空间可行性和绳索雅可比诊断。

Result: 在各种评估条件下，残差控制器相比纯计算力矩控制在轨迹跟踪和扰动抑制方面均有所改善，同时保持了可解释的模型基命令结构和代表性评估中的可行性约束。更广泛的测试表明跟踪改进可超越代表性案例，但也暴露了依赖轨迹的约束限制。

Conclusion: 有界残差学习可作为仿真康复机器人控制的实用鲁棒性增强策略，研究结果为后续约束感知和实验验证提供了动机与方向。

Abstract: Accurate trajectory tracking in cable-driven lower-limb rehabilitation robots is challenging because model uncertainty, external disturbances, joint constraints, and pull-only cable actuation can degrade nominal control performance. Conventional model-based controllers provide an interpretable control structure but remain sensitive to model mismatch, whereas fully learning-based control can reduce transparency and complicate constraint-aware operation. This study proposes a residual deep reinforcement learning-enhanced computed torque control framework in which computed torque control generates the nominal command and a bounded Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient policy supplies only an additional compensating torque. The approach is evaluated in simulation under nominal, uncertain, disturbed, combined, and generalization conditions, together with trajectory-tracking, joint-limit, cable-demand, workspace-feasibility, and cable-Jacobian diagnostics. Across the evaluated conditions, the residual controller improves tracking and disturbance rejection relative to computed torque control while preserving the interpretable model-based command structure and satisfying the reported feasibility checks in the representative evaluation. Broader tests indicate that tracking improvements can persist beyond the representative case while also exposing trajectory-dependent constraint limitations. These results support bounded residual learning as a practical robustness-enhancement strategy for simulation-based rehabilitation robot control and motivate further constraint-aware and experimental validation.

</details>


### [129] [MeshPriorDiT: Hierarchical Modeling for Action-Conditioned Cloth Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26766)
*Zihang Wang,Jianming Hu,Shang Su,Hao Huang,Mengkai Shi,Jun Gao,Shuo Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MeshPriorDiT，一种分层布料动力学预测模型，将网格GNN的结构化先验与Diffusion Transformer的生成式残差相结合，在15步自回归滚动预测中显著降低全局MSE，同时保持与GNN相当的材料应变精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布料动力学预测方法存在两难困境：基于网格的GNN能捕获局部物理响应但受限于消息传递范围，难以协调拓扑远距离区域且自回归滚动误差累积；基于Transformer的动力学模型虽能捕获长程交互但缺乏显式材料连接，需要从数据中学习局部拓扑响应。需要一种方法同时兼顾局部物理合理性与长程协调能力。

Method: 提出分层架构MeshPriorDiT，分解为两个模块：(1) 动作条件网格GNN先验，预测多步顶点位移，生成尊重材料拓扑和抓取约束的参考轨迹；(2) 残差DiT，以历史状态、规划动作和网格先验为条件，通过条件流匹配联合生成先验未捕获的残差运动；生成的残差进一步通过材料邻接信息进行缩放和解码，以协调相邻顶点的修正。

Result: 在三个布料操作任务的15步自回归滚动预测中，MeshPriorDiT相比GNN-Only基线平均降低全局MSE 43.42%，相比DiT-DDPM基线降低75.03%，同时Edge-strain MSE与GNN-Only相当。

Conclusion: 将结构化网格先验与生成式扩散Transformer残差相结合的分层范式，能够有效融合GNN的局部物理建模优势与Transformer的长程全局交互能力，为动作条件下的布料动力学预测提供了一种兼顾精度与物理合理性的新方案。

Abstract: Action-conditioned cloth dynamics prediction requires both locally plausible deformation and long-range coordination. Existing approaches largely follow two paradigms. Mesh-based GNNs capture local physical responses through material connectivity. However, their finite message-passing range limits coordination between topologically distant regions, while autoregressive rollouts tend to accumulate prediction errors. Transformer-based dynamics models capture long-range interactions through global attention, but often operate without explicit material connectivity and must learn local topological responses directly from data. We propose MeshPriorDiT, a hierarchical dynamics model that decomposes future cloth motion into a structured mesh prior and a generative residual. An action-conditioned mesh GNN first predicts multi-step vertex displacements, yielding a reference trajectory that respects material topology and grasp constraints. Conditioned on historical states, planned actions, and the mesh prior, a Residual DiT then uses conditional flow matching to jointly generate the residual motion not captured by the prior. The generated residual is further rescaled and decoded using material adjacency to coordinate corrections across neighboring vertices. We evaluate MeshPriorDiT on 15-step autoregressive rollouts across three cloth manipulation tasks. Averaged over the three tasks, MeshPriorDiT reduces average Global MSE by 43.42% relative to the GNN-Only baseline and by 75.03% relative to the DiT-DDPM baseline, while maintaining a favorable Edge-strain MSE comparable to that of GNN-Only.

</details>


### [130] [Online Joint Calibration of Steering Offset and Planar LiDAR Extrinsics for Wheeled Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26789)
*Subodh Mishra,Arindam Dhar,Suprotim Majumdar,Naveen Arulselvan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）的方法，在自行车运动学模型中在线估计仓库移动机器人（WMR）的转向偏移和平面LiDAR外参，替代传统手动标定流程，实验表明该方法能显著降低横向跟踪误差（CTE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库移动机器人依赖准确的转向感知和LiDAR到车身的外参标定来实现可靠路径跟踪。传统方法中，转向"零点"通常通过手动方式（如PS4手柄目测直线性）设定，LiDAR外参则假设来自CAD设计值且可能在维护后漂移。这些静态手动标定流程在安全关键环境中频频导致标定错误，引发蛇形摆动、横向误差增大等问题，亟需一种在线自动标定方案。

Method: 提出基于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）的在线估计方法，在自行车运动学模型框架内同时估计转向偏移和平面LiDAR外参（包括位置和姿态），将原本需要手动离线的标定过程转化为可在线持续更新的状态估计问题。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，校正转向偏移后能够大幅降低横向跟踪误差（CTE），验证了所提出方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该EKF在线标定方法为仓库移动机器人的手动标定流程提供了原则性的替代方案，能够适应维护后外参漂移等实际场景，提升路径跟踪性能与安全可靠性。

Abstract: Accurate steering sensing and LiDAR-to-vehicle extrinsics are crucial for reliable path tracking in warehouse mobile robots (WMRs); miscalibration often leads to snaking, weaving, and elevated cross-track error (CTE). In practice, steering ``zero'' is commonly set manually (e.g., eyeballing straightness via a PS4 joystick), while LiDAR extrinsics are assumed from CAD and may drift after maintenance. Such static, manual procedures frequently cause miscalibration in safety-critical environments. This paper presents an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF)--based method for online estimation of steering offset and planar LiDAR extrinsics within a bicycle-kinematics model, providing a principled alternative to manual calibration. Experiments on real datasets show that correcting steering offset reduces CTE substantially, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [131] [Rapid On-Robot Learning for Dynamic Manipulation Skills: Robot Juggling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26800)
*Taeyoon Lee,Chunpeng Wang,Christopher G. Atkeson,Alfred A. Rizzi,Nicolas Rojas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在线学习框架，使双手机器人能够在几分钟内直接在物理硬件上学习多样化的抛接杂耍模式，即使存在显著的sim2real差距。核心思想是：即使模型与真实相差甚远，仍可极有价值地用于学习——学习应建立在机器人现有知识之上而非取代它，通过正则化的记忆学习（保留全局先验模型来外推经验稀疏区域，同时从积累经验中学习局部模型）实现高效稳定的在线学习，并通过互可达集合确保安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在面对sim2real差距时通常依赖大规模试错或重新训练，效率低且不安全。作者的核心洞察是，即使先验模型与真实环境差距较大，其仍能提供有价值的信息，学习不应完全抛弃先验而应从零探索，而是应在现有知识基础上持续改进。

Method: 提出正则化记忆学习框架：从积累的物理经验中学习局部模型，同时保留全局先验模型用于在经验稀疏区域进行外推。此外，构建一个互可达集合（mutually reachable set），确保机器人在连续抛接动作之间可以安全过渡，不会将任一手臂驱动到违反关节或执行器限制的状态。

Result: 在配备多指手和机载视觉的双手机器人上，所提方法在不到5分钟的真实交互时间内，安全地学习并组合了五种经典三球杂耍模式：cascade、tennis、half-shower、shower和box。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了机器人可以构建在不完美的先验知识之上，通过自身真实世界经验持续精炼行为。更广泛地，这指向了机器人在面对现实与仿真差距时，应该利用一切可用知识的有效策略。

Abstract: We present an online learning framework that enables a bimanual robot to acquire diverse juggling patterns directly on physical hardware within minutes, even with a significant sim2real gap. One of the most important lessons from this work is that a model, even when far from reality, can be extremely useful for learning. This motivates a central philosophy of our approach: learning should build upon the robot's current knowledge rather than replace it. Our regularized memory-based learning puts this principle into practice by learning a local model from accumulated experience while retaining the global prior model to extrapolate where experience is sparse. This enables efficient and stable online learning from each new experience without resorting to uninformed exploration over a vast space of possible behaviors. Equally important to continual on-robot learning is safety, allowing the robot to repeatedly practice and improve in the real world. We construct a mutually reachable set that allows safe transitions between successive throws and catches, without driving either arm into a state from which its next action would require violating the robot's joint or actuator limits. Together, these ideas enable a bimanual robot with multi-fingered hands and onboard vision to safely learn and compose five canonical three-ball juggling patterns, including cascade, tennis, half-shower, shower, and box, within less than 5 minutes of real-world interaction. More broadly, this work points toward robots that build upon imperfect prior knowledge and continually refine their behavior through their own real-world experience.

</details>


### [132] [TemporalFlow-VLA: Learning Physically Grounded Execution History for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26821)
*Jiarui Yang,Yehao Lu,Yuning Su,Yu Zhong,Yufeng Xie,Yazhou Zhang,Haiyu Lan,Kaixiang Lu,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Junwei Liang,Enyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TemporalFlow-VLA通过物理接地的时间监督学习紧凑的执行历史表征，利用机器人状态、几何和标定相机构建训练专用的机器人-表面时间流目标，并监督两个执行对齐的时间查询，在无需部署时几何处理的情况下实现LIBERO和RoboTwin上的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型虽利用预训练的视觉-语言表征进行机器人控制，但简单添加历史帧无法可靠捕捉近期物理变化，在多阶段操作中视觉相似状态可能因先前执行不同而需要不同动作，需要一种紧凑且有物理依据的历史表征方案。

Method: 提出TemporalFlow-VLA框架：利用记录的机器人状态、机器人几何和标定相机构建机器人-表面时间流作为仅训练阶段的目标；监督两个执行对齐的时间查询，为动作专家提供结构化历史信息；几何监督路径在部署时不参与计算。

Result: 在LIBERO上平均成功率达97.63±0.26%，其中LIBERO Long为96.60±0.87%；在12个RoboTwin任务上Clean/Randomized成功率分别为85.5%/84.2%。在长时程多阶段操作上优势最为明显。受控历史干预实验表明动作预测同时依赖历史内容和时间顺序。通过异步特征缓存，时间条件化在服务器端保持单帧级采样延迟，无额外历史编码开销。

Conclusion: TemporalFlow-VLA提供了一种紧凑、物理接地的时间接口，能够利用有序执行历史而无需在部署时进行显式运动估计或几何处理，尤其擅长长时程多阶段操作任务。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models leverage pretrained vision-language representations for robot control, yet simply adding historical frames does not reliably capture recent physical change. This is especially problematic in multi-stage manipulation, where visually similar states may require different actions depending on prior execution. To address this challenge, we present TemporalFlow-VLA, which learns compact execution history through physically grounded temporal supervision. Using recorded robot states, robot geometry, and calibrated cameras, we construct robot-surface temporal flow as a training-only target and supervise two execution-aligned temporal queries that provide structured history to the action expert. The geometric supervision path is not evaluated at deployment. TemporalFlow-VLA achieves 97.63 +/- 0.26% average success on LIBERO, including 96.60 +/- 0.87% on LIBERO Long, and 85.5%/84.2% Clean/Randomized success across 12 RoboTwin tasks. It shows its clearest advantage over prior methods on longer-horizon, multi-stage manipulation. Controlled history interventions show that action prediction depends on both historical content and temporal order. With asynchronous feature caching, temporal conditioning maintains single-frame-level server-side sampling latency without additional historical-encoding overhead. Overall, TemporalFlow-VLA provides a compact, physically grounded interface for exploiting ordered execution history without explicit motion estimation or geometric processing at deployment.

</details>


### [133] [Active Surface-Driven Reconfigurable Gripper: Robust Grasping and Sequential Manipulation of Thin Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26883)
*Ziyi Zheng,Keqi Zhu,Hao Wu,Yanzhe Wang,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成主动表面与欠驱动柔顺性的新型机器人夹爪，用于薄物体（以书籍为代表）的稳定抓取。通过主动表面拇指实现目标物的手内重定位，欠驱动手指保证与环境柔顺接触，重配置结构适应不同书籍摆放状态。实验验证了该夹爪在平放薄物（书、纸、布料、塑料薄膜、鼠标垫）和竖直排列书籍抓取中的鲁棒性与高成功率，并能可靠完成长序列"抓取-放置"任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人夹爪在抓取和操作薄物体时面临巨大挑战，多数依赖高精度的接近和抓取运动，导致鲁棒性差、适用性有限。本文旨在无需复杂控制的前提下实现对薄物体的稳定抓取。

Method: 首先设计了一种集成主动表面的欠驱动夹爪，主动表面拇指无需调整机械臂或其余手指即可进行手内重定位，欠驱动手指与环境建立柔顺接触，重配置结构保障不同配置下的可靠抓取。其次建立夹爪运动学模型，确定两种典型场景（桌面平放书籍、书架竖直排列书籍）的初始抓取姿态。再次通过分析桌面平放书籍的物理模型及其与夹爪、环境的交互，系统优化结构参数与抓取策略。最后开展大量实验验证有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该夹爪在抓取平放薄物体（包括书、纸、布料、塑料薄膜和鼠标垫）时表现出强鲁棒性和适应性，在抓取竖直排列书籍时具有高成功率，并能可靠地完成长序列"抓取-放置"任务。

Conclusion: 所提出的主动表面与欠驱动柔顺性相集成的夹爪方案，无需复杂控制即可实现薄物体的稳定、鲁棒抓取，显著提升了实际应用中的适应性和可靠性。

Abstract: Robotic grippers face substantial challenges in grasping and manipulating thin objects. Most existing grippers rely on highly precise approach and grasp motions, which limits robustness and reduces applicability. This paper explores thin-object grasping using books as a representative example.
