Here, we propose a novel solution that integrates an active surface with underactuated compliance to achieve stable grasping of thin objects without complex control. First, an underactuated gripper with an active surface is designed. The active-surface thumb performs in-hand repositioning of the target book without requiring adjustments of the robot arm or the other fingers, while the underactuated fingers establish compliant contact conditions with the environment, and the reconfigurable structure enables reliable grasping of books under different configurations. Second, we establish a kinematic model of the gripper, and determine the initial grasp postures for two representative scenarios (books lying flat on a desktop and books vertically packed in a shelf). Third, by analyzing the physical model of a book lying on a table and its interaction with the gripper and the environment, we systematically optimize the structural parameters and grasping strategy. Finally, extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed gripper and strategy. The results demonstrate strong robustness and adaptability when grasping thin objects placed flat (including books, paper, fabric, plastic film, and mouse pad), as well as a high success rate when grasping vertically packed books. Moreover, the proposed gripper can reliably complete long sequential "grasp-place" tasks.

</details>


### [134] [Beyond Shallow-Water Photorealism: Physically and Sensor-Grounded Simulation for Deep-Sea Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26888)
*Michele Grimaldi,Enrico Di Maria,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于物理和传感器模型的Stonefish仿真器扩展，专注于深海环境下的水下仿真，增强了水动力模型、IMU/DVL漂移、磁力计扰动、地形力学、压力驱动环境变化和真实水下光学等模块，以实现实时且更真实的深海无人系统仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下仿真器过度强调视觉逼真度而忽视物理保真度，且主要针对浅水环境，在深水环境中应用有限且计算成本高昂。不准确的传感器和环境模型会在长时间导航和基于学习的自主性研究中引入非物理伪影和过于乐观的性能估计，因此需要更真实的深海物理与传感器仿真。

Method: 在Stonefish仿真器基础上进行物理与传感器接地扩展：补充随机IMU和DVL漂移模型、磁力计扰动、高阶水动力模型、地形力学（terramechanics）、压力驱动的环境变化，以及基于物理的水下光学模型。这些模块在保持实时仿真兼容性的前提下，旨在更准确地捕捉深海AUV、ROV、着陆器、ASV和滑翔机的受力与测量行为。

Result: 所提出的框架能在实时条件下为深海航行、感知和自主性研究提供更贴近实际深水工况的仿真基础，减少了因传感器和环境模型不精确导致的非物理伪影和过于乐观的性能评估问题。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了水下仿真向更具代表性的深水运行条件迈进，为深海导航、感知和自主性研究提供了实用基础；但复杂流固耦合和完整环境随机性建模仍是未来需要解决的挑战。

Abstract: Many recent underwater simulators emphasize visual realism at the expense of physical fidelity, focusing on shallow-water effects with limited relevance in deep-water environments and high computational cost. In this work, we shift the focus toward deep-sea physical and sensor realism. We present a physics- and sensor-grounded extension of the Stonefish simulator that augments its hydrodynamic models with stochastic IMU and DVL drift, magnetometer disturbances, higher-order hydrodynamics, terramechanics, pressure-driven environmental variability, and physically based underwater optics. These additions are designed to better capture the forces and measurements shaping the behavior of deep-ocean AUVs, ROVs, landers, ASVs, and gliders, while remaining compatible with real-time simulation. This work advances underwater simulation toward more representative deep-sea operating conditions, which is particularly relevant for long-duration navigation and learning-based autonomy, where inaccurate sensor and environmental models introduce non-physical artifacts and overly optimistic performance. While challenges remain, including complex fluid-structure interactions and full environmental stochasticity, the proposed framework provides a practical foundation for navigation, perception, and autonomy research under deep-sea conditions.

</details>


### [135] [Contact-Aided Factor-Graph Localization for Underwater Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26932)
*Michele Grimaldi,Yosaku Maeda,Hitoshi Kakami,Ignacio Carlucho,Yvan R. Petillot,Tomoya Inoue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种接触辅助因子图定位框架（Contact-Aided Factor-Graph Localization），将水下机器人吸盘式机械臂的物理接触事件建模为高置信度几何约束，与自适应视觉里程计、目标检测及惯性-DVL传感器紧耦合，用于解决近海底采样时低空视觉退化和DVL漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近海底采样的自主水下机器人在低空作业时，俯瞰相机面对无特征平坦海底产生尺度模糊、横向退化及特征跟踪不一致问题；而惯性-DVL融合缺乏结构漂移修正机制，导致状态估计精度不足。

Method: 构建基于平滑（smoothing-based）的因子图定位框架，主要包括：1) 将吸盘式机械臂接触事件视为高置信度因子，作为隐式闭环约束；2) 视觉里程计相对位姿因子和地标方位-距离因子按内点统计量进行不确定性缩放，防止弱视觉帧破坏估计器稳定性；3) 系统支持在线运动过程中完全初始化。

Result: 在实验水池、港口环境和仿真环境中进行验证，结果表明接触诱导约束相比基于滤波的导航方案和无接触的图优化方案，显著降低轨迹漂移并提高目标物体重访精度。

Conclusion: 物理交互可作为感知退化水下环境中的一种定位基本手段（localization primitive），弥补视觉和导航传感器的不足，为近海底精细作业提供更可靠的定位保障。

Abstract: Accurate state estimation for autonomous underwater vehicles performing close-range seafloor sampling remains challenging. In low-altitude operation, down-looking cameras over featureless planar seabeds produce scale ambiguity, lateral degeneracy, and inconsistent feature tracking. Meanwhile, inertial-Doppler Velocity Log (DVL) fusion alone provides no mechanism for structural drift correction. We propose a Contact-Aided Factor-Graph Localization framework that treats physical interaction as an informative geometric constraint within a smoothing-based localization formulation. The method tightly fuses suction-based manipulator contact events with adaptive visual odometry, learned object detections, and on-board sensors. Visual odometry relative-pose factors and landmark bearing-range factors are uncertainty-scaled according to inlier statistics to prevent visually weak frames from destabilizing the estimator, while contact events are modeled as high-confidence factors that induce implicit loop closures without appearance-based place recognition. Furthermore, the system can fully initialize online during motion. Experimental evaluation in tanks, harbor, and simulation environments demonstrates that contact-induced constraints significantly reduce trajectory drift and improve object revisit accuracy compared to filtering-based navigation and contact-free graph formulations. These results highlight the role of embodied physical interaction as a localization primitive in perception-degraded underwater environments

</details>


### [136] [4DSynth: Controllable Procedural World Synthesis for Dynamic Embodied Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26947)
*Zehao Qi,Haochen Luo,Jia-Wang Bian,Zeyu Ma,Shuyang Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出4DSynth，一个可控的过程式系统，可将自然语言描述、蓝图掩码或单张照片转化为具有显式几何、动画角色、无碰撞轨迹和物理仿真状态的4D环境。同时构建了4DSynth-Nav导航基准，并发现两个视觉语言模型在多数任务上失败，验证了该管线和基准的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需要视觉多样、物理可交互且随时间变化的环境。虽然过程式模拟器可生成大规模交互场景，4D生成器可产生动态视觉，但将两者结合仍需大量人工努力，且结果难以编辑和控制在规模上复用。

Method: 4DSynth通过多种场景输入路径（自然语言、蓝图掩码、单张照片）共享一个基于几何的表示，统一处理动画、相机规划、渲染和任务生成。基于此管线构建4DSynth-Nav导航基准，包含三个难度等级，并用两个视觉语言模型进行评估。

Result: 两个视觉语言模型在4DSynth-Nav的多数任务中失败，且在早期子任务后即停滞。过程式可控性使每个失败都可复现，每个难度轴可独立调节。

Conclusion: 本文同时呈现了可控的生成管线和其使能的可扩展基准，为开发和评估具身智能体提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Embodied agents need environments that are visually diverse, physically interactive, and changing over time. Procedural simulators can generate large interactive scene collections, and recent 4D generators produce compelling visual dynamics. Combining these properties in one environment, however, still demands extensive manual effort, and the result is rarely editable or controllable enough to reuse at scale.
  We present 4DSynth, a controllable procedural system that turns a natural-language description, a blueprint mask, or a single photograph into an editable 4D environment with explicit geometry, animated actors, collision-free trajectories, and physics-ready simulation state. Multiple scene routes share one geometry-grounded representation, so the same pipeline handles animation, camera planning, rendering, and task generation.
  To validate the full pipeline, we construct 4DSynth-Nav, an interactive navigation benchmark generated entirely from 4DSynth's procedural scenes. Two vision-language models evaluated across three difficulty tiers both fail the majority of tasks and stall after early subtasks. The same procedural controllability that produces these environments also makes each failure reproducible and each difficulty axis independently tunable. This paper presents both a controllable generation pipeline and the scalable benchmark it enables, offering a practical foundation for developing and evaluating embodied agents.

</details>


### [137] [Arbitrary-Order Hermite Interpolation of Rigid-Motion Jets via Hyper-Multidual Quaternions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27000)
*Daniel Condurache*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限阶刚体运动喷流的双边插值问题，使用单位对偶四元数表示。提出了阶-n多对偶（MD）代数及其超多对偶（HMD）四元数框架，推导了时间HMD变换的酉性和系数递归约束，将ScLERP扩展到HMD四元数但发现其非完整性问题，并提出基于对数坐标+Hermite插值+指数映射的完整替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体运动的插值在机器人学、计算机动画等领域有广泛应用。现有工作多局限于对偶四元数的一阶运动学（位置+速度），而高阶运动学（加速度、加加速度等）的插值尚未得到系统处理。本文旨在建立统一的代数框架，实现匹配端点及其导数（即"喷流"）的刚体运动插值方法。

Method: 本文方法包括：1）定义阶-n多对偶代数及超多对偶四元数，证明时间HMD变换及其相对描述子是酉的，并推导系数递归约束及容许对数坐标图下的局部可实现性逆命题；2）将ScLERP代数地扩展到单位HMD四元数，给出系数判据及端点与一阶内部接触缺陷的显式表达；3）提出完整替代方案：将端点变换映射到对数对偶四元数坐标，应用匹配至n阶导数的度-(2n+1)Hermite多项式，再通过指数映射提升；4）利用HMD算术恢复高阶刚体运动加速度场，无需显式微分dexp。

Result: 通过二阶（旋转和完整SE(3)）实验及三阶多项式检验，验证了所推导的缺陷量（defects）和端点喷流的正确性。实验证实直接HMD-ScLERP对任意端点喷流一般非完整的，而所提出的Hermite多项式+指数映射方法能够实现完整的插值。

Conclusion: 本文建立了刚体运动高阶喷流插值的完整代数框架。直接HMD-ScLERP虽然匹配完整端点变换但固有地产生非完整性缺陷；基于对数坐标的HMD Hermite插值则提供了完整（holonomic）的替代方案。该框架为刚体运动的高阶插值提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: We study bilateral interpolation of finite-order rigid-motion jets represented by unit dual quaternions. An order-$n$ multidual (MD) algebra is the truncated polynomial algebra $\mathbb{R}[\varepsilon]/(\varepsilon^{n+1})$; hyper-multidual (HMD) quaternions are dual quaternions with coefficients in this algebra. Temporal HMD transforms encode a pose and its derivatives, whereas a generic HMD curve need not be the temporal jet of its pose projection; we call this requirement holonomicity. We show that a temporal transform and its relative descriptor are unitary and derive recursive coefficient constraints, together with a local realizability converse in an admissible logarithm chart. We then extend screw linear interpolation (ScLERP) algebraically to unit HMD quaternions. Although it matches complete endpoint transforms, direct HMD--ScLERP is generically non-holonomic for arbitrary endpoint jets. We give a coefficient criterion and explicit endpoint and first-order interior contact defects. A holonomic alternative is obtained by mapping endpoint transforms to logarithmic dual-quaternion coordinates, applying the degree-$(2n+1)$ Hermite polynomial that matches derivatives through order $n$, and lifting by the exponential. HMD arithmetic also recovers higher-order rigid-motion acceleration fields without explicit differentiation of $\mathrm{dexp}$. Rotation and full $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ tests through second order, with an additional third-order polynomial check, reproduce the stated defects and endpoint jets.

</details>


### [138] [Riemann-1.0: An Embodied World Action Model for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27033)
*Haofeng Sun,Jiangbo Pei,Fei Kang,Zexiang Liu,Yaokun Li,Boyi Jiang,Hua Xue,Cindy Zhou,Wei Li,Yichen Wei,Mengyin An,Fanliang Zhao,Biao Jiang,Zile Wang,Yang Liu,Yangguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Riemann-1.0，一个基于完全因果自回归的"世界行动模型"（World Action Model），将多视角视觉观测、机器人状态和具身动作统一建模为一个因果自回归序列，同时实现在线策略执行和行动条件的世界模拟。通过基于20万小时交互数据的渐进式具身预训练框架，Riemann-1.0在仿真和真实世界操作任务上均达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界行动模型（WAMs）通常采用联合生成、视频优先预测或解耦建模等范式，无法在单一模型内统一"策略执行"与"世界模拟"两大功能，限制了具身智能体从大规模异质数据中获取可泛化的操作能力。需要一个统一框架来桥接在线策略执行与条件化世界模拟。

Method: Riemann-1.0采用三重设计：(1) 完全因果自回归架构，将多视角视觉、机器人状态和动作统一编码为因果状态转换序列；(2) 统一的World Action Modeling目标，使模型同时充当可执行策略和多具身视觉世界模拟器；(3) 渐进式具身预训练框架，通过统一的建模目标逐步整合来自第一视角人类视频、手持夹爪演示和异质机器人轨迹的经验，最终将大规模具身经验迁移为可执行的操作能力。

Result: 在RoboTwin2.0上成功率达94.3%，LIBERO上99.0%，RoboCasa-365上62.6%（超越此前最佳方法8.4%）；在长时程真实世界操作任务中SR达85.0%、PSR达94.4%，较最强开源基线提升15%的SR。

Conclusion: 统一的世界行动建模结合渐进式具身预训练，能够有效地将大规模具身经验转化为可泛化的机器人操作能力，验证了单一模型同时支撑策略执行与世界模拟的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce Riemann-1.0, a fully causal autoregressive World Action Model for embodied intelligence. Riemann-1.0 jointly models multi-view visual observations, robot states, and embodiment-specific actions within a unified causal autoregressive sequence, representing robot actions and world evolution as causal state transitions. Unlike existing WAMs based on joint generation, video-first prediction, or decoupled modeling paradigms, Riemann-1.0 unifies online robot policy execution and action-conditioned world simulation within a single model, enabling it to function as both an executable robot policy and a multi-embodiment visual world simulator. To scale embodied experience across heterogeneous data sources, we further develop a progressive embodied pretraining framework that unifies learning from egocentric human videos, handheld-gripper demonstrations, and heterogeneous robot trajectories under a shared World Action Modeling objective. Built upon 200K+ hours of interaction data, Riemann-1.0 progressively transfers large-scale embodied experience into executable robot manipulation capabilities. Riemann-1.0 achieves state-of-the-art performance across both simulation benchmarks and real-world manipulation tasks. It achieves success rates of 94.3% on RoboTwin2.0, 99.0% on LIBERO, and 62.6% on the long-horizon compositional benchmark RoboCasa-365, outperforming the previous best method by 8.4% On long-horizon real-world manipulation tasks, Riemann-1.0 achieves a Success Rate (SR) of 85.0% and a Progress Success Rate (PSR) of 94.4%, exceeding the strongest open-source baseline by 15% in SR. These results demonstrate that unified World Action Modeling together with progressive embodied pretraining effectively transforms large-scale embodied experience into generalizable robot manipulation capabilities.

</details>


### [139] [GRAFT: Grounded and Efficient Online Reinforcement Adaptation for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27079)
*Yibo Qiu,Haoliang Ye,Shu'ang Sun,Zan Huang,Ronald X Xu,Mingzhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAFT框架，通过区域级监督学习视图特定的视觉锚点，并利用单步动作生成和缓存式视觉-语言前缀复用，高效地将预训练VLA策略在线适配到精细生物医学操作任务，在四个任务上将成功率提升25个百分点并降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽为机器人操作提供强先验，但在线适配到精细生物医学任务仍具挑战：任务成功依赖细微且视角相关的视觉线索，而任务级奖励难以提供关于哪些区域重要的指导，限制了从有限真实机器人交互中学习任务相关的视觉 grounding。此外，VLA推理和基于回放的更新的计算成本进一步约束了在线适配。

Method: GRAFT框架包含三个核心组件：（1）利用区域级监督学习视图特定的视觉锚点（visual anchors），使感知聚焦于任务相关局部线索；（2）部署时无需区域提议（region proposals）；（3）结合单步动作生成与缓存的视觉-语言前缀复用（cached visual-language prefix reuse）以加速在线学习。

Result: 在四个生物医学操作任务上，在相同的适配预算下，GRAFT将成功率提升了25个百分点，同时降低了在线策略更新的计算开销。

Conclusion: GRAFT通过grounded perception机制有效解决了VLA策略在精细生物医学任务中在线适配的视觉grounding难题，兼顾了任务性能和计算效率，验证了区域级监督在VLA在线适配中的有效性。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies provide strong priors for robot manipulation, yet adapting them online to fine-grained biomedical tasks remains challenging. Task success often hinges on subtle, view-dependent visual cues, while task-level rewards provide little guidance about which regions matter, making it difficult to learn task-relevant visual grounding from limited real-robot interaction. Online adaptation is further constrained by the computational cost of VLA inference and replay-based updates. We introduce GRAFT (Grounded Reinforcement Adaptation for Fast Task Learning), a framework for efficient online VLA adaptation through grounded perception. GRAFT uses region-level supervision to learn view-specific visual anchors that focus perception on task-relevant local cues without requiring region proposals at deployment. It further combines single-step action generation with cached visual-language prefix reuse to accelerate online learning. Across four biomedical manipulation tasks, GRAFT improves success rates by 25 percentage points under matched adaptation budgets, while reducing the computational overhead of online policy updates.

</details>


### [140] [Pass the Bucket: Efficient, Robust, Local Load Balancing for Teams of Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27085)
*Tobias Wallner,Dominik Krupke,Arne Schmidt,Sándor P. Fekete*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究异构机器人团队的分布式、自组织任务共享问题，提出基于"桶队"机制的负载均衡方法。通过引入一种令牌（token）局部稳定机制来消除系统中的持续振荡，实现按机器人速度比例的区间划分，并通过事件驱动仿真验证了系统的收敛性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构机器人团队的分布式协作中（如运输任务），需要一种无需中央控制或全局信息的负载均衡机制。简单的桶队机制虽然有效，但可能产生混沌行为即持续振荡，影响系统稳定性和吞吐量。因此需要设计一种纯局部的稳定化机制来消除振荡并加速收敛。

Method: 采用桶队机制（bucket brigades）进行负载均衡，即异构机器人团队在一维受限空间中共享空间任务，仅依靠与邻居或墙壁的碰撞感知。针对可能的混沌行为，提出基于令牌（token）的稳定机制：令牌在遭遇后暂时使机器人减速，该纯局部改动可消除持续振荡。比较了单边界令牌与双向令牌两种方案，并优化减速因子以加速收敛。采用事件驱动仿真评估收敛时间和鲁棒性。

Result: 事件驱动仿真结果表明，系统在多种扰动（如机器人删除、位置或速度抖动）下均能可靠地重新收敛。令牌机制有效消除了持续振荡，系统收敛至稳定状态。双向令牌可加速收敛，优化减速因子进一步提升了系统性能。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一种基于令牌的局部、实用机制，能够实现异构机器人团队的鲁棒负载均衡，为更复杂场景提供了有效的基础工具。该系统无需中央控制或全局信息即可优化整体吞吐量，并按机器人速度比例实现区间划分。

Abstract: We study the problem of decentralized, self-organized task sharing for a swarm of heterogeneous robots that collaborate in transportation or other objectives that require coordinated motion planning. To this end, we present theoretical and practical results for the simple but effective mechanism of \emph{bucket brigades} for load balancing, in which a team of heterogenous robots share a spatial task in a confined, one-dimensional space, while only being able to sense collisions with neighbors or walls. The goal is to optimize throughput of the overall system, without central control or information, aiming at an interval partition proportional to robot velocities. We address possible chaotic system behavior by developing a stabilization mechanism based on simple local aid, a ``token'', that temporarily decelerates robots after an encounter. This purely local change eliminates persistent oscillations, resulting in convergence towards a stable system state. We accelerate system convergence by comparing a single boundary token to ubiquitous two-directional tokens and optimizing the deceleration factor. Event-driven simulations report convergence times and robustness: For a large variety of perturbations (such as robot deletion, position or velocity jittering), the system reliably re-converges. The results suggest a local, practical mechanism for robust load balancing for heterogeneous teams of robots that promises an effective tool as basis for more complex scenarios.

</details>


### [141] [Active sensing to characterize the heterogeneity of plant stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27088)
*Ayman Laaroussi,Peter Hanappe,David Colliaux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自主机器人平台，通过整合3D植物重建、几何分析和运动规划，实现对植物叶片的靶向叶绿素荧光测量，突破传统被动成像的表型分析局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数表型平台依赖基于图像的被动测量，而先进的植物表征需要整合主动生理传感模态（如叶绿素荧光），但目前缺乏能够自动化执行这类测量的机器人系统。

Method: 系统首先从多视角数据重建密集3D植物模型，基于朝向、可达性和传感约束提取候选叶片表面；随后将目标整合进任务级规划框架，引导机械臂末端执行器到达精确的接触或近接触构型，以完成点式荧光采集。

Result: 该平台实现了自动化、可重复且空间分辨的生理测量，超越了被动成像的能力，并展示了感知、几何推理与操控三者紧密耦合的有效性。

Conclusion: 该系统为高分辨率植物表型分析提供了一种机器人驱动的方法，为自主农业检测和植物感知操控开辟了新方向。

Abstract: While most phenotyping platforms rely primarily on image-based measurements, advanced plant characterization requires the integration of active physiological sensing modali- ties such as chlorophyll fluorescence. We present an autonomous robotic platform designed to perform targeted fluorescence measurements on plant leaves. The system combines 3D plant reconstruction, geometric analysis, and motion planning to localize suitable measurement points and generate collision-free trajectories for a robotic manipulator.
  A dense 3D model of the plant is reconstructed from multi-view data and used to extract candidate leaf surfaces based on orientation, accessibility, and sensing constraints. These targets are then integrated into a task-level planning framework that guides the end-effector to precise contact or near-contact configurations required for point-based fluorescence acquisition. The platform enables automated, repeatable, and spatially resolved physiological measurements that go beyond passive imaging. By tightly coupling perception, geometric reasoning, and manipulation, the proposed system provides a robotics-driven approach to high-resolution plant phenotyping and opens new directions for autonomous agricultural inspection and plant-aware manipulation.

</details>


### [142] [Planning a Shared Modular Fixture Layout Across Robotic Disassembly Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27151)
*Haohui Pan,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化真空吸附夹具系统，通过去噪扩散概率模型生成物理信息初始布局并用贝叶斯优化精调，为螺丝刀和剃须刀的完整拆解序列规划单一共用支撑布局，避免拆解过程中的夹具重构，实验验证了其可行性与稳定性（平均稳定裕度66.9%和81.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人拆解不规则形状产品的过程中，随着组件逐步移除，支撑表面、质量分布和任务载荷持续变化，针对某一工件状态设计的夹具布局可能在后续阶段失效。因此需要在整个拆解序列上规划统一的支撑方案，以避免夹具重构带来的不稳定问题。

Method: 提出一种模块化真空吸附夹具系统，为完整拆解序列规划单一共用支撑布局。在混合连续-离散布局空间中搜索时，采用去噪扩散概率模型生成物理信息引导的初始配置，再通过贝叶斯优化进行精调，以应对跨阶段重复评估的计算挑战。

Result: 机器人螺丝及组件移除实验验证了规划布局的拆解可行性；11项定向载荷测试量化了稳定性，测得螺丝刀平均经验稳定裕度为66.9%，剃须刀为81.6%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，针对特定产品设计的共用布局能够在整个测试的机器人拆解序列中提供稳定支撑，证明该方法有效解决了不规则产品拆解中的动态支撑难题。

Abstract: Stable support remains challenging in robotic disassembly of irregularly shaped products. As components are progressively removed, the available support surfaces, mass distribution, and task loads change throughout the process. A fixture layout designed for one workpiece state may therefore become infeasible at later stages, motivating unified support planning over the complete disassembly sequence. This paper presents a modular vacuum-based fixturing system that plans one shared support configuration for the complete disassembly sequence of a screwdriver or shaver, allowing each sequence to proceed without fixture reconfiguration. To search the mixed continuous--discrete layout space under repeated cross-stage evaluation, a denoising diffusion probabilistic model generates physics-informed initial configurations that are refined through Bayesian optimization. Robotic screw and component-removal experiments verified the disassembly feasibility of the planned layouts, while 11 directional-load tests quantified their stability. Comparisons between the measured operational loads and directional responses yielded mean empirical stability margins of 66.9% for the screwdriver and 81.6% for the shaver. These results demonstrate that a product-specific shared layout can provide stable support throughout the tested robotic disassembly sequence.

</details>


### [143] [Task-space model-based control of pneumatic soft actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27186)
*Nithin S. Kumar,Joshua Gaston,D. Caleb Rucker,Eric J. Barth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非最小坐标离散弹性杆模型的实时动态模型任务空间反馈与估计框架，用于平面气动软体执行器的精确闭环控制。该框架采用绝对坐标与完整约束，结合稀疏系统矩阵实现计算效率，并通过准静态前馈逆模型、任务空间PI控制器和动态观测器实现全状态估计。在三种不同几何形状的平面气动软体执行器上验证，精度运动RMSE为1.5-2.3 mm，中等带宽运动RMSE为5.5-12.4 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体执行器虽能实现灵巧且柔顺的交互，但其强非线性、分布式变形和动力学不确定性使得闭环任务空间控制极具挑战性。现有方法难以同时兼顾分布式力学的精确建模与实时计算效率，亟需一种既保持结构保真又能满足实时性要求的动态模型驱动控制框架。

Method: 提出基于非最小坐标离散弹性杆模型（绝对坐标+完整约束）的实时动态模型控制框架。核心包括：(1) 保持分布式力学结构的稀疏系统矩阵计算方案，支持多达10段离散杆的实时控制；(2) 准静态前馈逆模型与任务空间PI控制器相结合；(3) 将测量残差作为虚拟力的动态观测器，实现基于稀疏传感的全状态估计。

Result: 在三种不同几何形状的平面气动软体执行器上完成五项实验验证：工作空间内绘制0-9数字（尖端速度3-18 mm/s）、周期运动跟踪（最高37 cm/s）、跨平台泛化、降采样传感条件和实时用户定义参考轨迹。精度运动RMSE为1.5-2.3 mm，1-2 Hz中等带宽运动RMSE为5.5-12.4 mm。

Conclusion: 结构化的非最小动态模型能够实现平面软体气动执行器在自由空间中的实时、高精度、中等带宽任务空间控制，证明了在软体机器人控制中保留分布式力学结构而非过度简化模型的有效性。

Abstract: Soft actuators enable dexterous and compliant interaction, but closed-loop task-space control remains challenging due to strong nonlinearities, distributed deformation, and uncertainty in their dynamics. This paper presents a real-time dynamic-model-based task-space feedback and estimation framework based on a non-minimal coordinate discrete elastic rod model formulated in absolute coordinates with holonomic constraints. The resulting structure preserves distributed mechanics while maintaining computational efficiency through sparse system matrices, enabling real-time control with up to 10 discretized rods. A quasi-static feedforward inverse model is combined with a task-space PI controller and a dynamic observer that fuses measurement residuals as virtual forces, enabling full-state estimation from sparse sensing. The approach is experimentally validated on three planar pneumatic soft actuators with varying geometries. Across five tasks, including drawing the digits 0-9 across the workspace (3-18 mm/s tip speed), tracking periodic motion (up to 37 cm/s), cross-platform generalization, reduced sensing conditions, and real-time user-defined references, our method achieves 1.5-2.3 mm root mean square error (RMSE) for precision motions and 5.5-12.4 mm RMSE at 1-2 Hz. Results demonstrate that structured, non-minimal dynamic models can enable real-time, high-precision, moderate-bandwidth task-space control of planar soft pneumatic actuators in free space.

</details>


### [144] [Tensegrity Continuum Robots Enable Task-Adaptive Morphologies for Cooperative Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27221)
*Mahmud Hasan Saikot,Sydney Spiegel,Sudheera Akalanka Kariyawasam,Andrew Stefka,Josh Chrisler,Jianguo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合张拉整体结构与爪式连接机制的新型模块化可重构机器人（MRR），同时兼具连续体机器人的结构柔顺性和模块化机器人的自重构能力，可独立操作/运动，并能自主重组为链、环、分支等多种形态以完成协作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模块化可重构机器人大多依赖刚性模块，缺乏结构柔顺性，限制了其适应能力；而连续体机器人虽然具备柔顺性，却无法自重构为适应任务的多机器人配置。这两类架构的各自局限性促使研究者探索一种统一两种优势的新方案。

Method: 将张拉整体（tensegrity）结构作为柔顺躯干，结合爪式（claw-based）连接机制作为对接单元。每个机器人单元既能独立进行操纵和运动，又能通过爪式机构实现多机器人之间的自重构，形成链、环、分支等多种拓扑形态，以适应不同的协作操纵和运动任务。

Result: 实验验证了该机器人在多种任务和环境中的能力，包括协调物体操纵与运输、多模态运动（multimodal locomotion），以及真实场景中的移动操纵（loco-manipulation）任务。

Conclusion: 该工作为适应性多功能机器人集群奠定了基础，在制造业、太空探索和搜救等领域具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Robots that can change their morphologies and behaviors for different tasks and environments hold great promise for adaptable, multifunctional systems. Modular reconfigurable robots (MRRs) can achieve such functionalities by docking and rearranging individual units, but most rely on rigid modules that lack structural compliance, resulting in limited capabilities. Continuum robots offer compliance through flexible backbones, yet they cannot self-reconfigure into task-adaptive multi-robot configurations. Here, we introduce an MRR that unifies the advantages of both architectures by combining a tensegrity-based compliant body with claw-based connection mechanisms. Each robot can manipulate and locomote independently, and multiple robots can self-reconfigure into different morphologies (e.g., chains, loops, branches) for cooperative manipulation and locomotion. We demonstrate the robots' capability across diverse tasks and environments, including coordinated object manipulation and transport, multimodal locomotion, and loco-manipulation in real-world scenarios. These results lay a foundation for adaptable and multifunctional robotic collectives, with broad potential applications in manufacturing, space exploration, and search-and-rescue operations.

</details>


### [145] [STEP: State-Aware Task Estimation and Planning with Multi-Modal LLMs for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27225)
*Maitrey Gramopadhye,Prakash Baskaran,Xiao Liu,Songpo Li,Soshi Iba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对工业人机协作中MM-LLM缺乏系统状态理解和状态转换跟踪导致幻觉动作的问题，提出状态感知的任务估计与规划器（STEP），通过显式估计系统状态并预测动作引发的状态转换来实现任务收敛性规划，在模拟装配任务中相比SOTA在动作可执行性上提升32.8%，最终状态误差降低14.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MM-LLM在数据稀缺的工业场景中通过上下文学习进行任务规划，但存在两个根本缺陷：一是缺乏对系统状态的感知和状态转换的跟踪，导致产生偏离目标的幻觉动作；二是自然语言生成的动作计划局限于高层描述，在动作执行时引入模糊性。

Method: 提出STEP框架，核心思想是提示MM-LLM显式估计系统当前状态，并预测执行动作后产生的状态转换（即前瞻未来状态）。通过在预测动作的同时预测未来状态，STEP既保证了任务收敛性规划，又能提供执行预测动作所需的额外辅助参数。

Result: 在模拟环境中的机器人装配任务上进行评估，STEP相比最先进方法在动作可执行性上提升32.8%，在最终状态误差上降低14.8%。

Conclusion: 通过显式的状态估计与状态转换预测，可以有效缓解MM-LLM在任务规划中的幻觉问题，提升规划的动作可执行性和最终状态准确性，为工业人机协作中的长时程任务规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Effective human-robot collaboration in industrial settings requires robots to understand human intentions and assist with task planning, reducing workload. Recent works have explored the use of Multi-modal Large Language Models (MM-LLMs) for task planning in such data-scarce scenarios, leveraging in-context learning to interpret user actions and generate long-horizon action plans in natural language. However, MM-LLMs inherently lack an understanding of system states and do not track state transitions, often leading to hallucinated actions that deviate from the intended goal. Additionally, generating action plans in natural language tends to limit the generated plans to a high level, introducing ambiguity in action execution. To address these limitations, we propose the State-aware Task Estimator and Planner (STEP), which prompts a MM-LLM to explicitly estimate the state of the system and predict the state transitions resulting from executed actions. By forecasting future states alongside actions, STEP ensures task-convergent planning while also providing additional assistance parameters necessary for executing the predicted actions. We evaluate STEP in a simulated environment using a robot assembly task. Our approach outperforms the state-of-the-art by 32.8% in action executability and 14.8% in final-state error.

</details>


### [146] [Embodied Scene Rearrangement Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27371)
*Canzhi Chen,Zan Wang,Siqi Zhu,Qi Wu,Yixuan Li,Wei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出具身场景重排规划（ESRP）新任务，要求智能体仅依靠自我中心观察和俯视目标布局，将3D场景中的家具重排至目标配置，并构建了基于OmniGibson的ESRP-Bench基准（5400+场景对、8200+物体）及多级评估指标和四种基线方法，实验表明当前方法难以高效完成该任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排任务允许全局状态访问，但真实机器人应用中缺乏此类全局感知，且存在物体相互遮挡问题。为使重排任务更贴近现实部署约束，需要提出一个仅依赖局部自我中心观察与全局目标布局对齐的长时程规划任务。

Method: 论文定义了ESRP新任务：智能体仅通过自我中心观测将场景家具重排至目标配置；基于OmniGibson构建含5400+场景对和8200+物体的ESRP-Bench基准；设计三个多层级指标评估重排质量；提供四种基线方法，包括层次化任务与运动规划方法、基于视觉语言模型的方法、以及模仿学习和强化学习两种学习式方法。

Result: 实验表明现有四种基线方法均难以高效完成该任务，在长时程规划与部分观测对齐全局目标布局上表现有限，凸显了ESRP作为具身智能场景理解与长时程规划挑战性前沿任务的特性。

Conclusion: ESRP为具身智能体在真实世界场景中的家具重排部署提供了有效评估平台，当前方法性能不足表明该任务仍具挑战性，本工作为未来智能代理在实际场景中的落地应用奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces Embodied Scene Rearrangement Planning (ESRP), a novel task requiring embodied agents to rearrange furniture in 3D scenes to match a target configuration using only egocentric observations and a top-down target layout. Unlike prior rearrangement tasks, ESRP precludes global state access and introduces mutual object occlusions, reflecting the practical constraints of real-world robotic deployment. These factors make aligning partial egocentric observations with the global target layout particularly challenging for long-horizon planning. To facilitate research, we present ESRP-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark built on OmniGibson featuring over 5,400 scene pairs and 8,200 objects. We define three multi-level metrics to evaluate rearrangement quality and provide four baselines: a hierarchical task-and-motion planning method, a vision-language-model-based method, and two learning-based approaches (IL and RL). Experimental results demonstrate that current methods struggle to complete the task efficiently, highlighting ESRP as a challenging frontier for embodied agents in scene understanding and long-horizon task planning. This work serves as a stepping stone toward deploying intelligent agents in real-world scenarios. Project page: https://pie-lab.cn/ESRP/.

</details>


### [147] [FlashVLA: Streaming Action Decoding for Fast and Asynchronous VLA Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27384)
*Zekai Li,Jiaming Tang,Zhijian Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlashVLA是一个流式动作解码框架，通过维护不同噪声水平的多个动作块并使用块级因果注意力解码，在统一框架中同时解决VLA模型的高推理延迟和异步执行不稳定问题，实现单GPU上≥30Hz控制频率的平滑异步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作中表现优异，但实际部署受限于高推理延迟和异步执行不稳定。特别是基于流匹配的VLA模型，动作解码需要多步迭代且依赖VLM上下文。现有高效推理方法要么提升控制频率，要么减少执行空闲时间，但难以同时实现低延迟推理和准确、时间一致的异步执行。

Method: FlashVLA采用统一的流式动作解码框架：维护一个包含多个不同噪声水平动作块的流式动作缓冲区，使用块级因果注意力（chunk-wise causal attention）进行解码。该设计使每个推理步骤能生成一个可执行的动作块，同时其块级自回归公式隐含地保持了动作连续性，无需额外未来状态条件即可实现平滑的异步执行。

Result: 在大量仿真和真实世界实验中，FlashVLA显著提升推理速度的同时保持了强大的任务性能。在单GPU上可实现≥30Hz的控制频率，并在真实部署中实现平滑的异步推理。

Conclusion: FlashVLA通过统一框架同时解决了VLA模型推理延迟和异步执行两个挑战，实现了高控制频率与平滑异步执行的兼顾，为VLA模型在实际机器人部署中的实用化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly promising for robotic manipulation, yet their real-world deployment remains bottlenecked by high inference latency and unstable asynchronous execution. This challenge is particularly pronounced in flow-matching-based VLA models, where action decoding requires multiple iterative steps conditioned on the VLM context. While efficient inference methods improve control frequency and asynchronous methods reduce execution idle time, existing approaches often fail to jointly achieve low-latency inference and accurate, temporally consistent asynchronous execution. We introduce \textbf{FlashVLA}, a streaming action decoding framework that addresses both challenges in a unified formulation. FlashVLA maintains a streaming action buffer with multiple chunks at different noise levels and decodes them using chunk-wise causal attention. This design allows FlashVLA to produce one executable action chunk per inference step. Moreover, its chunk-wise autoregressive formulation implicitly preserves action continuity, enabling smooth asynchronous execution without extra future-state conditioning. Across extensive simulated and real-world experiments, FlashVLA substantially improves inference speed while maintaining strong task performance. It can achieve $\geq$30\,Hz control frequency on a single GPU with smooth asynchronous inference in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [148] [CLAP: Cross-Embodiment Video World Models are Zero-Shot Physical Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27406)
*Kechen Liu,Ola Shorinwa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLAP是一个跨具身（cross-embodiment）动作条件视频生成框架，能够利用互联网规模的异构视频数据（涵盖人类和多种机器人）进行训练。它通过端末执行器位姿、语言指令和潜在动作来统一不同动作空间，并引入基于课程学习的跨具身训练范式，先在大规模未标注视频上学习物理先验，再迁移到端末执行器动作空间实现零样本部署。实验表明其在DROID等挑战性环境中达到或超越单具身SOTA，并通过少样本适应进一步放大优势，发布了目前最全面的动作条件视频世界模型套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA动作条件视频模型通常局限于单一机器人具身，无法利用大规模异构视频数据中蕴含的丰富通用物理信号。跨具身学习的关键障碍在于不同机器人平台的动作表示差异巨大，且人类视频中通常缺乏动作标注。

Method: CLAP通过三个核心贡献解决跨具身学习挑战：(1) 使用端末执行器位姿、语言指令和潜在动作三种方式调和不同动作空间；(2) 提出基于课程学习的跨具身训练范式——先在无标注视频数据上用潜在动作学习基础物理先验，再将其锚定到端末执行器动作空间以实现零样本部署；(3) 通过少样本适应进一步放大单具身性能优势。

Result: CLAP在DROID等挑战性环境中达到或超越单具身SOTA视频模型的性能；通过少样本适应，性能优势进一步累积放大；最终发布了跨越多种动作条件空间（端末执行器、语言、潜在）和多种机器人形态（跨具身、DROID、Bridge、双臂YAM机器人、G1人形机器人）的最全面的动作条件视频世界模型套件。

Conclusion: CLAP验证了基于潜在动作从大规模异构视频中学习通用物理先验、再迁移到具体动作空间的跨具身训练范式的有效性，建立了训练单具身视频世界模型的新范式，并开源所有代码和模型。

Abstract: State-of-the-art action-conditioned video models are typically restricted to a single robot embodiment, preventing them from leveraging the vast corpus of heterogeneous video data that contains rich signals for learning generalizable physics. To bridge this gap, we introduce CLAP, a framework for cross-embodiment action-conditioned video generation capable of being trained on diverse, internet-scale videos across human and robotic agents. CLAP is grounded in the insight that universal physical laws govern spatiotemporal dynamics regardless of the actor. However, cross-embodiment learning is non-trivial because action representations vary sharply across robot platforms and are typically absent in human videos. CLAP addresses this fundamental challenge through the following core contributions. First, CLAP reconciles disparate action spaces using end-effector poses, language instructions, and latent actions. Second, to resolve their individual limitations, CLAP introduces a curriculum-based cross-embodiment learning recipe that first learns foundational physical priors across unlabeled video data using latent actions and subsequently grounds them in end-effector action spaces for zero-shot deployment to real-world tasks. Crucially, CLAP approaches or surpasses state-of-the-art single-embodiment video models in challenging environments like DROID. These performance advantages compound via few-shot adaptation to establish a novel paradigm for training single-embodiment video world models. Ultimately, CLAP delivers the most comprehensive suite of action-conditioned video world models to date - spanning diverse action-conditioning spaces (end-effector, language, and latent) and robot morphologies (including cross-embodiment, DROID, Bridge, bimanual YAM robots, and G1 humanoids). We open-source all code and models. Project Website at https://omni-clap.github.io .

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Reservoir: A Large-Scale Simulated Dataset for Training and Evaluating Epidemiological Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27408)
*Carson Dudley,Reiden Magdaleno,Marisa Eisenberg*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Reservoir，一个大规模开源流行病模拟器与数据集，包含50万个暴发轨迹、10亿模拟天，每个轨迹带有完整的地面真值标签（如真实感染数、时变再生数和反事实干预效果），为流行病学AI模型训练和验证提供了真实世界数据无法达到的规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病流行病学正越来越多地采用AI方法进行预测、监测和暴发分析，但可用于训练的时间序列数据规模比其他领域（如蛋白质结构预测和自然语言处理）小几个数量级。由于真实世界流行病学数据的范围无法实际扩大到训练大型AI模型所需的规模，模拟数据成为一种可行的替代方案。

Method: Reservoir由一个随机模拟器生成，该模拟器包含真实的噪声和报告伪影，以及具有可配置时间、依从性和年龄依赖性效力的干预措施。数据集覆盖多样化的病原特征、人口结构和干预制度，包含50万个暴发轨迹，共10亿模拟天。每个轨迹都携带完整的地面真值标签，包括无法在真实暴发中直接测量的量（真实感染数、时变再生数和反事实干预效果）。

Result: 当前版本包含50万个暴发轨迹，跨越10亿个模拟天，覆盖多样化的病原特征、人口结构和干预制度。Reservoir支持反事实实验、监测设计研究以及流行病模型的训练，其规模是真实世界数据集无法提供的。

Conclusion: Reservoir填补了流行病学AI训练数据规模不足的空白，通过大规模模拟数据为流行病学模型提供可训练、可验证的数据基础，支持反事实分析、监测设计和大型流行病模型的训练。

Abstract: Large-scale, standardized datasets have driven many advances in AI-based scientific modeling, from protein structure prediction to natural language processing. Infectious disease epidemiology is increasingly adopting AI methods for forecasting, surveillance, and outbreak analytics, but the time-series data available to train them remains orders of magnitude smaller than the corpora behind the advances seen in other fields. Because the scope of real-world epidemiological data cannot practically reach the scale needed to train truly large-scale AI methods, simulated data provides a possible alternative. Here we introduce Reservoir, a large open simulator and dataset of realistic epidemic simulations in which every trajectory carries complete ground-truth labels, including quantities that cannot be measured directly in a real outbreak, such as true infection counts, time-varying reproduction numbers, and counterfactual intervention effects. Reservoir is generated by a stochastic simulator with realistic noise and reporting artifacts, together with interventions with configurable timing, compliance, and age-dependent efficacy. The current release contains 500,000 outbreak trajectories spanning one billion simulated days across diverse pathogen characteristics, population structures, and intervention regimes. Reservoir enables counterfactual experiments, surveillance-design studies, and training of epidemic models at a scale real-world datasets cannot provide.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Beyond Participant-Level Cross-Validation: Reliable Inference for Longitudinal Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26205)
*Shahran Rahman Alve*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 纵向感测研究中，由于记录数多而独立参与者少，传统的窗口级推断容易因伪重复、划分选择和分析流程灵活性而产生虚假的显著结果。本文提出一种参与者级别的分析匹配置换检验方法，通过在置换参与者标签后重新运行完整分析流程来获得匹配的零分布，有效控制I类错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向感测研究通常从几十名参与者中收集数千条窗口记录，记录数量多但独立科学单元少。当结果按参与者定义时，窗口级推断会产生伪重复问题，同时分区选择和分析管道比较的灵活性也会导致虚假的精确性。现有方法（如按参与者分割）无法校准标签依赖的工作流（折构建、预处理、调参、校准和候选选择），因此需要一种参与者级别的有效推断框架。

Method: 本文定义参与者级别的目标估计量（estimand），通过置换参与者标签并重新运行完整分析工作流（包括折构建、预处理、调参、校准和候选选择）来构建分析匹配的零分布。在模拟中比较了窗口级推断、窗口自举、参与者自举和分析匹配置换检验的性能；在真实数据上应用于手腕活动记录（n=55）和智能手机感测（n=38）两个公共队列。

Result: 模拟显示：无效应时，窗口级推断在70-80%的重复中拒绝零假设，窗口自举以相同比率拒绝，参与者自举以10-17%拒绝，而分析匹配检验在20到80名参与者的队列中保持0.025-0.100的错误率。若冻结所选管道而非重复搜索，I类错误膨胀至0.240（8个候选），而重复搜索保持0.040。应用结果：手腕活动记录（n=55）参与者AUROC为0.928，p=0.0050，且在稳健统计下仍显著（p=0.0089）；智能手机感测（n=38，7个阳性）AUROC为0.636，p=0.1724，不拒绝零假设。

Conclusion: 实际原则很明确：每个读取标签的步骤都必须包含在置换分析内部，重复记录不会产生额外的独立参与者。分析匹配的参与者级置换检验能够有效控制纵向感测研究中的伪重复和流程灵活性导致的I类错误膨胀。

Abstract: Longitudinal sensing studies routinely collect thousands of windows from a few dozen participants. The records are numerous; the independent scientific units are not. When the outcome is defined per participant, this mismatch makes apparently precise findings vulnerable to pseudo-replication, to partition choice, and to the ordinary analytic flexibility of comparing several pipelines before reporting one. Splitting on participants prevents a person's records from straddling a split, but it does not calibrate the label-dependent workflow fold construction, preprocessing, tuning, calibration, and candidate selection that produced the reported number. We define a participant-level estimand and obtain an analysis-matched null by permuting participant labels and rerunning that entire workflow. In controlled simulation, window-level inference rejects in 70-80% of replicates when no effect exists and a window bootstrap rejects at the same rate; a participant bootstrap still rejects at 10-17%; the analysis-matched test holds 0.025-0.100 across cohorts of 20 to 80 participants. Freezing the selected pipeline instead of repeating the search inflates Type-I error to 0.240 with eight candidates, where repeating it holds 0.040. Applied to two public cohorts, wrist actigraphy (n=55) yields participant AUROC 0.928 with p=0.0050, a conclusion that persists under a scale-robust rank-pooled statistic and under a matched permutation null computed after excluding hospitalized participants (p=0.0089). Smartphone sensing (n=38, 7 positives) yields 0.636 and does not reject (p=0.1724) despite sufficient resolution, with sensitivity 0.143. The practical rule is narrow: every step that reads labels belongs inside the permuted analysis, and repeated records do not create additional independent participants.

</details>


### [151] [A causal graph-informed temporal convolution architecture for interpretable retail electricity price forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26234)
*Yufan Ji,Abdollah Shafieezadeh,Noah Dormady*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出CG-TCN（因果图信息时间卷积网络），将学习到的因果图嵌入到TCN中，用于零售电价预测。在俄亥俄州十年日度数据上，该模型在1步、10步和15步预测中分别达到3.08%、3.82%和5.43%的MAPE，优于基准模型，同时提供了可解释的因果洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放松管制下的零售电力市场存在显著价格波动，且与远期和期货产品复杂交互，给运营决策带来挑战。现有预测方法缺乏准确性和可解释性的兼顾，难以支撑市场分析、监管监督和风险管理等实际需求。

Method: 首先通过多分辨率分解将价格序列分离为半年度、季度和月度趋势及高频波动；随后基于域约束（保持因果方向性和外生性）在这些成分与批发远期价格、零售合同属性等协变量上发现因果图；将学习到的因果结构编码为邻接嵌入，条件化TCN的卷积和注意力机制，使表示学习与因果路径对齐。

Result: 在俄亥俄州十年日度12个月固定价格住宅合同数据上，发现批发远期价格主要决定长期零售价格趋势，而合同属性影响短期波动。CG-TCN在1步、10步和15步日度零售中位电价预测中分别取得3.08%、3.82%和5.43%的MAPE，一致优于基准模型。

Conclusion: CG-TCN通过将预测性能与可解释性结合，为竞争性电力市场中的市场分析、消费者保护、监管监督、风险评估和采购规划提供了透明且具有政策相关性的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Retail electricity markets in deregulated systems face significant price volatility and complex interactions with forward and futures products, posing challenges for effective operational decision-making. This study introduces a Causal Graph-Informed Temporal Convolutional Network (CG-TCN), a forecasting architecture that integrates a learned causal graph into a temporal convolutional network via a graph-neural embedding to enhance both forecasting accuracy and interpretability of retail electricity price dynamics. It first applies a multi-resolution decomposition to isolate semiannual, quarterly, and monthly trends from high-frequency fluctuations. A causal graph is then discovered over these components and key covariates, including wholesale forward prices and retail contract attributes such as early termination fees, with domain constraints that preserve causal directionality and exogeneity. The learned causal structure is encoded as an adjacency embedding that conditions the TCN's convolutions and attention, aligning representation learning with causal pathways. Using ten years of daily 12-month fixed-price residential contracts from Ohio's deregulated market, we find that wholesale forward prices primarily determine long-term retail price trends, whereas contract attributes influence short-term fluctuations. CG-TCN consistently outperforms benchmark models, achieving mean absolute percentage errors of 3.08%, 3.82%, and 5.43% for one-, ten-, and fifteen-step-ahead forecasts of daily retail electricity median prices, respectively. By combining predictive performance with interpretability, CG-TCN provides transparent, policy-relevant insight to support market analytics, consumer protection, regulatory oversight, risk assessment and procurement planning in competitive electricity markets.

</details>


### [152] [FLEX-CP-DT: A Flexible Conditional Power Framework for Interim Futility Analysis in Clinical Trials with Count Endpoints and Temporal Trends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26338)
*Yanzhao Wang,Dateng Li,Ningya Wang,Haitao Gao,Chenguang Wang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出FLEX-CP-DT，一个分段负二项（piecewise negative binomial）框架，在复发事件终点III期试验的中期分析中，不依赖恒定事件率和恒定治疗效应的假设即可计算条件功效（CP），从而改善延迟起效或效应随时间衰减场景下的无效性（futility）决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准负二项模型隐含事件率和治疗效应在研究期间恒定的假设。在中期分析时，大量患者随访不完整，若治疗效应延迟起效或随时间衰减，恒定率假设会使中期估计产生系统性偏差，导致错误的无效性终止决策。

Method: 提出分段负二项（piecewise negative binomial）框架FLEX-CP-DT，允许事件率和治疗效应随时间变化，无需恒定率假设；其条件功效计算基于解析公式，无需在中期阶段进行重采样或试验模拟。

Result: 通过覆盖恒定效应与延迟起效场景的广泛模拟，FLEX-CP-DT在恒定率假设成立时与标准方法表现相当；在假设被违反时能改善中期无效性决策。基于已发表的III期支气管扩张试验校准的案例研究表明，该方法能降低错误终止具有延迟起效疗效药物的概率。

Conclusion: FLEX-CP-DT为复发事件终点试验的中期无效性分析提供了更稳健的替代工具，尤其适用于治疗效应延迟出现或随时间衰减的真实场景，可减少因恒定率假设偏差导致的错误终止风险。

Abstract: Many large-scale phase III trials with recurrent event endpoints include a pre-planned interim analysis to evaluate early futility. Conditional power (CP), which quantifies the probability of achieving statistical significance at the final analysis given the interim data, is a commonly used tool to support such decisions. The standard negative binomial model with an offset term, widely adopted for analyzing recurrent events, implicitly assumes that event rates and treatment effects remain constant over the study period. At the interim analysis, however, a substantial proportion of patients have incomplete follow-up, and when the treatment effect is delayed in onset or diminishes over time, the constant-rate assumption introduces systematic bias into the interim estimate and can lead to incorrect futility decisions. In this paper, we propose FLEX-CP-DT, a piecewise negative binomial framework that captures temporal trends in both event rates and treatment effects without imposing the constant-rate assumption. The framework yields a formula-based conditional power calculation that does not require resampling or trial simulation at the interim stage. Through extensive simulations spanning constant-effect and delayed-onset scenarios, we demonstrate that FLEX-CP-DT performs comparably to the standard approach when the constant-rate assumption holds and improves interim futility decision-making when it is violated. A case study calibrated to a published phase 3 bronchiectasis trial further illustrates the practical advantage of the proposed method in reducing the probability of falsely terminating an efficacious drug with delayed treatment onset.

</details>


### [153] [Integrating Network Psychometrics and LLMs: The Ising-Embeddings-Model applied to Reliability Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26790)
*Matthias von Davier*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个整合网络心理测量学与语言集成可靠性审计（LiRA）的框架，通过改进的Ising模型对大规模评估中构建性响应的评分一致性进行建模，无需大规模双重评分即可完成可靠性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大规模评估中构建性响应（constructed-response）项目的评分一致性通常依赖双重评分（double-scoring），该方法使用小样本且假设响应之间相互独立，无法充分利用全量数据，也难以捕捉语义层面的依赖关系。

Method: 将网络心理测量学与LiRA整合到一个改进的Ising模型中：定义二元正确性标签的联合分布，其中成对交互项设为句子嵌入的余弦相似度，全局偏置参数用于建模题目难度。LiRA的语义邻域加权多数投票被证明可近似该Ising模型的条件逻辑分布。参数通过最大伪似然估计（maximum pseudo-likelihood）求解。

Result: 该框架支持基准分数生成、不确定性量化和缺失标签插补，可基于全量数据工作而不依赖大规模双重评分，能够解释语义依赖并提供评分者不一致性的诊断。

Conclusion: 将LiRA的可扩展方法与概率图模型相结合，为PIRLS、PISA和TIMSS等国际大规模评估提供了全面的评分可靠性评估工具。

Abstract: Scoring consistency for constructed-response items in large-scale assessments is typically estimated through double-scoring, which uses small samples and assumes independence among responses. We present an integrated framework combining network psychometrics with the Linguistic-Integrated Reliability Audit (LiRA) via a modified Ising model. The model defines a joint distribution over binary correctness labels with pairwise interactions set to the cosine similarity of sentence embeddings and a global bias parameter for item difficulty. LiRA's weighted majority voting over semantic neighborhoods is shown to approximate the conditional logistic distributions of this Ising model. The parametric framework supports benchmark score generation, uncertainty quantification, and missing label imputation; parameters are estimated by maximum pseudo-likelihood. The approach uses the full dataset without requiring extensive double-scoring, accounts for semantic dependencies, and provides diagnostics for rater inconsistencies. The integration of LiRA's scalable methodology with a probabilistic graphical model offers a comprehensive tool for reliability assessment in international assessments such as PIRLS, PISA, and TIMSS.

</details>


### [154] [Climate Physics Dynamic Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26907)
*Gurjeet Sangra Singh,Frantzeska Lavda,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: ClimPhyDM是一种将物理先验（平流方程）与数据驱动组件结合的变分模拟免训练框架，用于天气预报，在ERA5基准上（小时和月尺度）优于ClimODE和GB-DM，并能在12GB消费级GPU上训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型（如流匹配和扩散模型）在学习复杂动力系统时通常作为黑箱，忽略底层物理结构；而基于偏微分方程的物理模型往往因缺失源项或参数不确定而不完整。因此需要一种既能利用物理结构又能捕捉数据特征的混合方法。

Method: 提出Climate Physics Dynamic Matching (ClimPhyDM)，一种变分模拟免训练的动力学感知框架，将平流型物理先验与数据驱动组件结合在变分框架中，以捕捉未解析大气动力学的随机性和多模态性。

Result: 在ERA5基准上，在小时（42小时）和月（5个月）两种分辨率下，ClimPhyDM均优于ClimODE和GB-DM，且在扩展时间范围保持更低误差，表明其时间稳定性和抗误差累积能力更强；且模拟免训练范式使其可在单个12GB消费级GPU上训练。

Conclusion: ClimPhyDM通过物理先验与数据驱动的变分结合，既提升了天气预报的长期稳定性和准确性，又降低了计算资源需求，展示了物理信息与生成模型融合在动力学系统建模中的潜力。

Abstract: Deep generative models such as flow matching and diffusion models have shown potential for learning complex dynamical systems, but typically act as black boxes that neglect underlying physical structure, while physics-based models governed by partial differential equations are often incomplete due to missing source terms, or uncertain parametrisations. We present Climate Physics Dynamic Matching (ClimPhyDM), a variational simulation-free dynamics informed framework for weather forecasting that combines an advection-type physics prior with data-driven components in a variational framework. % to capture the stochasticity and multi-modality of unresolved atmospheric dynamics. On the ERA5 benchmark at hourly (42-hour) and monthly (5-month) resolutions, ClimPhyDM outperforms ClimODE, and GB-DM, keeping the lower error at extended horizon, indicating improved temporal stability and resistance to error accumulation, while its simulation-free paradigm also enables training on a single modest 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [155] [Modeling Spatially Obfuscated Street-Crime Data using Log-Gaussian Cox Processes on Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27117)
*Lulu Jiang,David Bolin*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于道路网络上犯罪数据建模的log-Gaussian Cox过程框架，解决了英国警方公布的街道级犯罪数据中空间位置被匿名化（非精确位置）导致的支撑不匹配问题。该方法通过度量图上的Whittle–Matérn高斯场SPDE表示对潜在log强度建模，并与平面点模型和平面聚合模型进行比较。模拟研究和伦敦金融城实际应用表明，度量图模型在参数恢复和拟合优度上均优于平面替代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国警方公布的街道级犯罪数据中，公布的坐标是匿名化的代理位置而非精确事件位置，这导致模型支撑与实际数据生成过程不匹配。现有方法要么将发布位置当作精确点处理（平面点模型），要么忽略道路网络结构（平面聚合模型），均无法正确反映犯罪事件在道路网络上的空间特性，因此需要一种既能处理空间模糊性又能尊重路网几何结构的建模框架。

Method: 采用log-Gaussian Cox过程框架，将每个观测值表示为街道网络上的聚合支撑（aggregated support）以解决因位置匿名化导致的支撑不匹配问题。潜在log强度通过SPDE表示建模为度量图上的Whittle–Matérn高斯场，使犯罪强度沿街道连续变化并尊重路网几何。与平面点模型（将发布位置视为精确点）和平面聚合模型（考虑空间聚合但忽略网络支撑）进行对比。

Result: 模拟研究中（数据在街道网络上生成），度量图模型在参数恢复准确性和整体拟合方面优于平面替代模型。在伦敦金融城的实际应用中，度量图模型在多项犯罪类型（扒窃、抢劫、毒品、自行车盗窃）中均取得最佳拟合。结果还表明环境设施与犯罪风险之间的关系因犯罪类型而异，超市与犯罪风险的正向关联最为一致。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析具有隐私保护和位置不精确特性的网络约束空间事件数据提供了一种原理性方法，能够更准确地刻画犯罪强度在道路网络上的空间分布，并为犯罪类型与城市环境因素之间的关系提供了更可靠的推断。

Abstract: We develop a log-Gaussian Cox process framework for modelling street-level crime data observed on a road network when the released event locations are spatially obfuscated. Motivated by UK Police street-level crime data, where published coordinates are anonymised proxy locations rather than exact event locations, we address the resulting support mismatch by representing each observation through an aggregated support on the street network. The latent log-intensity is modelled as a Whittle--Matérn Gaussian field defined on a metric graph through an SPDE representation, allowing the crime intensity to vary continuously along streets while respecting the geometry of the road network. We compare the proposed metric-graph aggregated model with two alternatives: a planar point model that treats the released locations as exact points, and a planar aggregated model that accounts for spatial aggregation but ignores the network support. In a simulation study where data are generated on a street network, the metric-graph model provides more accurate parameter recovery and better overall fit than the planar alternative. In the City of London application, the metric-graph model also achieves the best fit across several crime types, including theft from the person, robbery, drugs, and bicycle theft. The results further suggest that the relationship between environmental amenities and crime risk varies by crime type, with supermarkets showing the most consistent positive associations. The proposed framework provides a principled approach for analysing network-constrained spatial event data with privacy-protected and imprecise locations.

</details>


### [156] [The Wasserstein Distance for Mortality Comparisons: Absolute versus Net Differences in Survival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27120)
*Markus Sauerberg*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了出生时预期寿命差距与Wasserstein距离之间的关系。当生存函数不相交时，两者完全对应；当生存函数交叉时，净生存差异（预期寿命差距）可能很小，但人口死亡率模式差异很大。基于1990-2020年人类死亡率数据库的69,188对人群比较显示，59.8%的生存函数发生交叉，但交叉幅度通常很小，因此大多数情况下预期寿命差距足以量化两种死亡率制度之间的总体差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预期寿命差距是人口学中最常用的健康不平等指标之一，但该指标可能存在缺陷：当两个人群的生存函数发生交叉时，净生存差异可能很小，即使两个人口具有截然不同的死亡率模式。本文旨在揭示这种交叉现象的发生频率和幅度，评估预期寿命差距作为总体死亡率差异指标的充分性。

Method: 本文将Wasserstein距离（即生存函数的绝对差值）与出生时预期寿命差距（生存函数的净差值）进行对比。分析了两者对应的数学条件（生存函数不相交时等价），探讨了生存函数交叉的机制——由年龄特异性死亡率的累积差异驱动。使用人类死亡率数据库1990-2020年生命表数据，对69,188个人口对进行了实证分析，检验生存函数交叉的普遍性与幅度。

Result: 在69,188个人口对的比较中，59.8%的生存函数发生了交叉，表明这种情形在实证中相当普遍。然而，生存逆转的幅度通常很小，意味着尽管存在交叉，预期寿命差距在大多数情况下仍然能较好地反映两种死亡率制度之间的整体差异。

Conclusion: 预期寿命差距在大多数情况下不仅是均值比较，而是能够量化两个人口死亡率制度的整体差异。尽管生存函数交叉现象在超过一半的比较中出现，但其影响幅度较小，因此预期寿命差距作为一个简洁的总体健康不平等指标仍然具有很强的实用性。

Abstract: It is well known that the gap in life expectancy at birth can be seen as the net difference between two survivorship functions. When calculating the absolute difference between the two survivorship functions instead, we derive a distributional inequality measure which is called the Wasserstein distance. The measure quantifies how far apart two probability distributions are by the minimal cost of transporting one into the other. This paper relates the Wasserstein distance to the difference in life expectancy at birth. Both measures correspond to each other, whenever the survivorship functions do not cross, i.e., the net difference equals the absolute difference in the no crossing case. Whether the curves cross is driven by the accumulated difference in age-specific death rates. Crossing occurs in situations where an accumulated survival advantage for given populations is reversed because elevated death rates at later ages outweigh the earlier survival advantage. These cases are particularly interesting because the net difference in survival (or the life expectancy gap) may be small even though the two populations show very different mortality schedules. In our empirical analysis, we search for these cases using life table data from Human Mortality Database life tables for the period 1990 to 2020. Across 69 188 population pairs, survivorship functions cross in 59.8 % of comparisons. Yet, the magnitude of the reversal in survivorship is usually very small. We therefore conclude that in most cases, the gap in life expectancy at birth is not only a comparison of means but quantifies the overall difference between the two mortality regimes

</details>


### [157] [A spatio-temporal block aggregation model for latent log Gaussian outcomes: application on modelling wastewater virus concentration in Wales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27207)
*Stephen Jun Villejo,Peter Diggle,Guangquan Li,Ella White,Matthew Wade,Christopher Williams,Davey L. Jones,Alisha Davies,Marta Blangiardo*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯框架的时空模型，用于将底层空间连续过程与汇总（块聚合）的废水监测数据相连接，并通过线性化集成嵌套拉普拉斯近似（INLA）方法进行推断，应用于威尔士47个集水区2022年8月至2023年7月的SARS-CoV-2监测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 废水流行病学监测虽然能提供社区层面的疾病动态信号，但废水测量值往往以不规则空间单元上的聚合值形式被观测到，现有方法未能明确建模这种空间"块聚合"（block aggregation）过程与底层连续空间过程之间的关系，导致推断和预测不够准确。

Method: 提出一个用于潜在对数高斯结果的时空模型，将底层空间连续过程整合到任意空间配置中；采用贝叶斯框架，通过线性化集成嵌套拉普拉斯近似（INLA）方法进行推断；该框架支持任意空间单元的聚合推断，并可扩展到后续分析（如关联健康结果）。

Result: 模型应用于威尔士47个集水区的SARS-CoV-2 N1基因拷贝数据，成功捕捉了空间和时间模式，具有良好预测性能；估计的病毒基因拷贝数与地方当局级别的COVID-19 PCR检测阳性率强相关。

Conclusion: 明确对块聚合进行建模在分析废水监测数据时至关重要；所提出的框架为将环境监测数据整合到公共卫生监测系统提供了灵活且原则性的方法。

Abstract: Wastewater-based epidemiology has emerged as a valuable tool for monitoring community-level infectious disease dynamics, providing population-wide signals that complement clinical surveillance. However, wastewater measurements are often observed as aggregated values over irregular spatial units. This work develops an approach to link an underlying spatially continuous processes and an aggregated outcome. We propose a spatio-temporal model for latent log-Gaussian outcomes that provides a coherent framework for inference and prediction, allowing the process to be integrated over arbitrary spatial configurations. This framework can also be used for subsequent analyses, such as linking wastewater signal to health outcomes at administrative areas. We use a Bayesian framework for inference via the linearised integrated nested Laplace approximation (INLA) approach. We apply the proposed methodology to model SARS-CoV-2 N1 gene copies in wastewater across 47 catchment areas in Wales from the beginning of August 2022 to the end of July 2023. The results demonstrate that the model captures spatial and temporal patterns and has good predictive performance. Results also show that estimated viral gene copies are strongly linked to positivity rates from COVID-19 PCR tests at the local authority level. Our findings highlight the importance of explicitly modelling block aggregation when analysing wastewater surveillance data. The proposed framework provides a flexible and principled approach for integrating environmental surveillance data into public health monitoring systems.

</details>


### [158] [How exceptional was the Big Three era? Extremes and persistence in men's professional tennis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27362)
*Manuele Leonelli*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯动态Bradley-Terry状态空间模型，基于1968-2025年间197,926场比赛数据，量化衡量网球选手的统治力。研究发现只有德约科维奇在巅峰实力上真正脱离同侪，费德勒和纳达尔与博格、麦肯罗、伦德尔无显著差异；所谓"三巨头时代"的独特之处不在于个人峰值高度，而在于三人同期共同占据顶端的时间长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2003-2023年间三位球员赢得了81个大满贯中的66个，这一时代被广泛认为是网球史上最具统治力的时期。但评估这一论断面临核心挑战：这些顶尖选手从未交手，所有比较都必须通过他们各自的对手进行间接传递。此外，现有衡量方法依赖于不稳定的连续排名窗口，需要更稳健的统计框架来科学评估跨时代的统治力比较。

Method: 采用贝叶斯动态Bradley-Terry状态空间模型估计1968-2025年间所有球员的动态实力，通过超越高阈值来刻画统治力特征。用"选手相对同时代选手的领先幅度"衡量个体高度，"同时有多人站在顶端"衡量密度，"在顶端持续的时间"衡量持久性（采用固定窗口平均值而非不稳定的连续连胜记录）。

Result: 在巅峰实力方面，只有德约科维奇显著超出同时代整体水平，费德勒和纳达尔与博格、麦肯罗和伦德尔不可区分。近期时代的独特之处不是顶尖选手站得有多高，而是三位顶尖选手同时屹立的时间有多长——巅峰期三人占据前三位置的时间占比不低于五分之四，而历史上最强可比十年中这一位置由四人分享。更显著的反常发生在1990年起长达十二年的"上尾压缩"期。

Conclusion: "三巨头时代"的统治力本质在持久性而非峰值高度。真正的统计离群值是德约科维奇个人，而非整个时代；该时代的标志性特征是三位强手在漫长窗口内的同时在场，而非任何单一个体的绝对优势。

Abstract: Three players won 66 of the 81 Grand Slam titles contested between 2003 and 2023, and their era is widely held to be the most dominant in the history of tennis. Assessing it means comparing players who never met, so that every comparison passes through the opponents each did face. We measure dominance by how far a player stands above the field of his own day, how many stand there at once, and how long they remain, the last through averages over fixed windows rather than through unbroken runs, which are unstable when the strengths beneath them are themselves estimated. Strengths come from a Bayesian dynamic Bradley-Terry state space model, and dominance is characterized through exceedances of a high threshold. Applied to 197,926 matches played between 1968 and 2025, only Djokovic separates from the field on peak strength, Federer and Nadal being indistinguishable from Borg, McEnroe and Lendl. What distinguishes the recent period is not how high its best players stood but how long three of them stood there together. At its height three players held a top-three position in at least four fifths of all weeks, where in the strongest comparable decade of the past those positions were shared among four. The sharper anomaly lies between: for twelve years from 1990 the upper tail was not merely unoccupied but compressed.

</details>
